### [1] [Dive into the Scene: Breaking the Perceptual Bottleneck in Vision-Language Decision Making via Focus Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04046)
*Boyuan Xiao,Bohong Chen,Yumeng Li,Ji Feng,Yao-Xiang Ding,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneDiver通过粗到细的聚焦计划生成方法，先构建场景图建立初步理解，再逐步分解任务为子问题，从而减少VLM和VLA在具身视觉-语言决策中的视觉幻觉，同时保持计算效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM和VLA在具身任务中因无法区分任务相关物体与干扰物而产生视觉幻觉，限制其性能。简单的一步聚焦方法因缺乏深度场景理解而无效，因此需要更有效的聚焦机制。

Method: 提出SceneDiver方法：采用粗到细的聚焦计划生成策略，首先构建整体场景图建立初始理解，然后通过识别、理解和分析的迭代循环，逐步将任务分解为更简单的子问题；同时设计轻量级适配器，将VLM的刻意聚焦能力蒸馏到VLA中，使其具备反应式控制能力。

Result: 在标准具身AI基准上的评估表明，该方法显著减少了VLM和VLA的视觉幻觉，同时在需要快速执行的任务中保持了计算效率。

Conclusion: SceneDiver通过粗到细的聚焦计划生成有效缓解了具身视觉-语言决策中的视觉幻觉问题，兼顾了VLM的长程规划优势和VLA的反应控制优势。

Original Abstract: In embodied vision-language decision making tasks such as robotic manipulation and navigation, Vision-Language and Vision-Language-Action Models (VLMs & VLAs) are powerful tools with different benefits: VLMs are better at long-term planning, while VLAs are better at reactive control. However, their performance is limited by the same perceptual bottleneck: visual hallucinations arise due to the models' inability to distinguish task-relevant objects from distractors. In principle, accurate identification and focus on critical objects while filtering out irrelevant ones is the key to break this limitation. A straightforward solution is one-step focus: directly attending to essential objects. However, this approach proves ineffective because effective focus inherently requires deep scene understanding. To this end, we propose SceneDiver, a coarse-to-fine focus plan generation method for VLMs leveraging their long-term planning abilities, that first constructs a holistic scene graph to establish initial comprehension, then progressively decomposes the task into simpler sub-problems through an iterative cycle of recognition, understanding, and analysis. To enable reactive control, we also design a lightweight adapter for distilling the deliberate focus ability into VLAs. Evaluations on standard embodied AI benchmarks confirm that our method substantially reduces visual hallucinations for both VLMs and VLAs, while preserving computational efficiency in tasks requiring fast execution. Our code and data are released at: https://future-item.github.io/SceneDiver.

中文摘要翻译: 在具身视觉-语言决策任务（如机器人操作和导航）中，视觉-语言模型（VLM）和视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）是强大的工具，各有优势：VLM更擅长长程规划，而VLA更擅长反应式控制。然而，它们的性能受到相同的感知瓶颈限制：由于模型无法区分任务相关物体与干扰物，导致视觉幻觉的产生。原则上，准确识别并聚焦关键物体同时过滤掉无关物体是突破这一局限的关键。一个直接的解决方案是一步聚焦：直接关注必要物体。然而，这种方法被证明是无效的，因为有效的聚焦本质上需要深度场景理解。为此，我们提出SceneDiver，一种面向VLM的粗到细聚焦计划生成方法，利用其长程规划能力，首先构建整体场景图建立初始理解，然后通过识别、理解和分析的迭代循环，逐步将任务分解为更简单的子问题。为了实现反应式控制，我们还设计了一种轻量级适配器，将VLM的刻意聚焦能力蒸馏到VLA中。在标准具身AI基准上的评估证实，我们的方法显著减少了VLM和VLA的视觉幻觉，同时在需要快速执行的任务中保持了计算效率。我们的代码和数据已发布于：https://future-item.github.io/SceneDiver。

</details>


### [2] [Weakly Supervised Incremental Segmentation via Semantic Anchors and Spatial Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04060)
*Zhonggai Wang,Kai Fang,Guangyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对弱增量语义分割（WILSS）中噪声监督导致特征漂移和语义破坏的问题，本文提出SASA方法，通过语义锚点（可学习令牌作为刚性类级参考）和弹性残差自适应实现稳定表征学习，并设计空间标签仲裁机制进行几何感知的标签过滤，有效缓解特征漂移，在标准基准上持续优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弱增量语义分割（WILSS）中不断引入的噪声监督会逐步破坏类级表征，导致严重的特征漂移和语义破坏，使得新学类别覆盖旧类别。现有方法未能有效解决这一核心问题。

Method: 提出SASA方法，包含两个核心机制：(1) 表征层面：引入语义锚点（可学习令牌作为刚性类级参考）保持长期语义身份，配合弹性残差自适应实现受控的实例级精细调整；(2) 监督层面：设计空间标签仲裁机制，进行几何感知决策，直接过滤不可靠信号并强制执行"一个对象，一个类别"约束。

Result: 在标准基准上，SASA一致优于现有最先进方法，尤其在具有挑战性的多步增量设置中表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过协同稳定表征和提高监督可靠性，SASA有效缓解了弱监督下的特征漂移，为弱增量语义分割提供了有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Weakly Incremental Learning for Semantic Segmentation (WILSS) suffers from the continuous introduction of noisy supervision, which progressively corrupts class-level representations, leading to severe feature drift and semantic corruption, thereby causing newly learned classes to overwrite old ones. To address these issues, we propose a drift-resilient WILSS approach, named SASA, designed to stabilize semantic learning via Semantic Anchors and Spatial Arbitration. Specifically, at the representation level, we introduce semantic anchors of learnable tokens as rigid class-level references to preserve long-term semantic identity. Complementary to this, an elastic residual adaptation facilitates controlled, instance-specific refinement, ensuring a stable yet flexible learning trajectory. At the supervision level, we develop a Spatial Label Arbitration mechanism that performs geometry-aware decisions to directly filter unreliable signals and enforce a strict "one object, one class" constraint. By synergistically stabilizing representations and improving supervision reliability, SASA effectively mitigates feature drift under weak supervision. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, particularly in challenging multi-step incremental settings. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhonggaiWang/SASA.

中文摘要翻译: 弱增量语义分割（WILSS）因持续引入噪声监督而受到影响，噪声监督逐步破坏类级表征，导致严重的特征漂移和语义损坏，从而使得新学习的类别覆盖旧类别。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种抗漂移的WILSS方法，命名为SASA，通过语义锚点和空间仲裁来稳定语义学习。具体地，在表征层面，我们引入可学习令牌的语义锚点作为刚性类级参考，以保持长期语义身份。与之互补，弹性残差自适应实现受控的、实例级别的精细调整，确保稳定而灵活的学习轨迹。在监督层面，我们开发了一种空间标签仲裁机制，该机制执行几何感知决策，直接过滤不可靠信号并强制执行"一个对象，一个类别"的约束。通过协同稳定表征和提高监督可靠性，SASA有效减轻了弱监督下的特征漂移。在标准基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法一致优于现有最先进方法，尤其是在具有挑战性的多步增量设置中。代码可在 https://github.com/ZhonggaiWang/SASA 获取。

</details>


### [3] [Intra-Modal Neighbors Never Lie: Rectifying Inter-Modal Noisy Correspondence via Graph-Based Intra-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04061)
*Yang Liu,Wentao Feng,Shu-Dong Huang,Yalan Ye,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IN2R框架，通过模态内邻域感知的噪声矫正，将跨模态检索中的噪声对应处理范式从离散替代标签搜索转向连续软原型合成，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有噪声对应处理方法受限于“离散选择”范式，依赖单一离散代理会导致单点脆弱性和离散化误差，亟需一种更鲁棒的监督信号合成范式。

Method: 利用模态内数据固有的几何稳定性，通过图精炼器对动态跨模态记忆库检索到的邻居进行关系推理，合成反映局部语义邻域共识的连续软原型，从而矫正跨模态错位。

Result: 在Flickr30K、MS-COCO和CC152K三个数据集上显著超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 从“离散选择”转向“连续合成”的噪声矫正范式能够有效克服单点脆弱性和离散化误差，为跨模态检索中的噪声对应问题提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Large-scale web-harvested datasets have fueled the progress of cross-modal retrieval but inevitably suffer from noisy correspondence, which severely degrades model generalization. Existing methods primarily address this by filtering out noise or seeking a substitute label, yet they predominantly remain bound by a "Discrete Selection" paradigm. We argue that relying on a single discrete proxy induces Single-Point Fragility and Discretization Error. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel framework, Intra-modal Neighbor-aware Noise Rectification (IN2R), which shifts the paradigm from searching for a substitute to synthesizing a reliable supervision target. Leveraging the intrinsic geometric stability of intra-modal data, IN2R employs a Graph Refiner to perform relational reasoning over neighbors retrieved from a dynamic Cross-Model Memory. Instead of propagating discrete labels, our method synthesizes a continuous, soft prototype that reflects the consensus of the local semantic neighborhood, effectively rectifying inter-modal misalignment. Extensive experiments on Flickr30K, MS-COCO, and CC152K demonstrate that IN2R significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/liuyyy111/IN2R.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模网络抓取数据集推动了跨模态检索的进步，但不可避免地遭受噪声对应问题，这严重降低了模型的泛化能力。现有方法主要通过过滤噪声或寻找替代标签来应对这一问题，但它们仍然主要受限于“离散选择”范式。我们认为，依赖单一的离散代理会导致单点脆弱性和离散化误差。为克服这些局限，我们提出了一种新颖框架——模态内邻域感知噪声矫正（IN2R），该框架将范式从寻找替代标签转变为合成可靠的监督目标。利用模态内数据固有的几何稳定性，IN2R采用图精炼器对从动态跨模态记忆库中检索到的邻居进行关系推理。我们的方法不是传播离散标签，而是合成一个反映局部语义邻域共识的连续软原型，从而有效矫正跨模态错位。在Flickr30K、MS-COCO和CC152K上的大量实验表明，IN2R显著优于现有最先进方法。我们的代码和预训练模型已在https://github.com/liuyyy111/IN2R公开。

</details>


### [4] [Optimal Transport Flow Matching by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04092)
*Shimon Malnick,Matan Rusanovsky,Ohad Fried,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将先验分布视为可设计的选择而非固定输入，通过选择与数据具有OT最优恒等映射的低频投影作为先验，简化流匹配模型中的轨迹曲率问题，无需显式求解OT耦合即可实现直线、非交叉的生成轨迹，并提升少步生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流匹配模型通过最优传输（OT）耦合实现直线生成轨迹，但高维空间中求解OT耦合计算困难且存在偏差或开销过大的问题。本文旨在避免求解OT耦合，同时获得低曲率、非交叉的生成轨迹。

Method: 将先验从固定输入重新定义为可设计的选择。识别自然图像的低频投影作为先验，该先验与数据之间存在经验上OT最优的恒等映射。使用轻量模型在推理时采样该先验，剩余流匹配任务简化为合成高频细节。进一步引入高斯噪声插值以在保持OT耦合的同时提升生成质量。

Result: 在所有基准测试中，该方法相比现有流匹配方法将轨迹曲率降低了2倍以上，在少步生成场景下取得了更好的生成质量。该方法无需修改流模型本身，可自然集成于潜在空间模型、无分类器引导和一步生成框架。

Conclusion: 通过重新定义先验为可设计的选择，并利用低频投影作为OT最优恒等映射的先验，可以在不求解OT耦合的情况下实现低曲率、高质量的生成，为流匹配模型提供了一种简单有效的改进途径。

Original Abstract: Flow matching models learn to transport samples from a simple prior distribution to a complex data distribution. When prior-data pairs are coupled via optimal transport (OT), the learned trajectories are straight and non-crossing, enabling fast, even single-step, generation. However, computing the OT coupling in high dimensions is intractable, and existing methods attempt to solve the OT problem, at the cost of persistent bias or significant overhead. Rather than solving for the OT coupling, we reformulate the problem. Once the prior is treated as a design choice rather than a fixed input, the OT coupling between prior and data is no longer unique. Many priors admit an OT-optimal identity coupling to the data, leaving us free to choose one that is also tractable to sample. We identify low-frequency projection of natural images as such a choice. The identity coupling between data and its low-frequency representation is empirically OT-optimal, the prior is structured enough to be sampled by a lightweight model at inference, and the remaining flow-matching task reduces to synthesizing high-frequency detail. Interpolating the prior with Gaussian noise further improves generation quality while preserving the OT coupling. The approach requires no modifications to the flow model itself, and integrates naturally with latent-space models, classifier-free guidance, and one-step generation frameworks. Across all benchmarks, our method reduces trajectory curvature by more than $2\times$ compared to existing flow matching methods, yielding better generation quality in the few-step regime.

中文摘要翻译: 流匹配模型学习将样本从简单的先验分布传输到复杂的数据分布。当先验-数据对通过最优传输（OT）耦合时，学习到的轨迹是直线且非交叉的，从而能够实现快速生成，甚至单步生成。然而，在高维空间中计算OT耦合是棘手的，现有方法尝试求解OT问题，代价是存在持续的偏差或显著的开销。本文并非求解OT耦合，而是重新表述了该问题。一旦将先验视为设计选择而非固定输入，先验与数据之间的OT耦合就不再唯一。许多先验都允许与数据之间存在OT最优的恒等映射，这使我们能够自由选择一个在采样上也是易于处理（tractable）的先验。我们识别出自然图像的低频投影正是这样一种选择。数据与其低频表示之间的恒等映射在经验上是OT最优的，该先验结构足够有序，可以在推理时通过轻量模型进行采样，而剩下的流匹配任务则简化为合成高频细节。用高斯噪声插值该先验进一步提升了生成质量，同时保持了OT耦合。该方法无需对流模型本身进行任何修改，并自然地与潜在空间模型、无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）和一步生成框架集成。在所有基准测试中，我们的方法相比现有流匹配方法将轨迹曲率降低了2倍以上，在少步生成场景下取得了更好的生成质量。

</details>


### [5] [When Seeing Is Not Believing -- A Benchmark for Search-Grounded Video Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04098)
*Tao Yu,Yujia Yang,Shenghua Chai,Zhang Jinshuai,Haopeng Jin,Hao Wang,Minghui Zhang,Zhongtian Luo,Yuchen Long,Xinlong Chen,Jiabing Yang,Zhaolu Kang,Yuxuan Zhou,Zhengyu Man,Xinming Wang,Hongzhu Yi,Zheqi He,Xi Yang,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EVID-Bench基准，用于基于搜索的视频虚假信息检测，通过跨视频对比和网络搜索识别复杂操作（如AI生成、单源编辑、多源编辑），现有最优模型仅达61.43%点级准确率，AI生成内容尤为困难。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频虚假信息检测方法仅依赖输入视频本身，无法检测涉及证据缺失、重排、替换或重新语境化等语义和证据层面的操作，因此需要引入基于搜索的跨视频验证方法。

Method: 构建EVID-Bench基准，包含222个视频、9种操作类型（AI生成、单源编辑、多源编辑三类），所有样本经验证无法通过前沿模型的纯视觉检测发现；采用检索增强验证基线（检索开放网络相关视频后进行跨视频对比），评估9个前沿多模态模型。

Result: 最佳系统点级准确率仅61.43%，视频级准确率43.24%；AI生成类操作尤其困难；错误分析显示模型存在关注无关锚点、将合成内容误判为拼接编辑、过早终止搜索等问题。

Conclusion: 现有前沿多模态模型在搜索增强的视频虚假信息检测任务上表现有限，尤其是在AI生成内容检测方面，亟需更好的跨视频推理和搜索策略。

Original Abstract: Video misinformation increasingly operates at the semantic and evidential level: authentic footage may be selectively edited, temporally reordered, spliced across sources, or augmented with AI-generated content to construct false narratives. Such evidence-dependent manipulations cannot be reliably verified from the input video alone, because the missing, reordered, replaced, or recontextualized evidence lies outside the video itself. We introduce \textbf{EVID-Bench}, a benchmark for search-grounded video misinformation detection, where a system must search the open web for related videos and identify what information is false through cross-video comparison. EVID-Bench comprises 222 videos spanning 9 manipulation types across 3 categories: AI generation, single-source editing, and multi-source editing. All samples are verified to be undetectable by frontier models through visual inspection alone. We evaluate nine frontier multimodal models using a retrieval-augmented verification baseline. The best system achieves only 61.43\% point-level accuracy and 43.24\% video-level accuracy, while AI-generated manipulations remain especially challenging. Error analysis reveals recurring challenges: models fixate on irrelevant anchors, misattribute synthetic content to editorial splicing, and terminate search prematurely before fully explaining the manipulation.

中文摘要翻译: 视频虚假信息日益在语义和证据层面运作：真实片段可能被选择性编辑、时间顺序重排、跨源拼接，或通过AI生成内容增强以构建虚假叙事。这类依赖证据的操作无法仅从输入视频中可靠验证，因为缺失、重排、替换或重新语境化的证据位于视频本身之外。本文提出EVID-Bench，一个基于搜索的视频虚假信息检测基准，系统需在开放网络上搜索相关视频，并通过跨视频对比识别虚假信息。EVID-Bench包含222个视频，覆盖9种操作类型，分为三大类别：AI生成、单源编辑和多源编辑。所有样本均经验证，前沿模型仅通过视觉检测无法识别。我们采用检索增强验证基线评估了九个前沿多模态模型。最佳系统点级准确率仅为61.43%，视频级准确率为43.24%，其中AI生成类操作尤为困难。错误分析揭示了反复出现的挑战：模型关注无关锚点，将合成内容误判为编辑拼接，以及在完全解释操作之前过早终止搜索。

</details>


### [6] [Reflection Separation from a Single Image via Joint Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04107)
*Zheng-Hui Huang,Zhixiang Wang,Yu-Lun Liu,Yung-Yu Chuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种专门微调的扩散模型，用于在极端条件下（如眩光或弱反射）进行单图像反射分离。该方法通过统一扩散模型同时生成透射层和反射层，引入跨层自注意力机制、分离采样策略和潜在优化步骤，在多个真实基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在眩光或弱反射等极端场景下，由于信息不足难以同时恢复两个层次，导致反射分离效果不佳。

Method: 1) 使用统一扩散模型同时生成透射层和反射层；2) 提出跨层自注意力机制增强特征解耦；3) 设计分离采样策略迭代减少层间干扰；4) 引入潜在优化步骤，结合学习到的合成函数提升复杂真实场景效果。

Result: 在多个真实场景基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过扩散先验和精心设计的采样与优化策略，有效解决了极端条件下的单图像反射分离难题。

Original Abstract: Single-image reflection separation is highly challenging under extreme conditions like glare or weak reflections. Existing methods often struggle to recover both layers in glare or weak-reflection scenarios because of insufficient information. This paper presents a diffusion model explicitly fine-tuned for this task, leveraging generative diffusion priors for robust separation. Our method simultaneously generates transmission and reflection layers through a unified diffusion model, incorporating a novel cross-layer self-attention mechanism for better feature disentanglement. We further introduce a disjoint sampling strategy to iteratively reduce interference between the layers during diffusion and a latent optimization step with a learned composition function for improved results in complex real-world scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach surpasses state-of-the-art methods on multiple real-world benchmarks. Project page: https://brian90709.github.io/diff-reflection-separation/

中文摘要翻译: 单图像反射分离在极端条件（如眩光或弱反射）下极具挑战性。现有方法在眩光或弱反射场景中常因信息不足而难以同时恢复两个层次。本文提出了一种专门为此任务微调的扩散模型，利用生成式扩散先验实现鲁棒分离。我们的方法通过统一的扩散模型同时生成透射层和反射层，并引入新颖的跨层自注意力机制以增强特征解耦。我们进一步提出了一种分离采样策略，在扩散过程中迭代减少层间干扰，以及一个带有学习到的合成函数的潜在优化步骤，以改善复杂真实场景下的分离效果。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多个真实世界基准上超越了现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [7] [Prospective Dynamic 3D MRI Reconstruction via Latent-Space Motion Tracking from Single Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04249)
*Lixuan Chen,Zhongnan Liu,Jesse Hamilton,James M. Balter,Jeong Joon Park,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PDMR框架，通过离线学习低维流形上的运动场表示，实现快速在线适应的前瞻性动态三维MRI重建，在超高稀疏采样和严格延迟要求下取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 前瞻性MRI重建在临床应用中至关重要，但超高稀疏采样和严格的延迟要求使其面临巨大挑战，现有回顾性和在线方法难以兼顾重建精度与实时性。

Method: 核心思想是离线学习一个高效且可泛化的运动场低维流形，通过将形变矢量场（DVFs）参数化到低维流形上缩小搜索空间以加速在线适应，并采用三平面（tri-plane）表示实现几何感知且内存高效的3D运动编码。

Result: 在XCAT数字体模和院内腹部MRI数据集上，PDMR在多种前瞻性场景（即时采集和2分钟后采集）下均实现了高保真且时间一致的重建，性能优于最先进的回顾性和在线方法。

Conclusion: PDMR为临床实践中超快速、运动感知的前瞻性MRI重建提供了一条有前景的途径。

Original Abstract: Prospective reconstruction is crucial in many clinical applications such as MRI-guided radiotherapy, which demands accurate image reconstruction and fast motion estimation from currently acquired measurements. However, prospective reconstruction remains challenging due to ultra-sparse sampling and stringent latency requirements. In this work, we propose PDMR, a Prospective Dynamic 3D MRI Reconstruction framework with latent-space motion tracking. Our core idea is to learn an efficient and generalizable latent manifold of motion fields offline, enabling rapid online adaptation for prospective reconstruction. Specifically, we parameterize the deformation vector fields (DVFs) on a low-dimensional manifold, effectively reducing the search space for fast online adaptation, and employ a tri-plane representation to achieve geometry-aware and memory-efficient encoding of 3D motion. Experiments on both XCAT digital phantoms and in-house abdominal MRI datasets demonstrate that PDMR achieves high-fidelity and temporally consistent reconstruction across multiple prospective scenarios (Immediate and After-2min), outperforming state-of-the-art retrospective and online methods. Our results suggest a promising pathway toward ultra-fast, motion-aware prospective MRI reconstruction in clinical practice.

中文摘要翻译: 前瞻性重建在诸多临床应用中至关重要，例如MRI引导的放射治疗，这要求根据当前采集的测量数据实现精确的图像重建和快速的运动估计。然而，由于超高稀疏采样和严格的延迟要求，前瞻性重建仍然充满挑战。在这项工作中，我们提出了PDMR——一种具有潜在空间运动跟踪的前瞻性动态三维MRI重建框架。我们的核心思想是离线学习一个高效且可泛化的运动场潜在流形，从而实现快速在线适应以进行前瞻性重建。具体而言，我们将形变矢量场参数化到一个低维流形上，有效缩小了快速在线适应的搜索空间，并采用三平面表示来实现几何感知且内存高效的3D运动编码。在XCAT数字体模和院内腹部MRI数据集上的实验表明，PDMR在多种前瞻性场景（即时采集和2分钟后采集）下均实现了高保真且时间一致的重建，性能优于最先进的回顾性和在线方法。我们的结果展示了在临床实践中实现超快速、运动感知的前瞻性MRI重建的一条有前景的路径。

</details>


### [8] [Pinpoint: Grounded Worldwide Image Geolocation via Cross-Source Retrieval and Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04133)
*Nika Chuzhoy,Brian Hu,Amit A. Arora,Jae Ro,Sarthak S. Sahu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pinpoint提出了一种检索-重排序架构，通过联合利用互联网照片和街景图像，在粗到细的流程中实现全球图像地理定位，在不依赖多模态大语言模型的情况下达到了最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 互联网照片与街景图像具有互补优势：前者更符合用户拍摄查询的视觉分布，后者提供更密集、地理覆盖更全面的数据。以往工作将二者作为独立任务处理，未能充分利用其互补性。

Method: 采用粗到细的检索-重排序架构。首先，在Flickr用户上传照片和街景图像上联合训练对比图像-GPS嵌入模型，学习共享的图像-GPS嵌入空间以检索候选位置。然后，利用基于注意力的重排序器，结合候选级别的视觉与GPS特征以及来自邻近位置的跨源证据，对检索到的候选位置重新评分。

Result: 在互联网照片基准（IM2GPS3k、YFCC4k）和街景图像基准（OSV-5M）的所有指标上均达到最先进结果。

Conclusion: Pinpoint通过融合互补数据源并采用高效的检索-重排序架构，在不依赖多模态大语言模型的前提下，实现了更快、更具可复现性的推断，并取得了最优的地理定位性能。

Original Abstract: Image geolocation aims to estimate where a photograph was taken from its visual content. At worldwide scale, this remains challenging because visual evidence is often ambiguous, diverse, and unevenly distributed. Prior work has typically treated geolocation of ordinary internet photos and street-view imagery as separate tasks, despite their complementary strengths: internet photos better match the appearance distribution of user-captured queries, while street-view imagery provides denser, geographically grounded coverage. We present Pinpoint, a retrieve-and-rerank architecture that combines both sources in a coarse-to-fine pipeline. A contrastive image-GPS embedder is trained on both user-uploaded Flickr photos and street-view imagery, learning a shared image-GPS embedding space that is used to retrieve candidate locations. An attention-based reranker then rescores retrieved candidates by combining candidate-level visual and GPS features with cross-source evidence from nearby locations to ground the prediction. Unlike recent prior work, Pinpoint does not rely on multimodal large-language models, making inference faster and more reproducible. Pinpoint achieves state-of-the-art results across all metrics on standard benchmarks for internet photos (IM2GPS3k and YFCC4k) and street-view imagery (OSV-5M).

中文摘要翻译: 图像地理定位旨在通过视觉内容估计照片的拍摄位置。在全球尺度上，这仍然具有挑战性，因为视觉证据往往具有模糊性、多样性且分布不均。以往工作通常将普通互联网照片和街景图像的地理定位视为独立任务，尽管两者具有互补优势：互联网照片更符合用户拍摄查询的视觉分布，而街景图像则提供更密集、具有地理覆盖的参考数据。我们提出了Pinpoint，一种检索-重排序架构，在粗到细的流程中融合了这两种数据源。该模型在用户上传的Flickr照片和街景图像上联合训练了一个对比图像-GPS嵌入器，学习共享的图像-GPS嵌入空间以检索候选位置。随后，基于注意力的重排序器通过结合候选级别的视觉和GPS特征以及来自邻近位置的跨源证据，对检索到的候选位置重新评分，从而确定最终的预测位置。与近期工作不同，Pinpoint不依赖多模态大语言模型，使得推断速度更快且更具可复现性。Pinpoint在互联网照片基准（IM2GPS3k和YFCC4k）以及街景图像基准（OSV-5M）的所有指标上均达到了最先进的结果。

</details>


### [9] [An Open-Source Two-Stage Computer Vision Pipeline for Fine-Grained Vehicle Classification using Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05149)
*Gandhimathi Padmanaban,Fred Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源两阶段计算机视觉流水线，结合RT-DETR检测器和微调后的Vision Transformer，用于从自然场景视频中对车辆进行六类车身分类（乘用车、SUV、皮卡、小型货车、大型货车、商用卡车），并采用置信度保留机制处理不确定预测。在3805个超车事件上准确率达0.94，在域外数据上为0.89。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 车辆类型是自行车骑行者在与车辆超车碰撞中受伤严重程度的重要决定因素，但现有公开文献中缺乏能自动将车辆分类为与伤害风险相关的类别的工具。标准目标检测基准仅提供粗粒度标签，而现有细粒度识别系统缺乏部署鲁棒性评估。

Method: 提出一个开源两阶段计算机视觉流水线：第一阶段使用预训练RT-DETR检测器进行粗粒度车辆定位；第二阶段使用微调的Vision Transformer (ViT-Base/16) 进行六类车身分类。采用置信度保留机制，当softmax输出低于0.60时放弃第二阶段预测，输出未知标签而非错误分类。

Result: 在3805个域内超车事件中，流水线准确率达0.94，各类F1分数0.91-0.97；在311个域外事件中准确率为0.89，四个主要类别中有三个在域迁移下F1保持0.90以上，小型货车退化最大（F1=0.72），主要由放弃率从2.4%升至25.0%驱动而非主动误分类。

Conclusion: 该两阶段流水线结合置信度保留机制能有效实现车辆细粒度分类，在域内和域外数据上均表现良好，且模型不确定性可通过放弃机制可靠传播。开源发布的完整流水线支持可重复性和在自行车安全研究中的复用。

Original Abstract: Vehicle body type is a significant determinant of cyclist injury severity in overtaking crashes, yet automated tools for classifying vehicles into injury-risk-relevant categories from naturalistic roadway video do not exist in the open literature. Standard object detection benchmarks provide only coarse vehicle labels (car, truck, bus, motorcycle), while existing fine-grained recognition systems are trained on controlled imagery and lack evaluation for deployment robustness across recording sites. This paper presents an open-source two-stage computer vision pipeline combining a pre-trained RT-DETR detector for coarse vehicle localization with a fine-tuned Vision Transformer (ViT-Base/16) for six-category body-type classification: passenger car, SUV, pickup truck, minivan, large van, and commercial truck. A confidence-based abstention mechanism withholds Stage 2 predictions when softmax output falls below 0.60, producing unknown labels rather than silent misclassifications. Evaluated on 3,805 annotated overtaking events from a bicycle-lane corridor in Ann Arbor, Michigan (in-distribution), the pipeline achieved 0.94 accuracy with per-class F1 scores from 0.91 (minivan) to 0.97 (SUV). On an independent out-of-distribution evaluation of 311 events from an open cycling dataset without retraining, accuracy was 0.89. Three of four well-represented categories maintained F1 at or above 0.90 under domain shift. The largest degradation was observed for minivan (F1 = 0.72), driven by abstention rate rising from 2.4% to 25.0% rather than active misclassification, consistent with the mechanism propagating genuine model uncertainty. The full pipeline, including inference scripts, training code, evaluation utilities, and model weights, is released as open-source software to support reproducibility and reuse across roadside video archives and cycling safety research.

中文摘要翻译: 车辆车身类型是超车碰撞中自行车骑行者受伤严重程度的重要决定因素，然而，公开文献中尚不存在能够从自然场景道路视频中将车辆自动分类为与伤害风险相关的类别的工具。标准目标检测基准仅提供粗粒度车辆标签（轿车、卡车、公交车、摩托车），而现有的细粒度识别系统在受控图像上训练，缺乏跨记录站点部署鲁棒性的评估。本文提出一个开源两阶段计算机视觉流水线，结合预训练的RT-DETR检测器用于粗粒度车辆定位，以及微调的Vision Transformer (ViT-Base/16) 用于六类车身分类：乘用车、SUV、皮卡、小型货车、大型货车和商用卡车。基于置信度的保留机制在softmax输出低于0.60时放弃第二阶段预测，生成未知标签而非静默误分类。在来自密歇根州安娜堡自行车道走廊的3805个标注超车事件（分布内）上评估，流水线达到0.94的准确率，各类F1分数从0.91（小型货车）到0.97（SUV）。在来自开放骑行数据集的311个事件的独立分布外评估中（无需重新训练），准确率为0.89。四个代表性良好的类别中有三个在域迁移下F1保持0.90及以上。最大退化出现在小型货车（F1=0.72），由放弃率从2.4%升至25.0%驱动，而非主动误分类，这与传播真实模型不确定性的机制一致。完整流水线，包括推理脚本、训练代码、评估工具和模型权重，已作为开源软件发布，以支持跨道路视频档案和自行车安全研究的可重复性和复用。

</details>


### [10] [End-to-End Text Line Detection and Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04166)
*Benjamin Kiessling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Orli，一种端到端模型，将历史文档的文本行检测和阅读顺序排序统一为图像到序列的生成问题，采用和弦框架参数化文本行基线，在多个基准上达到或超越现有最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有历史文档文本识别流程中，布局分析被分解为行检测和阅读顺序排序两个子任务，后者通常依赖手工编码的几何启发式方法，难以处理旁注、多栏、表格等复杂布局，缺乏通用性和鲁棒性。

Method: 将行检测和阅读顺序排序统一为图像到序列问题，使用和弦框架参数化（chord-frame parameterization）表示基线，通过垂直偏移编码局部几何；采用迭代精化头和局部视觉精化器生成最终曲线，以自回归方式直接从页面图像生成按阅读顺序排列的文本行基线。

Result: 在涵盖10种书写系统的196,691页异构语料上训练，在cBAD行检测上略超此前最优结果（无需数据集特定训练），在多个阅读顺序基准上零样本达到近乎完美的覆盖率和排序，在领域外布局上通过少量微调即可适应。

Conclusion: Orli有效统一了历史文档的文本行检测与阅读顺序排序任务，提供了一个端到端的通用解决方案，开源代码和模型权重有利于后续研究和应用。

Original Abstract: Practical text-recognition pipelines for historical documents typically decompose layout analysis into line detection followed by a separate reading-order step, with the latter most often handled by a hand-coded geometric heuristic that struggles with marginalia, multiple columns, tables, and source-specific editorial conventions. This article introduces Orli (Ordered Regression of Lines), an end-to-end model that casts both sub-tasks as a single image-to-sequence problem: from a page image, Orli autoregressively generates text-line baselines directly in reading order. Baselines are represented in a chord-frame parameterization that anchors a line's position, orientation, and extent while encoding local geometry through perpendicular offsets; an iterative refinement head and a local visual refiner produce the final curve. Trained on a heterogeneous corpus of 196,691 pages spanning ten writing systems, Orli marginally exceeds the previously reported state of the art for cBAD line detection without dataset-specific training, reaches near perfect coverage and ordering on multiple reading-order benchmarks zero-shot, and adapts to more specialized out-of-domain layouts with limited fine-tuning. The method's source code and model weights are available under an open license at https://github.com/mittagessen/orli.

中文摘要翻译: 针对历史文档的实用文本识别流程通常将布局分析分解为行检测和后续独立的阅读顺序排序步骤，其中后者多由手工编码的几何启发式方法处理，在旁注、多栏、表格以及特定来源的编辑惯例上表现不佳。本文提出Orli（Ordered Regression of Lines），一种端到端模型，将两个子任务统一为单个图像到序列问题：给定页面图像，Orli以自回归方式直接按阅读顺序生成文本行基线。基线采用和弦框架参数化表示，该框架锚定文本行的位置、方向和范围，同时通过垂直偏移编码局部几何；迭代精化头和局部视觉精化器生成最终曲线。在涵盖十种书写系统的196,691页异构语料上训练后，Orli在无需数据集特定训练的情况下略超此前cBAD行检测的最先进水平，在多个阅读顺序基准上零样本达到近乎完美的覆盖率和排序，并且能通过少量微调适应更专业的领域外布局。该方法的源代码和模型权重以开放许可形式发布于https://github.com/mittagessen/orli。

</details>


### [11] [GroupToM-Bench: Benchmarking Group Theory of Mind and Nonlinear Social Emergence in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04184)
*Weidong Tang,Jierui Li,Yueling Hou,Zihan Mei,Can Zhang,Xinyan Wan,Zhiyuan Liang,Pengfei Zhou,Yang You,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了GroupToM-Bench，首个针对群体级心智理论（ToM）的多模态基准测试，通过七级认知审计框架评估多模态大语言模型在微观（BDI状态）、中观（群体张力与结构约束）和宏观（结果预测与机制归因）三个层面的集体行为推理能力，发现现有模型与人类基线存在显著差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在个体级心智推理方面虽有进展，但在群体级心智理论任务上表现不佳。集体行为从社会张力、 conformity动态和结构约束中非线性涌现，无法通过简单求和个体意图来恢复，因此需要专门的基准来评估模型在这方面的能力。

Method: 构建GroupToM-Bench多模态基准，基于从微观BDI状态（信念、欲望、意图）、中观群体张力与结构约束到宏观结果预测与机制归因的因果链，设计了七级认知审计框架来全面探测模型的群体级ToM推理能力。

Result: 实验表明当前多模态大语言模型与人类基线之间存在显著差距，模型在处理社会结构和非线性集体动态方面存在失败。

Conclusion: 现有模型缺乏真正的通用智能所必需的群体级社会世界模型能力，尤其是在处理社会结构和非线性集体动态方面存在明显不足。

Original Abstract: True general intelligence requires not only a model of the physical world but also a social world model: the capacity to infer how individual mental states interact and crystallize into group-level outcomes. Despite notable progress in individual-level Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning, existing multimodal large language models fail at this broader task. Collective behavior emerges non-linearly from social tensions, conformity dynamics, and structural constraints, meaning it cannot be recovered by merely summing individual intentions. We present GroupToM-Bench, the first multimodal benchmark for group-level ToM, built around a causal chain spanning micro-level BDI states (belief, desire, intention), meso-level group tension and structural constraints, and macro-level outcome prediction and mechanistic attribution. To probe this full arc, we develop a seven-level cognitive audit framework. Experiments reveal a gap between current models and human baselines, highlighting a failure to process social structures and non-linear collective dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 真正的通用智能不仅需要对物理世界建模，还需要对社会世界建模：即推断个体心理状态如何相互作用并结晶为群体层面结果的能力。尽管在个体级心智理论推理方面取得了显著进展，但现有的多模态大语言模型在这一更广泛的任务上表现不佳。集体行为从社会张力、从众动态和结构约束中非线性涌现，这意味着它无法通过简单求和个体意图来恢复。我们提出了GroupToM-Bench，这是首个针对群体级心智理论的多模态基准测试，它构建于一个因果链之上，该因果链涵盖微观层面的BDI状态（信念、欲望、意图）、中观层面的群体张力和结构约束，以及宏观层面的结果预测和机制归因。为了全面探测这一完整链路，我们开发了一个七级认知审计框架。实验揭示了当前模型与人类基线之间的差距，突显了模型在处理社会结构和非线性集体动态方面的失败。

</details>


### [12] [Spatial Artifact Coherence Determines Codec Robustness in Patch-Based rPPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04198)
*Achraf Ben Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间伪影相干性（SAC）这一物理度量，用于解释为何在视频编解码压缩下，空间分解方法优于全局投影方法，并据此设计PatchPCA算法族。实验表明SAC可解释93.8%的编解码变体间PCA优势差异，为远程临床监测中的rPPG算法选择提供了物理依据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有rPPG研究在无压缩基准上表现良好，但实际部署于远程医疗、新生儿ICU等压缩视频场景。此前没有工作从物理量角度解释为何空间分解方法在编解码压缩下优于全局投影方法。

Method: 定义空间伪影相干性（SAC），即4×4跨块绿色通道协方差矩阵中非对角与对角能量的比值（带通0.75-2.5 Hz）；提出PatchPCA算法族（四种编解码感知的rPPG算法）；在3个公开数据集、280名受试者、11种编解码退化变体、13种算法上进行Wilcoxon检验评估。

Result: SAC解释了93.8%的编解码变体间PCA优势方差（r=+0.969）；非MPEG-4变体SAC在0.10-0.18区间，PCA胜率84-90%；MPEG-4变体SAC在0.48-0.59区间，胜率61%，平均改进幅度降低5.8倍；78%的受试者确认预期模式（p<10^-22, dz=0.73）；MPEG-4的影响是结构性的（宏块DCT几何特性），而非噪声幅度所致。

Conclusion: SAC为编解码感知的rPPG算法选择提供了物理度量标准；P-Hybrid被认为是最鲁棒的部署算法；PatchPCA优势的两个必要条件是SAC<0.30且运动幅度低至中等，这直接排除了原始到MPEG-4转码流水线。

Original Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) achieves low heart-rate error on uncompressed benchmarks yet is deployed over compressed video channels in telehealth, neonatal ICU, and driver fatigue applications. No prior work identifies the physical quantity determining when spatial decomposition outperforms global-projection methods under codec compression.
  We propose Spatial Artifact Coherence (SAC), defined as the ratio of off-diagonal to diagonal energy in the 4x4 inter-patch Green-channel covariance matrix (bandpass 0.75-2.5 Hz), and the PatchPCA algorithm family (four codec-aware rPPG algorithms). We evaluate 280 subjects across three public datasets, 11 codec degradation variants (MPEG-4, H.265, H.264, JPEG, chroma subsampling), and 13 algorithms via Wilcoxon tests (BH-FDR, q < 0.05, 904 tests).
  SAC explains 93.8% of between-variant variance in PCA advantage (r = +0.969), with zero overlap between codec families: non-MPEG-4 variants cluster at SAC 0.10-0.18 with 84-90% PCA win rates, while MPEG-4 variants cluster at SAC 0.48-0.59 with 61% win rate and a 5.8x reduction in mean improvement. Within subjects, 78% confirm the expected pattern (p < 10^-22, dz = 0.73). Within-variant subject-level SAC correlation is r = +0.099, confirming SAC classifies codec families rather than predicting individual outcomes. MPEG-4's effect is structural (macroblock DCT geometry, not noise amplitude), governed by source codec state, not resolution.
  P-Hybrid is identified as the most deployment-robust algorithm. Two necessary operating conditions for PatchPCA advantage are established: SAC < 0.30 and low-to-moderate motion, directly ruling out raw-to-MPEG-4 transcoding pipelines. SAC provides a physically grounded metric for codec-aware rPPG algorithm selection in clinical remote monitoring systems.

中文摘要翻译: 远程光电容积描记法（rPPG）在无压缩基准上可实现低心率误差，但在远程医疗、新生儿重症监护室和驾驶员疲劳监测等应用中，rPPG通过压缩视频通道部署。此前没有工作确定当视频编解码压缩下，空间分解方法何时优于全局投影方法的物理量。我们提出了空间伪影相干性（SAC），定义为4×4跨块绿色通道协方差矩阵（带通0.75-2.5 Hz）中非对角能量与对角能量的比值，以及PatchPCA算法族（四种编解码感知的rPPG算法）。我们在三个公开数据集、280名受试者、11种编解码退化变体（MPEG-4、H.265、H.264、JPEG、色度子采样）和13种算法上，通过Wilcoxon检验（BH-FDR，q<0.05，904次检验）进行了评估。SAC解释了PCA优势中93.8%的变体间方差（r=+0.969），编解码族之间无重叠：非MPEG-4变体聚类在SAC 0.10-0.18区间，PCA胜率84-90%；而MPEG-4变体聚类在SAC 0.48-0.59区间，胜率61%，平均改进幅度降低5.8倍。在受试者层面，78%确认了预期模式（p<10^-22，dz=0.73）。变体内部受试者水平的SAC相关性为r=+0.099，证实SAC分类的是编解码族而非预测个体结果。MPEG-4的影响是结构性的（宏块DCT几何特性，而非噪声幅度），受源编解码状态而非分辨率控制。P-Hybrid被确定为部署鲁棒性最强的算法。建立了PatchPCA优势的两个必要运行条件：SAC<0.30且低至中等运动，这直接排除了原始到MPEG-4转码流水线。SAC为临床远程监测系统中编解码感知的rPPG算法选择提供了物理度量标准。

</details>


### [13] [Overview of the EReL@MIR 2025 Multimodal Document Retrieval Challenge (Track 1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04240)
*Jingbiao Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首届多模态文档检索挑战赛（MIR Challenge Track 1）的设计、数据集、评估协议和最终排名，并分析了三个获胜系统的技术方案。所有获胜系统均基于Qwen2-VL系列多模态大语言模型嵌入器，主要差异在于是否使用微调集成、无训练多路融合或零样本后期交互。无训练系统与微调获胜者差距在0.1分以内。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉丰富的文档（图文表格混排）检索对多模态检索增强生成至关重要，但大多数检索器仍忽略视觉通道。为填补这一空白，举办了多模态文档检索挑战赛，旨在推动构建统一的检索系统，同时处理闭集文档页面检索和开放域多模态段落检索两种互补任务。

Method: 挑战赛设计了两个互补任务：闭集长文档页面检索（MMDocIR）和开放域维基百科风格段落检索（M2KR）。系统按两个任务上Recall@1,3,5的宏观平均排名。获胜系统均采用基于解码器的Qwen2-VL多模态大语言模型嵌入器，三种方案分别为：微调集成、无训练多路融合+强视觉语言重排序器、零样本后期交互。

Result: 挑战赛吸引了22支团队共455名参赛者提交586次。所有获胜系统均采用Qwen2-VL系列模型而非CLIP风格编码器。无训练多路融合系统与微调获胜者的分数差距在0.1分以内。

Conclusion: 基于解码器的多模态大语言模型嵌入器在视觉丰富文档检索任务上优于CLIP风格编码器；无训练的后期交互或多路融合方案可以达到接近微调集成的性能，展示了零样本后期交互的潜力。

Original Abstract: Retrieval over visually-rich documents, pages that interleave text with figures, tables, and charts, is essential for multimodal retrieval-augmented generation, yet most retrievers still discard the visual channel. The \emph{Multimodal Document Retrieval Challenge}, Track~1 of the MIR Challenge at the first EReL@MIR workshop, co-located with The Web Conference 2025, asks participants to build a \emph{single} retrieval system that handles two complementary regimes: closed-set document page retrieval within long documents from a text query (MMDocIR), and open-domain retrieval of Wikipedia-style passages from an image or image-plus-text query (M2KR). Systems are ranked by the macro-average of mean Recall@$\{1,3,5\}$ over the two tasks. The challenge drew 455 entrants and 586 submissions across 22 teams. This report describes the challenge design, datasets, and evaluation protocol; reports the final standings; and analyses the three winning teams' systems. All three build on decoder-based Multimodal-LLM embedders from the Qwen2-VL family rather than on CLIP-style encoders, and differ chiefly in whether they reach the top through fine-tuned ensembles, training-free multi-route fusion with a strong vision-language re-ranker, or zero-shot late interaction. The training-free system finished within $0.1$ point of the fine-tuned winner.

中文摘要翻译: 检索视觉丰富的文档——即文本与图表、表格和图片交错排列的页面——对于多模态检索增强生成至关重要，然而大多数检索器仍然舍弃了视觉通道。多模态文档检索挑战赛（MIR挑战赛第一赛道）由首届EReL@MIR研讨会举办，与2025年国际万维网大会同期举行，要求参与者构建一个统一的检索系统，以处理两种互补的任务场景：基于文本查询在长文档中进行闭集的文档页面检索（MMDocIR），以及基于图像或图文混合查询在开放域中检索维基百科风格的段落（M2KR）。系统按两个任务上Recall@1,3,5的宏观平均排名。该挑战赛共吸引了22支团队、455名参赛者，提交了586次结果。本报告描述了挑战赛的设计、数据集和评估协议，公布了最终排名，并分析了三个获胜团队的方案。所有获胜方案均基于Qwen2-VL系列解码器式多模态大语言模型嵌入器，而非CLIP风格的编码器；它们的主要区别在于：是通过微调集成、无训练的多路融合搭配强视觉语言重排序器，还是零样本的后期交互来取得领先。无训练方案与微调获胜者的分数差距在0.1分以内。

</details>


### [14] [SBP-Net: Learning Thin Structure Reconstruction with Sliding-Box Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04251)
*Ofir Gilad,Andrei Sharf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部深度投影的薄结构3D重建方法，通过滑动窗口生成正交深度投影，利用神经网络在2D中重建缺失的薄结构，再融合回3D模型，在血管和管道重建任务上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 由于薄3D结构存在稀疏性、尺度变化大和几何复杂等问题，现有神经方法在处理密集表面时表现良好，但难以恢复精细的薄几何结构。

Method: 使用滑动框遍历3D模型，生成本地正交深度投影作为2D表示，通过神经网络处理这些投影以重建缺失的薄结构，最后将局部重建结果融合回3D模型，形成连贯且细节丰富的形状。

Result: 在CT体积的肺动脉重建和合成/真实扫描的工业管道重建实验中，相比现有方法，该方法更好地保留了精细结构细节。

Conclusion: 基于局部深度投影的方法能有效恢复薄3D结构的精细几何细节，在医学和工业领域具有实用价值。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing thin 3D structures is challenging due to their sparsity, scale variation, and complex geometry. Such structures arise in a wide range of domains, including medical imaging of vascular systems and industrial pipe systems. While recent neural methods perform well on dense surfaces, they often fail to recover fine thin geometries. We propose a reconstruction approach based on local depth projections, which provide an efficient and informative 2D representation of thin structures. Specifically, we traverse the 3D model with a sliding box to generate local orthographic depth projections, which are processed by a neural network to reconstruct missing thin structures in 2D. The local reconstructions are subsequently fused back into the 3D model to produce a coherent and detailed shape. Experiments on pulmonary artery reconstruction from CT volumes and industrial pipeline recovery from synthetic and real scans demonstrate improved preservation of fine structural details over existing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 重建薄3D结构因其稀疏性、尺度变化和复杂几何形状而具有挑战性。这类结构广泛出现在医学成像中的血管系统和工业管道系统中。尽管最近的神经方法在密集表面上表现良好，但它们通常难以恢复精细的薄几何结构。我们提出了一种基于局部深度投影的重建方法，该方法为薄结构提供了高效且信息丰富的2D表示。具体来说，我们使用滑动框遍历3D模型，生成本地正交深度投影，然后通过神经网络处理这些投影，在2D中重建缺失的薄结构。随后，将局部重建结果融合回3D模型，以产生连贯且细节丰富的形状。在CT体积的肺动脉重建以及合成和真实扫描的工业管道重建实验中，该方法相比现有方法更好地保留了精细结构细节。

</details>


### [15] [UniCanvas: A Diffusion-base Unified Model for Text-in-Image Joint Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04264)
*Zeyuan Yang,Hao-Wei Chen,Xueyang Yu,Yuncong Yang,Haoyu Zhen,Ziqiao Ma,Maohao Shen,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCanvas 首次尝试通过文本-图像融合生成的方式，在扩散模型中统一处理多模态内容。模型将语言表示为图像中的视觉图案，在共享像素画布上同时生成文本和图像，实现了无缝的多模态生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的统一视觉-语言模型要么（如自回归VLM）无法生成高质量图像，要么（如扩散模型）难以生成连贯文本，缺乏一个能同时处理视觉和文本生成的统一模型。

Method: 提出UniCanvas，利用扩散模型在共享像素画布上进行变换的能力，将语言嵌入为图像中的视觉模式，使模型在图像合成过程中自然地"绘制"文本，实现文本与图像在统一像素空间中的联合生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，UniCanvas在性能上优于先前的统一模型，证明了基于扩散模型的文本-图像生成作为一种统一多模态生成范式的有效性。

Conclusion: UniCanvas验证了将扩散模型用于文本-图像融合生成是统一多模态生成的有前途的新范式，能够同时处理视觉和文本生成任务。

Original Abstract: Recent years have seen remarkable progress in unified vision-language models handling both multimodal understanding and generation within a single architecture. While autoregressive VLMs can reason across modalities, they fail to generate high-quality images. In contrast, diffusion models produce photorealistic visuals yet struggle to generate coherent text, making it challenging to develop a single unified model that can seamlessly handle both visual and text generation. Recent advances suggest that language can be effectively embedded within visual representations, allowing models to reason about textual semantics directly from images. To this end, we propose UniCanvas, a first attempt that unifies diffusion models to generate interleaved multimodal contents through text-in-image generation. Diffusion models naturally capture transformations on a shared pixel canvas, which can be viewed as world models of visual change. Instead of producing discrete text tokens, the model learns to represent language as visual patterns inside images, leveraging its inherent multimodal embedding space. This design allows the model to "draw" text naturally within a single pixel canvas during image synthesis, achieving seamless multimodal generation. Experiments demonstrate that UniCanvas improves performance over previous unified models, positioning text-in-image generation with diffusion models as a promising unified multimodal generation paradigm.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，在单一架构内同时处理多模态理解与生成的统一视觉-语言模型取得了显著进展。虽然自回归视觉语言模型能够跨模态进行推理，但它们无法生成高质量的图像。相比之下，扩散模型可以生成逼真的视觉内容，却难以产生连贯的文本，这使得开发一个能无缝处理视觉和文本生成的统一模型充满挑战。最新研究表明，语言可以有效地嵌入到视觉表征中，使模型能够直接从图像中推理文本语义。为此，我们提出了UniCanvas，这是首次尝试通过文本-图像融合生成来统一扩散模型以生成交错多模态内容。扩散模型天然地捕捉共享像素画布上的变换，这可以视为视觉变化的世界模型。该模型不生成离散的文本标记，而是学习将语言表示为图像内部的视觉模式，利用其固有的多模态嵌入空间。这种设计使得模型在图像合成过程中能够在单个像素画布上自然地"绘制"文本，从而实现无缝的多模态生成。实验表明，UniCanvas在性能上优于先前的统一模型，将基于扩散模型的文本-图像生成定位为一种有前景的统一多模态生成范式。

</details>


### [16] [StandardE2E: A Unified Framework for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04271)
*Stepan Konev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StandardE2E是一个统一接口框架，标准化了多个端到端自动驾驶数据集（如Waymo、Argoverse等）的预处理、加载和多数据集联合训练流程，并以开源Python包形式发布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶数据集各自使用不同的文件格式、API、坐标系和模态覆盖，导致跨数据集实验和预处理工作重复实现，缺乏统一的标准化接口。

Method: 提出StandardE2E框架：(i) 在统一数据模式下标准化各个数据集的预处理流程；(ii) 将多个数据集整合到单个PyTorch DataLoader中，支持跨数据集预训练、辅助任务监督和场景级过滤；(iii) 新增数据集只需编写从原始帧到标准模式的映射，下游流程无需修改。

Result: 开箱支持6个数据集：Waymo End-to-End、Waymo Perception、Argoverse 2 Sensor、Argoverse 2 LiDAR、NAVSIM (OpenScene-v1.1)和WayveScenes101，并作为开源Python包发布。

Conclusion: StandardE2E通过统一接口显著降低了跨数据集实验的重复工作，简化了新数据集的接入流程，为端到端自动驾驶研究提供标准化基础工具。

Original Abstract: Autonomous driving has shifted from modular perception-prediction-planning stacks toward end-to-end (E2E) models that map sensor inputs directly to vehicle control, often regularized by auxiliary tasks such as 3D detection, motion forecasting, and HD-map perception. Progress is driven by a fast-growing ecosystem of sensor-rich driving datasets, yet each ships its own file formats, APIs, coordinate conventions, and modality coverage, leaving cross-dataset experimentation and even basic per-dataset preprocessing to be re-implemented per project. We present StandardE2E, a framework that provides a single unified interface over E2E driving datasets. StandardE2E (i) standardizes per-dataset preprocessing under one shared data schema; (ii) combines multiple datasets in a single PyTorch DataLoader for cross-dataset pretraining, auxiliary-task supervision, and scenario-level filtering; and (iii) reduces adding a new dataset to a single per-dataset mapping from raw frames to the canonical schema, leaving the entire downstream pipeline unchanged. The framework supports six datasets out of the box: Waymo End-to-End, Waymo Perception, Argoverse 2 Sensor, Argoverse 2 LiDAR, NAVSIM (OpenScene-v1.1), and WayveScenes101, and is released as the open-source standard-e2e Python package, available at https://github.com/stepankonev/StandardE2E.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶已从模块化的感知-预测-规划流水线转向端到端模型，后者直接将传感器输入映射到车辆控制，通常通过3D检测、运动预测和高精地图感知等辅助任务进行正则化。该领域的进展得益于传感器丰富的驾驶数据集生态系统的快速发展，然而每个数据集都自带独特的文件格式、API、坐标约定和模态覆盖范围，导致跨数据集实验乃至基本的逐数据集预处理都需要在每个项目中重新实现。我们提出StandardE2E，一个为端到端驾驶数据集提供统一接口的框架。StandardE2E (i) 在统一数据模式下标标准化每个数据集的预处理流程；(ii) 将多个数据集整合到单个PyTorch DataLoader中，支持跨数据集预训练、辅助任务监督和场景级过滤；(iii) 新增数据集只需编写从原始帧到标准模式的映射，整个下游流程无需改动。该框架开箱支持六个数据集：Waymo End-to-End、Waymo Perception、Argoverse 2 Sensor、Argoverse 2 LiDAR、NAVSIM (OpenScene-v1.1)和WayveScenes101，并以开源standard-e2e Python包形式发布，代码地址为 https://github.com/stepankonev/StandardE2E。

</details>


### [17] [FindIt: A Format-Informed Visual Detection Benchmark for Generalist Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04282)
*Eshika Khandelwal,Jingjing Pan,Mingfang Zhang,Quan Kong,Lorenzo Garattoni,Hilde Kuehne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专门评估通用多模态大模型(MLLM)在可提示定位能力上的综合基准测试，涵盖目标检测、指代表达检测、实例级检测和视频检测四大任务，并通过统一框架标准化评估流程，揭示了当前MLLM在定位任务中的表现优劣及其对输出格式约束的高度敏感性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前多模态大模型(MLLM)的评估主要集中在自由形式的视觉-语言任务（如VQA、描述、摘要），但其实际应用已扩展到结构化计算机视觉任务（尤其是定位相关任务），然而缺乏一个标准化的基准来系统评估这些能力。因此，需要建立一个专门评估MLLM可提示定位能力的综合基准。

Method: 1) 构建了一个涵盖四类核心定位任务的基准：目标检测、指代表达检测、实例级检测和视频检测；2) 开发了统一的评估框架，标准化输入、强制可解析的边界框输出、定义透明的评估协议；3) 在多种开源和商业MLLM上进行系统评估。

Result: 1) 对多种开源和商业MLLM进行了深入分析，揭示了它们的性能与局限性；2) 发现当前系统对输出格式约束高度敏感，即使是微小的格式变化也常常导致泛化失败；3) 指出了最先进MLLM在定位场景中的优缺点。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在定位任务中虽有优势但存在明显不足，尤其是在输出格式遵循能力上表现脆弱；研究结果为改进多模态模型设计和评估指明了重要方向。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are predominantly evaluated on free-form vision-language tasks such as visual question answering, captioning, and summarization. However, their practical use is rapidly expanding to more structured computer vision settings, where users prompt models to perform localization-centric tasks such as object detection, often within larger agentic or decision-making systems. Despite this shift, there is currently no standardized benchmark that systematically evaluates these capabilities at scale. In this work, we introduce the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to assess the promptable localization abilities of generalist MLLMs. Our benchmark spans four core task categories: object detection, referring expression detection, instance-level detection, and video-based detection. To enable consistent and fair evaluation, we develop a unified framework that standardizes inputs, enforces parsable bounding box outputs, and defines transparent evaluation protocols across tasks. Using this suite, we evaluate a diverse set of open-source and proprietary MLLMs, providing an in-depth analysis of their performance and limitations. Beyond accuracy, we examine models' ability to adhere to output format specifications, showing that current systems are highly sensitive to formatting constraints and often fail to generalize even to minor variations. Our results highlight both the strengths and shortcomings of state-of-the-art MLLMs in localization settings, and point toward important directions for improving multimodal model design and evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型(Multimodal Large Language Models, MLLMs)主要在自由形式的视觉-语言任务上进行评估，例如视觉问答、图像描述和摘要生成。然而，它们的实际应用正迅速扩展到更结构化的计算机视觉场景中，用户提示模型执行以定位为核心的任务（如目标检测），通常是在更大的智能体或决策系统框架内。尽管发生了这种转变，目前尚无标准化的基准能够大规模系统地评估这些能力。在本工作中，我们提出了首个专门设计用于评估通用多模态大模型可提示定位能力的综合基准。该基准涵盖四类核心任务：目标检测、指代表达检测、实例级检测和视频检测。为了实现一致且公平的评估，我们开发了一个统一框架，该框架标准化了输入格式、强制生成可解析的边界框输出，并跨任务定义了透明的评估协议。利用这一评估套件，我们对多种开源和商业多模态大模型进行了评估，深入分析了它们的性能与局限性。除了准确性之外，我们还考察了模型遵循输出格式规范的能力，结果表明当前系统对格式约束高度敏感，即使是微小的格式变化也常常无法泛化。我们的研究结果既展示了最先进多模态大模型在定位场景中的优势与不足，也为改进多模态模型设计和评估指明了重要方向。

</details>


### [18] [A Cookbook of 3D Vision: Data, Learning Paradigms, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04291)
*Hongyang Du,Zongxia Li,Dawei Liu,Runhao Li,Haoyuan Song,Qingyu Zhang,Yubo Wang,Jingcheng Ni,Shihang Gui,Congchao Dong,Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以数据为中心的3D视觉分类法，将几何表示、数据集、学习框架和应用整合到一个统一的概念图中，系统分析了点云、网格、体素和3D高斯等表示方式，以及2D监督3D学习、隐式神经表示和4D世界建模等前沿方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前3D视觉领域在数据表示、基准测试和学习范式上高度碎片化，缺乏统一的视角来理解效率、保真度和可扩展性之间的平衡关系。

Method: 采用数据驱动的分类方法，构建了一个连接几何表示、数据集设计、学习框架和应用的概念图，综合分析点云、网格、体素、3D高斯等结构表示及其采集流程，并考察数据集设计、基准构建和监督范式对进展的影响。

Result: 阐明了不同表示、学习范式与下游任务（重建、生成、视频建模）之间的关系，揭示了在效率与保真度之间寻求平衡以及多模态几何 grounding 的新兴趋势。

Conclusion: 3D视觉正朝着效率与保真度平衡、多模态几何 grounding 的方向发展，本文提供的整合性视角有助于理解该领域的演变趋势。

Original Abstract: 3D vision has rapidly evolved, driven by increasingly diverse data representations, learning paradigms, and modeling strategies. Yet the field remains fragmented across representations and benchmarks, making it difficult to develop unified perspectives on efficiency, fidelity, and scalability. This work provides a data-centric taxonomy of 3D vision that connects geometric representations, datasets, learning frameworks, and applications within a single conceptual map. We begin by analysing the principal structural representations of 3D data--point clouds, meshes, voxels, and 3D Gaussians--along with their acquisition pipelines. We then examine how dataset design, benchmark construction, and supervision regimes shape recent advances, spanning 2D-supervised 3D learning, implicit neural representations, and 4D world modeling. Through this integrative lens, we clarify the relationships among representations, learning paradigms, and downstream tasks in reconstruction, generation, and video modeling, offering a consolidated view of emerging trends toward balancing efficiency and fidelity and toward multimodal geometric grounding.

中文摘要翻译: 3D视觉在日益多样化的数据表示、学习范式和建模策略的驱动下迅速发展。然而，该领域在表示方式和基准测试上仍然碎片化，使得难以建立关于效率、保真度和可扩展性的统一视角。本文提出了一种以数据为中心的3D视觉分类法，将几何表示、数据集、学习框架和应用连接到一个单一的概念图中。我们首先分析了3D数据的主要结构表示——点云、网格、体素和3D高斯——及其采集流程。然后，我们考察了数据集设计、基准构建和监督范式如何塑造了近期进展，涵盖2D监督的3D学习、隐式神经表示和4D世界建模。通过这种整合性视角，我们阐明了表示、学习范式与下游任务（重建、生成和视频建模）之间的关系，为理解新兴趋势——即在效率与保真度之间寻求平衡以及多模态几何 grounding——提供了统一的观点。

</details>


### [19] [XSSR: Cross-Domain Self-Supervised Representative Selection for Efficient Annotation in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04301)
*Byunghyun Ko,Aleksei Anisimov,Kobe Ke,Suhas Bharthepude,Jeongkyu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出XSSR框架，通过无监督预训练+贪婪选择算法在目标域仅用5%标注样本即可达到接近全量数据的医学图像分割性能，并自动校准多样性-新颖性平衡参数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像标注成本高昂，尤其在跨域场景（不同设备、人群、临床站点）下，目标域标注获取更加困难。现有方法缺乏在目标域极小标注预算下仍能保持高性能的解决方案。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在无标注源域数据上训练MAE，建立共享嵌入空间；(2) 贪心选择算法基于密度、新颖性和多样性三个指标对目标域未标注样本评分，并通过最小化嵌入空间覆盖度自动校准α参数；(3) 仅用所选子集训练U-Net分割模型。

Result: 在三个公开基准（胸部X光、RIGA+视网膜眼底、多站点前列腺MRI）上，固定5%标注预算下：胸部X光仅用22个标注样本即达全量数据99.3%性能；前列腺MRI上比随机选择高2.5 Dice点；在所有数据集上比CoreSet基线高0.4~1.2 Dice点。消融实验表明多样性是最关键的评分组件，跨站点性能与源域扫描仪相似度相关。

Conclusion: XSSR通过在无监督嵌入空间中进行代表性样本选择，能够在极小标注预算下有效实现跨域医学图像分割，且无需手动调参，显著降低了目标域标注成本。

Original Abstract: Acquiring labeled medical image data is resource-intensive and a challenge further exacerbated in cross-domain scenarios where source and target datasets differ in imaging equipment, population, or clinical site. This study introduces XSSR (Cross-Domain Self-Supervised Representative Selection), a framework designed to minimize annotation effort in the target domain while maintaining robust segmentation performance. XSSR comprises three stages: first, a Masked Autoencoder (MAE) is trained on unlabeled source data to establish a shared embedding space without requiring target labels; second, a greedy selection algorithm scores unlabeled target samples based on a composite density, novelty, and diversity criterion; and third, a U-Net segmentation model is trained exclusively on the selected subset. The novelty-diversity trade-off parameter, alpha, is automatically calibrated by minimizing embedding-space coverage, eliminating manual tuning. We evaluate XSSR on three public benchmarks: Chest X-ray, RIGA+ retinal fundus imaging, and multi-site Prostate MRI, each under a fixed 5% annotation budget. XSSR achieves 99.3% of full-data performance on Chest X-ray using only 22 labeled samples, surpasses random selection by up to 2.5 Dice points on Prostate MRI, and consistently outperforms the CoreSet baseline by 0.4 to 1.2 Dice points across all datasets. Ablation studies indicate that diversity is the most influential scoring component, and per-site analysis shows that performance correlates with scanner similarity to the source domain.

中文摘要翻译: 获取标注的医学图像数据需要大量资源，而在跨域场景（源域与目标域在成像设备、人群或临床站点上存在差异）中这一挑战更为严峻。本研究提出了XSSR（跨域自监督代表性选择）框架，旨在最小化目标域标注工作量的同时保持稳健的分割性能。XSSR包含三个阶段：首先，在无标注源域数据上训练掩码自编码器（MAE），建立无需目标标签的共享嵌入空间；其次，贪心选择算法基于密度、新颖性和多样性的复合标准对无标注目标样本进行评分；最后，仅在所选子集上训练U-Net分割模型。新颖性-多样性权衡参数α通过最小化嵌入空间覆盖度实现自动校准，无需手动调参。我们在三个公开基准上评估了XSSR：胸部X光、RIGA+视网膜眼底成像以及多站点前列腺MRI，均在固定5%标注预算下进行。XSSR在胸部X光上仅使用22个标注样本即达到全量数据性能的99.3%，在前列腺MRI上相比随机选择提升高达2.5个Dice点，并在所有数据集上持续超越CoreSet基线0.4至1.2个Dice点。消融研究表明多样性是最具影响力的评分组件，逐站点分析显示性能与目标站点和源域的扫描仪相似度相关。

</details>


### [20] [Answer Self-Consistency with Margin-Triggered Question Re-Arbitration for the CVPR 2026 VidLLMs Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04323)
*Tomoya Miyazawa,Hiroyasu Okuno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASC-MQRA框架，一种无需训练、在测试时通过答案自一致性与边际触发问题再仲裁提升视频关系推理性能的方法。在CVPR 2026 VidLLMs挑战赛Track 2中，ASC（答案自一致性）显著优于单次推理，而MQRA（边际触发问题再仲裁）在验证集上有提升但测试集上略有下降，最终提交版本采用ASC，在验证集和测试集上分别达到72.73%/78.34%和81.16%/80.91%的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视频中的视觉关系推理任务要求模型推断那些不总是显式可见的关系，单次推理往往不稳定。作者希望通过无需额外训练的测试时推理策略来提升多模态推理模型的鲁棒性和准确性，特别是利用投票分布中的不确定性信号来进一步优化推理结果。

Method: 提出ASC-MQRA框架：1) ASC（答案自一致性）：对同一视频问题进行多次随机推理，聚合答案选择通过答案级自一致性来提升稳定性；2) MQRA（边际触发问题再仲裁）：对第一轮投票分布中边际较低（不确定性高）的样本，缩小候选集并让模型重新观看视频后再次作答；最终提交版本仅使用ASC。

Result: 验证集上ASC达到72.73平均准确率和78.34类别宏平均准确率；测试集上达81.16和80.91。MQRA在验证集上进一步提升，但在测试集上略有下降，表明再仲裁对触发子集的大小和类别分布敏感。

Conclusion: 答案自一致性（ASC）是一种简单有效的测试时推理增强策略；边际触发问题再仲裁（MQRA）在验证集上有效但测试集上不稳定，未来需要更稳健的再仲裁机制；投票边际分布可作为有用的不确定性信号。

Original Abstract: In this report, we present our solution for Track 2 of the CVPR 2026 VidLLMs Challenge. This track evaluates visual relational reasoning in videos, where models must infer relations that are not always explicitly visible. We propose Answer Self-Consistency with Margin-Triggered Question Re-Arbitration (ASC-MQRA), a training-free test-time reasoning framework built on a multimodal reasoning model. The core ASC component performs multiple stochastic video question-answering runs and aggregates their answer choices through answer-level self-consistency. This substantially improves over single-pass inference and forms our final test submission. We further study MQRA, a conditional re-arbitration module for low-margin examples where the first-stage vote distribution indicates uncertainty. Our vote-margin analysis shows that low-margin examples often retain the ground-truth answer among the top candidates, motivating MQRA to narrow the candidate set and re-watch the video only over the retained candidates. On validation, MQRA further improves over ASC, indicating that low-margin vote distributions can provide a useful uncertainty signal. On test, however, MQRA slightly degrades performance relative to ASC, suggesting that re-arbitration is sensitive to the size and category distribution of the triggered subset. Our final test submission therefore uses ASC without re-arbitration, achieving 72.73 average accuracy and 78.34 category-wise macro average accuracy on validation, and 81.16 average accuracy and 80.91 category-wise macro average accuracy on test. This report details our prompting strategy, implementation setup, ablation studies, and diagnostic analyses. The code is available at https://github.com/data-analytics-labo/ASC-MQRA

中文摘要翻译: 本报告介绍了我们在CVPR 2026 VidLLMs挑战赛Track 2中的解决方案。该赛道评估视频中的视觉关系推理能力，模型需要推断那些并非总是显式可见的关系。我们提出了**答案自一致性结合边际触发问题再仲裁（ASC-MQRA）**，这是一种基于多模态推理模型、无需训练、在测试时进行的推理框架。核心组件ASC通过多次随机视频问答运行，并在答案级别进行自一致性聚合，显著优于单次推理，构成了我们的最终测试提交。我们进一步研究了MQRA，这是一个针对低边际样本（即第一阶段投票分布表明存在不确定性的样本）的条件性再仲裁模块。投票边际分析表明，低边际样本的正确答案通常仍保留在候选答案中，这促使MQRA缩小候选集并让模型仅针对保留的候选答案重新观看视频。在验证集上，MQRA相比ASC有进一步提升，表明低边际投票分布可作为有效的不确定性信号。然而在测试集上，MQRA的性能相比ASC略有下降，表明再仲裁对触发子集的大小和类别分布较为敏感。因此，我们的最终测试提交采用不含再仲裁的ASC，在验证集上达到72.73平均准确率和78.34类别宏平均准确率，在测试集上达到81.16平均准确率和80.91类别宏平均准确率。本报告详细介绍了我们的提示策略、实现配置、消融研究和诊断分析。代码已开源：https://github.com/data-analytics-labo/ASC-MQRA

</details>


### [21] [Robust Multi-view Clustering against Imperfect Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04343)
*Zhichao Huang,Haochen Zhou,Hao Wang,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种统一框架PLCI，通过将跨视角对应建模为潜在变量并融合实例级可靠性与原型级语义传输，同时解决多视角聚类中的不完整视角（IV）和噪声对应（NC）问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有IV和NC导向的多视角聚类方法分别针对单一问题设计，要求可靠的对应关系或足够完整的实例，无法同时应对两种不完美信息挑战。作者观察到两者根源相同——锚点实例在另一视角的对应信息可能缺失或不可靠，因此希望提出统一解决方案。

Method: 提出后验引导的潜在对应推理（PLCI）框架，将每个锚点实例的跨视角对应形式化为潜在变量，结合实例级可靠性评估和原型级语义传输来推断该潜在对应的后验分布，从而统一处理IV和NC问题。

Result: 在六个广泛使用的多视角数据集上，与10种最新多视角聚类方法相比，PLCI在处理不完美信息问题上表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: PLCI能够统一有效地处理多视角数据中的不完整视角和噪声对应问题，为不完美信息场景下的鲁棒多视角聚类提供了新的解决范式。

Original Abstract: Real-world multi-view data always suffer from imperfect information problem, where the view-specific observations are absent (i.e., Incomplete Views, IV) and cross-view correspondences are mismatched (i.e., Noisy Correspondences, NC) for certain instances. As a remedy, numerous IV- and NC-oriented multi-view clustering (MvC) methods have been proposed, which however require either reliable correspondences or sufficiently complete instances, thus stopping short of addressing the imperfect information problem. In contrast, we observe that both IV and NC challenges originate from the same issue of imperfect cross-view counterpart information, where the counterpart of an anchor instance in another view might be either unavailable or unreliable. Based on the observation, we propose a novel robust MvC framework, termed Posterior-guided Latent Counterpart Inference (PLCI), which could handle both IV and NC in a unified manner. Specifically, PLCI formulates the desired cross-view counterpart of each anchor instance as a latent variable, and integrates both instance-level reliability and prototype-level semantic transport to infer the posterior distribution of the latent counterpart. Extensive experiments on six widely-used multi-view datasets against 10 state-of-the-art MvC methods demonstrate the effectiveness of PLCI for tackling the imperfect information problem. The code will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界中的多视角数据常常存在不完美信息问题，即某些实例的视角特定观测缺失（即不完整视角，IV）以及跨视角对应关系错配（即噪声对应，NC）。为此，现有研究提出了大量面向IV或NC的多视角聚类方法，但这些方法要么需要可靠的对应关系，要么需要足够完整的实例，因而无法解决不完美信息问题。相比之下，我们观察到IV和NC挑战均源于同一问题——不完美的跨视角对应信息，即锚点实例在另一视角中的对应项可能不可用或不可靠。基于这一观察，我们提出了一种新颖的鲁棒多视角聚类框架，称为后验引导的潜在对应推理（PLCI），该框架能够以统一方式处理IV和NC问题。具体而言，PLCI将每个锚点实例所需的跨视角对应形式化为潜在变量，并融合实例级可靠性评估与原型级语义传输来推断该潜在对应的后验分布。在六个广泛使用的多视角数据集上，与10种最新的多视角聚类方法进行的大量实验表明，PLCI在处理不完美信息问题上的有效性。代码将在论文接收后发布。

</details>


### [22] [HYolo: An Intelligent IoT-Based Object Detection System Using Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04345)
*Isha Abid,Fawad Khan,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HYolo将超图学习集成到YOLO架构中，以建模对象与上下文特征间的高阶关系，在COCO数据集上mAP@50提升约12%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统YOLO模型主要捕捉成对特征交互，难以建模对象与上下文特征之间的复杂高阶关系，限制了检测性能。

Method: 在YOLO架构中引入超图学习，通过超图结构捕获更丰富的上下文依赖关系，增强目标表示能力。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，mAP@50提升约12%，检测精度和鲁棒性均显著优于基线YOLO模型。

Conclusion: 将超图学习集成到目标检测流程中，为构建智能且上下文感知的IoT视觉系统提供了有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: This paper presents HYolo, an intelligent IoT-based object detection framework that integrates hypergraph learning into the YOLO architecture. Traditional YOLO-based object detection models primarily capture pairwise feature interactions and may fail to model complex high-order relationships among objects and contextual features. To address this limitation, HYolo incorporates hypergraph learning to capture richer contextual dependencies and improve object representation. Experimental evaluation on the COCO dataset demonstrates significant performance improvements over baseline YOLO models. The proposed approach achieves approximately 12% improvement in mAP@50 while enhancing overall detection accuracy and robustness. By modeling high-order feature relationships, HYolo provides improved contextual understanding and more reliable object detection performance in IoT-based environments. The results indicate that integrating hypergraph learning into object detection pipelines offers a promising direction for intelligent and context-aware IoT vision systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出HYolo——一种智能的基于IoT的目标检测框架，将超图学习集成到YOLO架构中。传统的基于YOLO的目标检测模型主要捕捉成对特征交互，可能无法建模对象与上下文特征之间的复杂高阶关系。为解决该局限性，HYolo引入超图学习以捕获更丰富的上下文依赖关系并改进目标表示。在COCO数据集上的实验评估表明，该方法相较于基线YOLO模型取得了显著的性能提升。所提出的方法在mAP@50上提升约12%，同时增强了整体检测精度和鲁棒性。通过建模高阶特征关系，HYolo在基于IoT的环境中提供了改进的上下文理解能力和更可靠的目标检测性能。结果表明，将超图学习集成到目标检测流程中为智能且上下文感知的IoT视觉系统提供了有前景的研究方向。

</details>


### [23] [MorphoQuant: Modality-Aware Quantization for Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04349)
*Yue Wu,Changyuan Wang,Zixuan Wang,Shilin Ma,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MorphoQuant，一种面向4-bit全模态大语言模型（OLLMs）的模态感知后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过分布感知偏置补偿（DABC）和形态导向量化函数优化（MDQFO）解决跨模态分布异质性与离群点模式差异问题，在W4A4量化下超越SOTA方法，甚至优于W4A16基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统PTQ方法在处理4-bit全模态大语言模型时面临极端分布异质性和跨模态离群点模式差异，导致量化精度严重下降，亟需一种能感知模态特性并保留跨模态形态的量化框架。

Method: 1) 分布感知偏置补偿（DABC）：将长尾离群点选择性吸收到通道偏置中，保留离群点幅值的同时对密集内嵌点进行高精度离散化。2) 形态导向量化函数优化（MDQFO）：联合优化量化网格与偏置掩码，实现跨模态的细粒度对齐。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni模型上，W4A4量化在ScienceQA上达到76.63%，显著超越现有SOTA W4A4方法，并出人意料地超过W4A16基线，展现了优异的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: MorphoQuant框架有效解决了全模态大模型在低位宽量化下的跨模态分布异质性问题，证明了模态感知的量化策略能够在极致压缩下保持甚至提升模型精度。

Original Abstract: Conventional Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) methods struggle with 4-bit Omni-modal Large Language Models (OLLMs) due to the extreme distribution heterogeneity and disparate outlier patterns across modalities. To address this, we propose MorphoQuant, a modality-aware PTQ framework engineered to preserve cross-modal morphology and mitigate outlier loss. Specifically, we introduce Distribution-Aware Bias Compensation (DABC), which selectively absorbs long-tailed outliers into channel-wise biases. This mechanism safeguards outlier magnitudes while maintaining high-precision discretization for dense inliers, thereby preserving accurate discretization across diverse modal distribution. Complementing this, we propose Morphology-Directed Quantization Function Optimization (MDQFO) to co-optimize the quantization grid with the bias mask, ensuring fine-grained alignment across modalities. Extensive evaluations on Qwen2.5-Omni across benchmarks like MMMU and Video-MME demonstrate our approach's superiority. Notably, our W4A4 model achieves 76.63% on ScienceQA, significantly outperforming SOTA W4A4 methods and surprisingly surpassing the W4A16 baseline, which fully demonstrates the exceptional accuracy-efficiency trade-off of our framework.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的后训练量化（PTQ）方法在处理4比特全模态大语言模型（OLLMs）时，由于跨模态间极端的分布异质性和差异化的离群点模式而面临挑战。为此，我们提出MorphoQuant，一个模态感知的PTQ框架，旨在保留跨模态形态并缓解离群点损失。具体地，我们引入了分布感知偏置补偿（DABC），该方法选择性地将长尾离群点吸收到通道偏置中。该机制在保护离群点幅值的同时，对密集内嵌点保持高精度离散化，从而在多样化的模态分布上实现精确的离散化。在此基础上，我们提出形态导向量化函数优化（MDQFO），联合优化量化网格与偏置掩码，确保跨模态的细粒度对齐。在Qwen2.5-Omni模型上，针对MMMU和Video-MME等基准的广泛评估展示了我们方法的优越性。值得注意的是，我们的W4A4模型在ScienceQA上达到76.63%，显著超越了当前最先进的W4A4方法，并出人意料地超过了W4A16基线，这充分证明了我们框架在精度与效率之间实现了卓越的权衡。

</details>


### [24] [Video2LoRA: Parametric Video Internalization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04351)
*Manan Suri,Sarvesh Baskar,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video2LoRA提出了一种通过感知器超网络从视频中直接预测LoRA适配器参数的方法，将视频信息内化为模型参数，从而在推理时消除视觉token，大幅降低计算开销，并在视频摘要、字幕和问答任务上达到与直接视频上下文推理相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言模型处理视频时，每帧视频需占用上百个token，推理成本随帧数和查询次数线性增长，计算开销巨大。现有方法需要将视频帧作为上下文输入，导致高延迟和高token消耗，亟需更高效的视频信息内化方式。

Method: 使用感知器超网络读取冻结的视觉-语言模型在逐层编码视频时产生的中间表示，并在单次前向传播中直接生成LoRA适配器参数。训练完成后，冻结的VLM可仅凭该适配器回答查询，推理时上下文中不含任何视觉token。

Result: 在SmolVLM2 500M和2.2B两个规模上，Video2LoRA在5个字幕基准上均达到非劣效于直接视频上下文推理的性能；在8个视频问答基准-规模配对中7个达到同等水平。支持高达1024帧和1024px分辨率（训练时仅用12帧384px），将推理时视觉token负载降低最多1500倍，查询首token延迟(TTFT)降低6-80倍。独立生成的非重叠视频段适配器可在秩空间组合。

Conclusion: Video2LoRA通过参数化视频内化有效解决了视频推理的高计算成本问题，在保持输出质量的前提下显著降低推理开销，并且支持长视频分块内化与组合，为高效长视频理解提供了新路径。

Original Abstract: Processing video in vision-language models is expensive: each frame occupies hundreds of tokens, and inference cost scales with every frame and every repeated query. We introduce Video2LoRA, a method for parametric video internalization. A perceiver hypernetwork reads the intermediate representations produced layer-by-layer as a frozen VLM encodes a video, and generates a Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapter in a single forward pass. Unlike standard LoRA fine-tuning, which requires iterative gradient updates, Video2LoRA predicts these weights directly from the video. Trained for SmolVLM2 500M and 2.2B on video summarization and captioning, Video2LoRA enables the same frozen VLM to answer queries from the adapter alone, with zero visual tokens in its context at query time. Video2LoRA is statistically non-inferior and equivalent to direct video-in-context inference across all five captioning benchmarks at both model scales, and across seven of eight video question answering benchmark-scale pairings. Although trained only on 12 frames at 384px, it remains stable up to 1,024 frames and 1024px, where direct video-in-context inference often degenerates. Across this sweep, it reduces answer-time visual-token load by up to 1,500x and query TTFT by 6-80x, while preserving video-faithful outputs. We also find that independently generated adapters for non-overlapping video segments can compose in rank space, suggesting a path toward chunked long-video internalization.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型处理视频的计算成本高昂：每一帧视频占据数百个token，推理成本随每一帧和每一次重复查询而线性增长。我们提出Video2LoRA，一种参数化视频内化方法。一个感知器超网络读取冻结的VLM在逐层编码视频时产生的中间表示，并在单次前向传播中生成低秩适配(LoRA)适配器。与需要迭代梯度更新的标准LoRA微调不同，Video2LoRA直接从视频预测这些权重。在SmolVLM2 500M和2.2B上针对视频摘要和字幕任务训练后，Video2LoRA使同一冻结VLM能够仅凭适配器回答查询，推理时上下文中不含任何视觉token。在两个模型规模的所有五个字幕基准以及八个视频问答基准-规模配对中的七个上，Video2LoRA在统计上非劣效于直接视频上下文推理。尽管仅在12帧384px分辨率下训练，它在高达1024帧和1024px分辨率下仍保持稳定，而直接视频上下文推理在此条件下常常退化。在此扫描范围内，它将推理时的视觉token负载降低多达1500倍，查询首token延迟(TTFT)降低6-80倍，同时保持忠实于视频的输出。我们还发现，独立生成的非重叠视频片段的适配器可以在秩空间组合，这为分块长视频内化开辟了一条道路。

</details>


### [25] [Spatially Grounded Concept Bottleneck Models via Part-Factorized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04364)
*Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种部分分解的概念瓶颈模型（Part-Factorized CBM），通过强制每个概念头仅从对应身体区域读取特征，在保持分类性能的同时显著提升定位精度，并消除了对逐图像关键点标注的依赖。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的概念头可自由关注图像任意位置，导致命名指向某个身体部位的概念头可能被其他区域的证据激活，降低了模型的可解释性和审计可靠性。本文旨在通过结构性约束消除这一自由度。

Method: 方法基于冻结的DINOv3视觉Transformer，包含三个组件：(1) 学习的前景门控，利用DINOv3 patch特征抑制背景区域；(2) 一组部位查询（part queries）交叉关注patch特征，并将312个CUB属性通过固定的概念-部位映射路由到对应部位token；(3) 可学习的二维高斯先验（在log空间中加性注入注意力logits），打破部位查询间的排列对称性，其均值从数据集平均关键点位置初始化，无需逐图像关键点监督。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011上，空间先验模型达到与全监督基线相当的分类精度（88.85% vs 88.95% top-1），同时将指向精度提升16个点（52.6% vs 36.4%）。用PCA前景目标替代边界框监督并结合高斯先验，消除了所有逐图像监督，达到88.6% top-1和约70%指向精度。仅需0.5%训练集（约27张图像）即可初始化先验而无性能损失。无空间先验时指向精度骤降至2.9%。

Conclusion: 通过结构性部分分解和弱监督空间先验，可以有效约束概念瓶颈模型中的部位-概念对应关系，在几乎不损失分类精度的前提下大幅提升可解释性（定位精度），并显著降低对精细标注的依赖。

Original Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) predict a layer of human-named attributes before predicting a class, which makes their decisions auditable. On fine-grained recognition tasks the concept heads are usually free to attend anywhere in the image, so a head named for one body region can be satisfied by evidence on another. This work studies a part-factorized CBM that removes that freedom by construction. The method has three components built on a frozen DINOv3 vision transformer. A learned foreground gate, trained on DINOv3 patch features, suppresses background patches inside the part attention. A set of part queries cross-attends to patch features and each of the 312 CUB attributes is routed, through a fixed concept-to-part map, to read only from the part token its name implies. A learnable two-dimensional Gaussian prior, injected additively in log space into the attention logits, breaks the permutation symmetry among part queries; its means are initialized from the dataset-average keypoint location of each part, which requires no per-image keypoint supervision at training or test time. On CUB-200-2011 the spatial-prior model matches a fully supervised baseline (88.85% versus 88.95% top-1) while raising pointing accuracy by 16 points (52.6% versus 36.4%). Replacing bounding-box supervision with a PCA foreground target and combining it with the Gaussian prior removes all per-image supervision and reaches 88.6% top-1 at about 70% pointing accuracy. A keypoint-fraction sweep shows that 0.5% of the training set (about 27 images) suffices to initialize the prior with no measurable loss. Removing part identity entirely is the harder case: without any spatial prior, pointing accuracy collapses to $2.9\%$.

中文摘要翻译: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在预测类别之前先预测一层人类命名的属性，从而使模型决策可审计。在细粒度识别任务中，概念头通常可以自由关注图像中的任何位置，因此一个以某个身体区域命名的头可能被其他区域的证据激活。本文研究了一种部分分解的CBM，通过构造约束消除了这种自由度。该方法基于冻结的DINOv3视觉Transformer，包含三个组件。一个学习得到的前景门控，在DINOv3的patch特征上训练，抑制部位注意力中的背景patch。一组部位查询交叉关注patch特征，并且312个CUB属性中的每一个都通过固定的概念-部位映射路由，仅从其名称所暗示的部位token读取信息。一个可学习的二维高斯先验，以对数空间加性注入注意力logits的方式，打破了部位查询之间的排列对称性；其均值从数据集平均关键点位置初始化，在训练或测试时无需逐图像的关键点监督。在CUB-200-2011上，空间先验模型与全监督基线性能相当（88.85% vs 88.95% top-1），同时将指向精度提升了16个点（52.6% vs 36.4%）。用PCA前景目标替代边界框监督，并结合高斯先验，消除了所有逐图像监督，达到88.6% top-1和约70%的指向精度。关键点比例的消融实验表明，仅需0.5%的训练集（约27张图像）即可初始化先验而无明显性能损失。完全移除部位身份是更困难的情况：没有任何空间先验时，指向精度骤降至2.9%。

</details>


### [26] [Multi-Granularity 3D Kidney Lesion Characterization from CT Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04365)
*Renjie Liang,Zhengkang Fan,Jinqian Pan,Chenkun Sun,Jiang Bian,Russell Terry,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LesionDETR，将肾脏CT表征重构为每个病灶的集合预测任务，能够为每个肾脏输出可变数量的病灶及其四种临床属性。在2,619例CT上训练，采用尺寸距离匈牙利匹配和层次化损失函数，达到双侧异常AUC约0.80，并实现零样本外推至KiTS23。分割掩码输入和同域腹部预训练是关键设计，而通用大语料预训练无优势。病灶级检测性能受限于罕见病变数据稀缺。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D肾脏CT分析方法仅能在患者或器官级别进行预测，无法提供每个病灶的类型、尺寸、强化特征和衰减等细粒度临床属性。本文旨在将肾脏CT表征重构为每个病灶的集合预测任务，以生成更精细、可直接用于结构化报告的病灶级预测。

Method: 提出LesionDETR，一种DETR风格的架构。核心组件包括：(1) 尺寸距离匈牙利匹配，用于将预测病灶与真实病灶对应；(2) 层次化损失函数，将每个slot的输出聚合到侧级别目标；(3) 使用分割掩码作为输入通道，结合同域腹部预训练（SuPreM）。支持不同输入表示和编码器初始化方案。

Result: 在UF-Health数据集上达到双侧侧级别异常AUC 0.799±0.009，在KiTS23零样本验证上达到0.817±0.072。囊性病变的病灶级mAP为0.190±0.083，但实性病变AP处于噪声水平。关键发现：分割掩码输入和同域腹部预训练（SuPreM）是两大主导设计选择，通用大语料预训练并不优于随机初始化。

Conclusion: LesionDETR成功将肾脏CT表征从器官/患者级推进到病灶级集合预测，但病灶级检测性能瓶颈在于罕见病变（如实性病变）的数据稀缺，而非架构本身。该框架可为下游结构化报告生成提供可验证的病灶级预测。

Original Abstract: Radiology reports describe kidney lesions by type, size, enhancement, and attenuation, yet existing 3D methods predict only at the patient or organ level. We reformulate kidney CT characterization as a per-lesion set-prediction task: one model emits a variable number of lesions per kidney, each with four clinical attributes. We curated 2,619 CT volumes from 788 patients at one academic medical center, with multi-granularity side- and per-lesion labels, and used KiTS23 (489 cases) for zero-shot external validation. We propose \textbf{LesionDETR}, a DETR-style architecture with size-distance Hungarian matching and a hierarchical loss that aggregates per-slot outputs to side-level objectives. Across four input representations and six encoder initializations, two design choices dominate: a segmentation mask as an input channel, and same-domain abdominal pretraining (SuPreM); generic large-corpus pretraining is no better than random initialization. LesionDETR reaches bilateral side-level abnormality AUC $0.799 \pm 0.009$ on UF-Health and $0.817 \pm 0.072$ on KiTS23. A count-conditioned variant reaches per-lesion mAP $0.190 \pm 0.083$ on cystic lesions; rare solid-lesion AP stays at the noise floor, pointing to targeted data collection, not architecture, as the next bottleneck. The framework yields verified per-lesion predictions for downstream structured report generation.

中文摘要翻译: 放射学报告通过类型、尺寸、强化特征和衰减来描述肾脏病灶，然而现有的3D方法仅能在患者或器官级别进行预测。我们将肾脏CT表征重构为每个病灶的集合预测任务：一个模型为每个肾脏输出可变数量的病灶，每个病灶附带四种临床属性。我们从一家学术医疗中心收集了788名患者的2,619例CT容积数据，带有细粒度的侧别和病灶级标注，并使用KiTS23（489例）进行零样本外部验证。我们提出\textbf{LesionDETR}，一种DETR风格的架构，采用尺寸距离匈牙利匹配和层次化损失函数，将每个slot的输出聚合到侧别级目标。在四种输入表示和六种编码器初始化方案中，两个设计选择占据主导地位：将分割掩码作为输入通道，以及同域腹部预训练（SuPreM）；通用大语料预训练并不优于随机初始化。LesionDETR在UF-Health数据集上达到双侧侧别级异常AUC $0.799 \pm 0.009$，在KiTS23上达到$0.817 \pm 0.072$。计数条件变体在囊性病变上达到病灶级mAP $0.190 \pm 0.083$；罕见的实性病变AP停留在噪声水平，表明下一个瓶颈在于目标性数据收集，而非架构。该框架可为下游结构化报告生成提供可验证的病灶级预测。

</details>


### [27] [VT-3DAD: Cross-Category 3D Anomaly Detection via Visual-Text Normal Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04369)
*Zi Wang,Katsuya Hotta,Yawen Zou,Koichiro Kamide,Yijin Wei,Chao Zhang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VT-3DAD提出了一种无需训练的跨类别3D异常检测框架，通过视觉-文本正态空间对齐，利用少样本正常参考点云检测异常，在ShapeNetPart上达到SOTA，将one-shot AUC-ROC从92.49%提升至94.80%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于训练的方法需要逐类别优化，而基于多视图CLIP视觉特征的无训练方法主要依赖视觉相似性，容易受到几何形状相似类别的混淆，导致检测性能受限。

Method: VT-3DAD首先将少样本正常参考和测试点云生成多视图深度图，用冻结的CLIP视觉编码器提取视图特征，计算视觉偏差；同时用深度感知和3D感知提示通过CLIP文本编码器构建文本正态锚点，提供语义正态性约束；最终融合视觉偏差与语义偏差得到异常分数。

Result: 在ShapeNetPart数据集上，VT-3DAD达到SOTA，one-shot平均AUC-ROC从92.49%提升至94.80%，平均标准差从5.64降至3.41。

Conclusion: 通过视觉-文本双模态对齐，VT-3DAD有效提升了跨类别3D异常检测的性能和稳定性，且无需训练即可泛化到新类别。

Original Abstract: Few-shot cross-category 3D anomaly detection aims to determine whether an unknown point cloud belongs to a target normal category using only a few normal references. Existing training-based methods usually require category-wise optimization, while recent training-free methods based on multi-view CLIP visual features mainly rely on visual similarity and may be confused by geometrically similar categories. In this paper, we propose VT-3DAD, a training-free framework for cross-category 3D anomaly detection via Visual-Text Normal Space Alignment. Given few-shot normal references and a test point cloud, VT-3DAD first generates realistic multi-view depth maps and extracts view-wise features using a frozen CLIP visual encoder. The visual branch measures reference-test deviation in the multi-view feature space. In parallel, depth-aware and 3D-aware prompts are encoded by the frozen CLIP text encoder to construct textual normal anchors, which provide semantic normality constraints for the target category. The final anomaly score is obtained by fusing visual deviation from normal references and semantic deviation from the textual normal space. Experiments on the ShapeNetPart dataset demonstrate that VT-3DAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. In particular, VT-3DAD improves the one-shot average AUC-ROC from 92.49% to 94.80% compared with the visual-only baseline, while also reducing the average standard deviation from 5.64 to 3.41.

中文摘要翻译: 少样本跨类别3D异常检测旨在仅使用少量正常参考样本，判断未知点云是否属于目标正常类别。现有的基于训练的方法通常需要逐类别优化，而最近基于多视图CLIP视觉特征的无训练方法主要依赖视觉相似性，可能被几何形状相似的类别混淆。本文提出VT-3DAD，一种通过视觉-文本正态空间对齐的无需训练的跨类别3D异常检测框架。给定少量正常参考样本和测试点云，VT-3DAD首先生成逼真的多视图深度图，并使用冻结的CLIP视觉编码器提取视图特征。视觉分支在多视图特征空间中度量参考-测试偏差。同时，深度感知和3D感知提示由冻结的CLIP文本编码器编码，构建文本正态锚点，为目标类别提供语义正态性约束。最终异常分数由正常参考的视觉偏差与文本正态空间的语义偏差融合得到。在ShapeNetPart数据集上的实验表明，VT-3DAD取得了最先进的性能。特别地，与仅使用视觉的基线相比，VT-3DAD将one-shot平均AUC-ROC从92.49%提升至94.80%，同时将平均标准差从5.64降低至3.41。

</details>


### [28] [Selective Coupling of Decoupled Informative Regions: Masked Attention Alignment for Data-Free Quantization of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04373)
*Biao Qian,Yang Wang,Yong Wu,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MaskAQ，一种针对视觉Transformer的无数据量化方法，通过掩码注意力对齐解决合成样本与量化模型之间的分布不匹配问题，在多个骨干网络和下游任务上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无数据量化方法在ViT上存在合成样本与量化模型Q期望输入分布之间的分布不匹配问题，导致性能次优。

Method: 1) 利用差分熵最大化解耦合成样本中的信息区域与噪声背景；2) 通过掩码注意力对齐目标选择信息区域来对齐全精度模型与量化模型；3) 引入周期性样本刷新策略，使方法持续适应量化模型的演化状态。

Result: 在多个骨干网络和下游任务上，MaskAQ超越了现有的最先进方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: MaskAQ通过关注自注意力机制中的稀疏信息区域并对其进行对齐，有效提升了ViT无数据量化的性能，代码已公开。

Original Abstract: Data-Free Quantization (DFQ) addresses data security concerns by synthesizing samples, without accessing real data. It has garnered increasing attention in the context of Vision Transformers (ViTs), owing to the superiority of the self-attention mechanism compared to classical convolutional operation. However, previous DFQ arts for ViTs often suffer from a distribution mismatch between synthetic samples and input distribution expected by quantized models Q, resulting in the suboptimal performance. In this paper, we propose a novel Masked Attention Alignment approach for Data-Free Quantization of ViTs, named MaskAQ, revealing that: 1) the semantics in the self-attention mechanism is predominantly localized to a sparse subset of patches, called informative regions; 2) the informative regions dominate the mutual information between synthetic samples and Q's outputs. To these ends, we incorporate differential entropy maximum over patch similarity of synthetic samples, to decouple informative regions from noisy background. To couple with varied Q, the informative regions are selected to align full-precision models with Q via a masked attention alignment objective, thus yielding high-quality synthetic samples. Furthermore, a periodic sample refreshing strategy comes up to endow MaskAQ with the capacity to continually adapt to the evolving state of Q throughout the training process, to preserve desirable mutual information with synthetic samples. Extensive experiments verify the merits of MaskAQ over state-of-the-art approaches across multiple backbones and downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/hfutqian/MaskAQ.

中文摘要翻译: 无数据量化（DFQ）通过合成样本来解决数据安全问题，无需访问真实数据。由于自注意力机制相比经典卷积操作具有优越性，DFQ在视觉Transformer（ViT）领域受到了越来越多的关注。然而，现有的ViT无数据量化方法往往存在合成样本与量化模型Q期望输入分布之间的分布不匹配问题，导致性能次优。本文提出了一种新颖的面向ViT无数据量化的掩码注意力对齐方法，命名为MaskAQ，揭示了：1）自注意力机制中的语义信息主要集中于一个稀疏的补丁子集，称为信息区域；2）信息区域主导了合成样本与Q输出之间的互信息。为此，我们在合成样本的补丁相似度上引入差分熵最大化，以将信息区域与噪声背景解耦。为了适配不同的Q，通过掩码注意力对齐目标选择信息区域来对齐全精度模型与Q，从而生成高质量的合成样本。此外，一种周期性样本刷新策略使MaskAQ能够在整个训练过程中持续适应Q的演化状态，以保持与合成样本之间良好的互信息。大量实验验证了MaskAQ在多个骨干网络和下游任务上优于现有最先进方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/hfutqian/MaskAQ获取。

</details>


### [29] [Geometry-Preserving Unsupervised Alignment for Heterogeneous Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04385)
*Shuwen Yu,Zhanxuan Hu,Yi Zhao,Yonghang Tai,Huafeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GPUA框架，通过无监督正交映射对齐视觉基础模型（VFM）与视觉语言基础模型（VLM）的特征空间，在保留几何结构的同时弥合模态差距，无需标签或参数更新，显著提升跨模型兼容性和下游零样本任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM具有语言对齐的语义但视觉较粗糙，VFM能学习判别性感知几何但缺乏语义基础，两者仅部分兼容，亟需一种无需标签、无需参数更新的方法来融合两者互补优势。

Method: GPUA将VFM特征视为一种"视觉语言"，学习一个正交映射矩阵将VFM特征空间转换为VLM语义空间，在转换过程中保持几何结构并缩小模态差距。该方法任务无关，仅需访问预训练模型的特征级别输出，无需标签或更新模型参数。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，GPUA显著提升了跨模型兼容性，并在零样本识别和分割等下游任务上取得了强性能提升，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: GPUA通过无监督正交对齐有效融合了VFM与VLM的互补优势，是一种轻量级、任务无关且无需标签的特征空间对齐方案，为视觉基础模型之间的跨模态兼容提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Foundation models have driven rapid progress in computer vision, yet the two dominant paradigms, vision-language foundation models (VLMs) and vision-only foundation models (VFMs), remain only partially compatible. VLMs offer language-grounded semantic alignment but are often visually coarse, while VFMs learn discriminative perceptual geometry but lack semantic grounding. We propose GPUA (Geometry-Preserving Unsupervised Alignment), a framework that integrates the complementary strengths of VFMs and VLMs. Inspired by cross-lingual alignment, GPUA treats VFM features as a visual language and learns an orthogonal mapping that translates the VFM space into the VLM semantic space, preserving geometry and narrowing the modality gap without labels or model parameter updates. GPUA is task-agnostic and requires only feature-level access to pretrained models. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved cross-model compatibility and strong gains in downstream zero-shot recognition and segmentation with negligible overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/Yuteam14/GPUA

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型推动了计算机视觉的快速发展，然而两种主导范式——视觉-语言基础模型（VLM）和仅视觉基础模型（VFM）——仅能部分兼容。VLM提供了基于语言的语义对齐，但视觉上往往较为粗糙；而VFM学习判别性的感知几何结构，但缺乏语义基础。我们提出GPUA（几何保持无监督对齐）框架，融合了VFM和VLM的互补优势。受跨语言对齐启发，GPUA将VFM特征视为一种视觉语言，学习一个正交映射，将VFM空间转换为VLM语义空间，在保留几何结构的同时缩小模态差距，且无需标签或模型参数更新。GPUA是任务无关的，仅需访问预训练模型的特征级别输出。在多个不同基准上的实验表明，该方法提升了跨模型兼容性，并在下游零样本识别和分割任务中取得了显著性能提升，而计算开销可忽略不计。代码已开源：https://github.com/Yuteam14/GPUA

</details>


### [30] [An Empirical Study of Data Scale, Model Complexity, and Input Modalities in Visual Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04409)
*Luoyidi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文实证研究数据规模、模型复杂度和输入模态对深度神经网络视觉泛化性能的影响，发现数据规模持续提升泛化性能，模型复杂度增益不稳定，颜色信息缺失损害性能，先验特征效果因架构而异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代深度神经网络在计算机视觉中表现优异，但其泛化性能的来源难以用传统统计学习理论解释，因此需要从数据规模、模型复杂度和输入模态等基本可控变量出发，实证分析它们对视觉泛化性能的影响。

Method: 首先通过一维非线性函数构造初步实验，改变训练样本数和多项式次数观察数据规模与模型复杂度的影响；主实验在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，在不同训练数据规模、模型架构和输入模态下比较模型性能。

Result: 增加训练数据规模持续提升泛化性能；模型复杂度变化不能稳定提升性能；去除颜色信息会降低模型性能；梯度、边缘、小波等显式先验特征在不同模型架构下效果不一致。

Conclusion: 本研究实证分析了数据规模、模型复杂度、输入模态与视觉泛化性能之间的关系，为理解深度神经网络泛化机制提供了实验依据。

Original Abstract: Modern deep neural networks usually have large parameter scales and nonlinear hierarchical structures, and they have achieved strong performance in computer vision. However, the source of their generalization performance remains difficult to explain using traditional statistical learning theory. Among the factors that may affect visual generalization, data scale, model complexity, and input modalities are fundamental and controllable variables. This study empirically analyzes how these three factors influence model generalization performance. Specifically, in a preliminary experiment, we construct a one-dimensional nonlinear function and vary the number of training samples and the polynomial degree to observe the effects of data scale and model complexity on model performance. In the main experiments, we compare model performance on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 under different training data scales, model architectures, and input modalities. The experimental results show that increasing the training data scale consistently improves generalization performance, whereas changes in model complexity do not provide stable gains. In addition, removing color information degrades model performance, while explicit prior features such as gradients, edges, and wavelets have inconsistent effects across different model architectures. Overall, this study provides an empirical analysis of the relationships among data scale, model complexity, input modalities, and visual generalization performance. Code and experimental logs are available at: https://github.com/zlyd-CV/DeepLearning-Empirical-Studies.
