中文摘要翻译: 现代深度神经网络通常具有庞大的参数规模和非线性层次结构，在计算机视觉中取得了强大的性能。然而，其泛化性能的来源仍难以用传统统计学习理论来解释。在可能影响视觉泛化的因素中，数据规模、模型复杂度和输入模态是基本且可控的变量。本研究实证分析了这三个因素如何影响模型的泛化性能。具体而言，在初步实验中，我们构造了一维非线性函数，改变训练样本数量和多项式次数，以观察数据规模和模型复杂度对模型性能的影响。在主实验中，我们在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上比较了不同训练数据规模、模型架构和输入模态下的模型性能。实验结果表明，增加训练数据规模持续提升泛化性能，而模型复杂度的变化无法提供稳定的性能增益。此外，去除颜色信息会降低模型性能，而梯度、边缘和小波等显式先验特征在不同模型架构下效果不一致。总体而言，本研究提供了关于数据规模、模型复杂度、输入模态与视觉泛化性能之间关系的实证分析。代码和实验日志已开源：https://github.com/zlyd-cv/DeepLearning-Empirical-Studies。

</details>


### [31] [Ultra-Fast Neural Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04410)
*Jiahao Li,Wenxuan Xie,Zhaoyang Jia,Bin Li,Zongyu Guo,Xiaoyi Zhang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于块（chunk）的编码框架DCVC-UF，将多帧视频编码为单个紧凑潜在表示并并行解码，显著提升编码吞吐量并降低计算复杂度，在率失真-复杂度权衡上达到新的最先进水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经视频编码器（NVCs）虽然压缩比优越，但计算复杂度过高，严重阻碍了实际部署。现有逐帧顺序处理方式限制了编码吞吐量和对长期时间相关性的有效建模。

Method: 提出基于块的编码框架：1）将连续多帧编码为单个紧凑潜在表示并同时解码；2）引入跨帧交互模块实现联合时空建模；3）设计帧特定解码器实现并行重建；4）提出简化熵编码机制，将比特流交互合并为单步，降低解码开销。

Result: DCVC-UF实现了超快编码和解码速度，显著优于以往领先的视频编码器，在率失真-复杂度权衡上树立了新的最先进性能标杆。

Conclusion: DCVC-UF标志着神经视频编码演进中的重要里程碑，通过创新的块编码框架有效解决了NVC的计算复杂度瓶颈，为实际部署铺平了道路。

Original Abstract: While neural video codecs (NVCs) have demonstrated superior compression ratio, their prohibitive computational complexity remains a critical barrier to real-world deployment. This paper introduces a chunk-based coding framework designed to significantly improve the rate-distortion-complexity trade-off. Instead of processing frames sequentially, our approach encodes a chunk of multiple frames into a single compact latent representation and decodes them simultaneously. This is enabled by cross-frame interaction modules for joint spatial-temporal modeling and frame-specific decoders for parallel reconstruction. This paradigm not only dramatically enhances coding throughput but also facilitates more effective modeling of long-term temporal correlations. To further boost speed, we propose a streamlined entropy coding mechanism that consolidates bit-stream interactions into a single step, substantially reducing decoding overhead. Building on these innovations, we present DCVC-UF (Ultra-Fast), a new NVC that sets a new SOTA in performance. Our experiments show that DCVC-UF can achieve ultra-fast encoding and decoding speeds, significantly outperforming previous leading codecs. DCVC-UF serves as a notable landmark in the journey of NVC evolution. The code is at https://github.com/microsoft/DCVC.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管神经视频编码器（NVCs）已展现出优越的压缩比，但其惊人的计算复杂度仍然是实际部署的关键障碍。本文提出一种基于块的编码框架，旨在显著改善率失真-复杂度权衡。不同于逐帧顺序处理，我们的方法将包含多帧的块编码为单个紧凑的潜在表示，并同时解码它们。这一方案通过跨帧交互模块实现联合时空建模，并利用帧特定解码器进行并行重建。该范式不仅大幅提升了编码吞吐量，还促进了更有效的长期时间相关性建模。为进一步提高速度，我们提出了一种简化的熵编码机制，将比特流交互整合为单一步骤，大幅降低解码开销。基于这些创新，我们推出了DCVC-UF（Ultra-Fast），一种在性能上树立新最先进水平的新型神经视频编码器。实验表明，DCVC-UF能够实现超快的编码和解码速度，显著优于以往领先的编码器。DCVC-UF是神经视频编码器演进历程中的一个重要里程碑。代码已开源在https://github.com/microsoft/DCVC。

</details>


### [32] [Motion-Guided Causal Disentanglement for Robust Multi-View Cine Cardiac MRI Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04414)
*Chuankai Xu,Cristiane De Carvalho Singulane,Mohammad Abuannadi,Stephen Chandler,Jeremy Slivnick,Karolina Zareba,Jane Cao,Vidya Nadig,Fabio Fernandes,Seth Uretsky,Diego Perez de Arenaza,Amit Patel,Jianxin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种运动引导的视图-疾病解耦框架MoViD，基于ViT-MAE骨干网络，通过双分支监督对比学习和梯度反转对抗约束将CMR影像中的视图特异性特征与疾病判别性特征解耦，并引入无标注的时序运动特征和焦点重加权机制，在多数据集上 outperforms 标准Transformer基线模型，验证了结构解耦在医学图像分析中的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的CMR分析模型学习统一的潜在嵌入，将视图特定的解剖变化与疾病相关特征纠缠在一起，导致分类器偏向结构属性而非视图不变的病理模式。此问题在低数据场景（尤其是代表性不足的心脏疾病）中尤为严重，有限的样本增加了捷径学习和视图依赖决策边界的风险。

Method: 1）基于ViT-MAE骨干网络，显式将潜在表示分解为视图特定和疾病判别两个分支；2）使用双分支监督对比目标和梯度反转对抗约束，最小化疾病信息泄漏到视图嵌入中；3）引入基于帧间差异图的无标注时序运动特征，定位跳动心脏区域并抑制背景伪影；4）在对比损失中融入焦点重加权机制以缓解类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在私人临床静脉血栓数据集和两个公开基准（M&Ms, M&Ms2）上，MoViD在疾病分类和心脏分割任务中一致优于标准Transformer基线模型，并与大规模预训练基础模型相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 结构解耦策略能有效分离视图特异性特征与疾病判别性特征，提升CMR分析中模型在低数据场景下的泛化能力和鲁棒性，验证了其在医学图像分析中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multi-view cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging provides complementary anatomical information and is widely used for noninvasive disease assessment. Recent transformer-based models have demonstrated strong representation learning capabilities for CMR analysis; however, they typically learn unified latent embeddings that entangle view-specific anatomical variations with disease-related features. Such entanglement biases classifiers toward structural attributes rather than view-invariant pathological patterns. This issue is exacerbated in low-data regimes, particularly for underrepresented cardiac conditions, where limited samples increase the susceptibility to shortcut learning and view-dependent decision boundaries. To address this, we propose a Motion-Guided View--Disease Disentanglement framework MoViD built upon a ViT-MAE backbone. The model explicitly factorizes latent representations into view-specific and disease-discriminative components using dual-branch supervised contrastive objectives and a gradient-reversal adversarial constraint that minimizes disease leakage into the view embedding. Additionally, an annotation-free temporal motion feature, derived from inter-frame difference maps, is introduced to localize the beating heart region and suppress background artifacts. A focal reweighting mechanism is incorporated into the contrastive loss to mitigate class imbalance. We evaluate the framework on a private clinical venous thrombosis dataset and two public benchmarks (M&Ms, M&Ms2). Across disease classification and cardiac segmentation tasks, our approach consistently outperforms standard transformer baselines and demonstrates competitive performance against large-scale pretrained foundation models, validating the efficacy of structural disentanglement in medical image analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 多视图心脏磁共振（CMR）成像提供了互补的解剖信息，被广泛用于无创疾病评估。最近的基于Transformer的模型在CMR分析中展现了强大的表示学习能力；然而，它们通常学习统一的潜在嵌入，将视图特定的解剖变化与疾病相关特征纠缠在一起。这种纠缠使分类器偏向结构属性而非视图不变的病理模式。该问题在低数据场景（尤其是代表性不足的心脏疾病）中更为严重，因为有限的样本增加了捷径学习和视图依赖决策边界的风险。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于ViT-MAE骨干网络构建的运动引导视图-疾病解耦框架MoViD。该模型通过双分支监督对比目标和梯度反转对抗约束，将潜在表示显式分解为视图特定组件和疾病判别组件，后者可最小化疾病信息泄漏到视图嵌入中。此外，引入了一种基于帧间差异图推导出的无标注时序运动特征，用于定位跳动心脏区域并抑制背景伪影。对比损失中融入了焦点重加权机制以缓解类别不平衡。我们在私人临床静脉血栓数据集和两个公开基准（M&Ms, M&Ms2）上评估了该框架。在疾病分类和心脏分割任务中，我们的方法一致优于标准Transformer基线模型，并与大规模预训练基础模型相比展现出具有竞争力的性能，验证了结构解耦在医学图像分析中的有效性。

</details>


### [33] [Implicit Fuzzification via Bounded Noise Injection for Robust Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04427)
*Bisheng Tang,Zhangfeng Ma,Chuchu Zhai,Feng Dong,Yaoqun Wu,Ammar Oad,Yifei Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NoiseUNet，通过在跳跃连接中注入有界扰动来正则化跨尺度特征融合，从而增强模型对边界模糊区域的鲁棒性，并在自建甲状腺超声数据集 ThyR 上验证了其在分割精度和边界保真度上的提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像分割方法（如 U-Net）在采样导致的信息损失和像素级标注的内在不确定性下，容易产生过度自信的预测，无法捕捉过渡区域的模糊性，因此需要一种能处理边界模糊性的分割框架。

Method: 提出 NoiseUNet，在编码器-解码器架构的跳跃连接中注入有界扰动，以正则化跨尺度的特征融合，强制模型对局部特征变化具有鲁棒性，从而促进边界感知表示。理论上，这种扰动产生了隐式模糊化效果，无需显式模糊建模即可得到软性的数据驱动隶属度。

Result: 在自建的甲状腺超声数据集 ThyR 上的实验表明，NoiseUNet 在分割精度和边界保真度上均有一致性提升。

Conclusion: NoiseUNet 通过简单有效的扰动注入机制，有效缓解了边界模糊问题，为处理医学图像中固有的模糊边界提供了一种轻量化的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Image segmentation remains fundamentally limited by boundary ambiguity arising from sampling-induced information loss and inherent uncertainty in pixel-wise labeling. Although encoder-decoder architectures such as U-Net achieve strong performance, they often produce overconfident predictions that fail to capture transition-region ambiguity. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{NoiseUNet}, a simple yet effective framework that injects bounded perturbations into skip connections to regularize cross-scale feature fusion. This mechanism enforces robustness to local feature variations and promotes boundary-aware representations. Theoretically, the perturbation induces an implicit fuzzification effect, yielding soft, data-driven memberships without requiring explicit fuzzy modeling. We further introduce \textbf{ThyR}, a real-world thyroid ultrasound dataset with inherently ambiguous boundaries. Experiments demonstrate that NoiseUNet consistently improves both segmentation accuracy and boundary fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 图像分割仍然从根本上受到边界模糊性的限制，这种模糊性源于采样导致的的信息损失以及像素级标注中的固有不确定性。尽管诸如 U-Net 等编码器-解码器架构取得了优异性能，但它们通常会产生过度自信的预测，无法捕捉过渡区域的模糊性。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了 NoiseUNet——一个简单而有效的框架，它在跳跃连接中注入有界扰动，以正则化跨尺度特征融合。该机制增强了对局部特征变化的鲁棒性，并促进了边界感知的表示。从理论上讲，该扰动会引发隐式模糊化效应，无需显式模糊建模即可生成软性的、数据驱动的隶属度。我们进一步引入了 ThyR，一个真实世界的甲状腺超声数据集，其边界具有固有的模糊性。实验表明，NoiseUNet 在分割精度和边界保真度上均有一致性提升。

</details>


### [34] [DSA: Dynamic Step Allocation for Fast Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04432)
*Thanh-Tung Le,Yunhan Zhao,Menglei Chai,Zhengyang Shen,Zhe Cao,Danhang Tang,Xiaohui Xie,Deying Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DSA框架，通过轻量级置信度头动态调整视频扩散模型的去噪步数，在保持生成质量的同时实现实时自回归视频生成（22.63 FPS）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型推理成本高，虽通过蒸馏降低延迟，但仍使用固定去噪步数，在简单帧上浪费计算，在复杂帧上细化不足。

Method: 提出DSA框架，引入一个轻量级置信度头，与生成器在分布匹配蒸馏目标下联合训练，估计每帧去噪可靠性；推理时根据置信度信号动态调整扩散步数——简单帧提前终止，复杂帧额外细化。

Result: 在H100 GPU上达到22.63 FPS、亚秒级延迟的实时自回归视频生成，VBench质量与近期自回归和双向视频扩散模型相当或更优。

Conclusion: 置信度引导的自适应采样为交互式视频生成提供了一条有效且实用的路径。

Original Abstract: Video diffusion transformers have achieved state-of-the-art visual quality, but their high inference cost remains a major bottleneck for real-time applications. Recent distillation frameworks produce autoregressive video diffusion models with reduced latency, yet these models still use a fixed number of denoising steps per frame, wasting computation on predictable frames and under-refining challenging ones. We present DSA, a confidence-guided adaptive computation framework for AR video diffusion. DSA introduces a lightweight confidence head, trained jointly with the generator under a distribution-matching distillation objective, to estimate per-frame denoising reliability. At inference, this confidence signal dynamically adjusts the number of diffusion steps: simple frames terminate early for speed, while complex frames receive additional refinement. Our method requires no extra video data, no heuristics, and little architectural modification. Experiments show that DSA achieves real-time autoregressive video generation, reaching 22.63 FPS with sub-second latency on H100 GPUs, while maintaining competitive or superior VBench quality compared to recent autoregressive and bidirectional video diffusion models. Our results demonstrate that confidence-guided adaptive sampling provides an effective and practical path toward interactive video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散Transformer在视觉质量上达到了最先进的水平，但其高昂的推理成本仍然是实时应用的主要瓶颈。最近的蒸馏框架生成了具有降低延迟的自回归视频扩散模型，但这些模型仍然对每一帧使用固定数量的去噪步数，在可预测的帧上浪费计算资源，而对具有挑战性的帧细化不足。我们提出了DSA，一种面向自回归视频扩散的置信度引导自适应计算框架。DSA引入了一个轻量级置信度头，与生成器在分布匹配蒸馏目标下联合训练，以估计每帧的去噪可靠性。在推理时，该置信度信号动态调整扩散步数：简单帧提前终止以提高速度，而复杂帧则获得额外的细化处理。我们的方法不需要额外的视频数据、启发式规则以及很少的架构修改。实验表明，DSA实现了实时自回归视频生成，在H100 GPU上达到22.63 FPS且延迟低于一秒，同时在VBench质量上与近期自回归和双向视频扩散模型相当或更优。我们的结果表明，置信度引导的自适应采样为交互式视频生成提供了一条有效且实用的路径。

</details>


### [35] [Stateful Visual Encoders for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04433)
*Zirui Wang,Junwei Yu,Adam Yala,David M. Chan,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有视觉-语言模型(VLM)的视觉编码器是无状态的，每个图像独立编码，无法感知先前视觉上下文，导致微小但关键的变化在语言模型比较之前就被弱化。本文提出状态化视觉编码器(Stateful Visual Encoder)，通过将每个视觉表示建立在先前视觉特征之上，在监督微调下实现了跨图像空间聚合、多目标视觉差分和视觉轨迹行为克隆等任务的持续改进，并在真实任务（纵向放射学、精细图像比较、遥感）中超越通用VLM基线，匹配或超越特定领域模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放权重VLM在多头轮次智能体场景中，视觉编码器是无状态的——每个图像独立编码，无法访问先前视觉上下文。导致任务关键性的微小变化（尤其是不影响高级语义的变化）在语言模型有机会比较之前就被弱化，从而限制了模型在需要视觉比较的任务上的表现。

Method: 提出状态化视觉编码器(Stateful Visual Encoder)，使每个视觉表示都依赖于先前的视觉特征。通过监督微调，让VLM中的视觉编码器具备跨图像的状态记忆能力，从而在编码当前图像时能够感知和利用历史视觉上下文信息。

Result: 在跨图像空间聚合、多目标视觉差分和视觉轨迹行为克隆等受控任务上取得一致改进；这些改进在不同输入分辨率、语言模型大小和VLM骨干网络上保持稳定；在真实任务（纵向放射学、精细图像比较、遥感）上持续改进通用VLM基线，并在特定领域匹配或超越专用模型。

Conclusion: 状态化视觉编码器能够有效提升VLM在需要视觉比较的多图像、多头轮次任务中的性能，且改进具有跨设置的一致性，在真实场景中具有实用价值。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in multi-image, multi-turn agentic settings where decisions depend on visual changes. However, in existing open-weight VLMs, visual comparisons happen only inside the language model, while the visual encoder itself remains stateless: each image is encoded independently, without access to the prior visual context. As a result, small but task-critical changes may be attenuated before the language model has a chance to compare them, especially when those changes do not affect the high-level semantics of the scene. We introduce a Stateful Visual Encoder, which conditions each visual representation on prior visual features. Under supervised finetuning, VLMs equipped with stateful encoders achieve consistent improvements on controlled tasks involving cross-image spatial aggregation, multi-object visual differencing, and visual trajectory behavior cloning. These improvements are consistent across input resolutions, language model sizes, and VLM backbones. Finally, we validate our model on real-world tasks, including longitudinal radiology, fine-grained image comparison, and remote sensing, where stateful encoders consistently improve generalist VLM baselines and can match or surpass specialized models in selected domains. Project page: https://statefulvisualencoders.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型(VLM)越来越多地被用于多图像、多头轮次的智能体场景中，这些场景下的决策依赖于视觉变化。然而，在现有的开放权重VLM中，视觉比较仅在语言模型内部发生，而视觉编码器本身仍然是无状态的：每个图像被独立编码，无法访问先前的视觉上下文。因此，微小但对任务至关重要的变化可能在语言模型有机会比较之前就被弱化，尤其是当这些变化不影响场景的高级语义时。我们提出了一种状态化视觉编码器，它使每个视觉表示都依赖于先前的视觉特征。在监督微调下，配备状态化编码器的VLM在涉及跨图像空间聚合、多目标视觉差分和视觉轨迹行为克隆的受控任务上取得了一致的改进。这些改进在不同输入分辨率、语言模型大小和VLM骨干网络上保持一致。最后，我们在真实世界任务上验证了我们的模型，包括纵向放射学、精细图像比较和遥感，在这些任务中，状态化编码器持续改进了通用VLM基线，并在特定领域中能够匹配或超越专用模型。项目页面：https://statefulvisualencoders.github.io/

</details>


### [36] [Hyper-ICL: Attention Calibration with Hyperbolic Anchor Distillation for Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04434)
*Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Hossein Kashiani,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hyper-ICL提出了一种无需推理时上下文示例的多模态上下文学习方法，通过训练轻量级低秩logit适配器和查询自适应调制机制，在多种多模态基准上提升精度和稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态ICL在实际应用中存在高推理延迟和对演示格式、顺序及内容敏感的稳定性问题，因此需要一种无需推理时上下文示例但仍能重建演示效果的方法。

Method: 提出Hyper-ICL框架：(1) 学习参数高效的低秩logit级适配器，校准注意力分布以匹配演示诱导的注意力重分布；(2) 设计查询自适应调制机制，根据当前查询在token级别自适应控制各层和各头的干预强度；(3) 提出逐层双曲锚点蒸馏损失，通过Lorentz测地距离对齐学生模型与演示条件教师模型的中间特征，重建演示-查询关系。

Result: 在VQAv2、OK-VQA、COCO Caption等六个多模态基准上，Hyper-ICL在精度和稳定性上一致优于传统ICL及现有最优方法。

Conclusion: Hyper-ICL通过训练框架有效消除了推理时对上下文示例的依赖，同时重建了演示效果，实现了更高效、更稳定的多模态上下文学习。

Original Abstract: Multimodal In-Context Learning (ICL) has emerged as a practical inference paradigm for Multimodal Large Language Models, where a small set of interleaved image-text In-Context Demonstrations (ICDs) conditions the model to solve new tasks. Despite its flexibility, multimodal ICL incurs high inference latency and suffers from instability due to sensitivity to demonstration formatting, ordering, and content. To address these limitations, we propose Hyper-ICL, a lightweight, training-based framework for demonstration-free multimodal ICL that reconstructs demonstration effects directly without requiring ICDs at inference time. Hyper-ICL learns a parameter-efficient low-rank logit-level adapter that calibrates attention distributions to better match demonstration-induced attention redistribution. To capture how demonstration influence varies across queries, we introduce a query-adaptive modulation mechanism that adaptively controls intervention strength at token level across layers and heads based on the current query. Finally, we propose a layer-wise hyperbolic anchor distillation loss that aligns intermediate student features to a demonstration-conditioned teacher via Lorentz geodesic distance. This loss encourages the student to reconstruct the demonstration-query relationships induced by ICDs. Extensive experiments across six different multimodal benchmarks (including VQAv2, OK-VQA, and COCO Caption) demonstrate that Hyper-ICL consistently improves accuracy and stability over vanilla ICL and existing state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态上下文学习已成为多模态大语言模型的一种实用推理范式，通过少量交错的图像-文本上下文示例来引导模型解决新任务。尽管其灵活性高，但多模态ICL存在高推理延迟，并且由于对演示的格式、顺序和内容敏感而表现出不稳定性。为解决这些局限，我们提出Hyper-ICL——一个轻量级、基于训练的无演示多模态ICL框架，它直接在推理时无需上下文示例即可重建演示效果。Hyper-ICL学习一个参数高效的低秩logit级适配器，通过校准注意力分布来更好地匹配演示诱导的注意力重分布。为捕捉演示影响随查询变化的方式，我们引入查询自适应调制机制，根据当前查询在token级别自适应控制各层和各头的干预强度。最后，我们提出逐层双曲锚点蒸馏损失，通过Lorentz测地距离将学生模型的中间特征与演示条件教师模型对齐。该损失鼓励学生模型重建由ICD诱导的演示-查询关系。在包括VQAv2、OK-VQA和COCO Caption在内的六个多模态基准上的广泛实验表明，Hyper-ICL在精度和稳定性上一致优于传统ICL及现有最优方法。

</details>


### [37] [3DThinkVLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Latent 3D Priors via 3D-Thinking-Guided Co-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04436)
*Jiaxin Shi,Xidong Zhang,Fucai Zhu,Zhe Li,Siyu Zhu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种3D思维引导的共训练框架，让VLA模型在动作预测时隐式进行3D空间推理。通过解耦3D几何感知和3D空间推理能力，在不同特征层级注入，无需3D传感器、外部模型或显式文本生成，在多个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D空间推理能力，而传统的3D推理方法需要3D传感器、外部模型或链式思维文本生成，增加了部署复杂度和推理开销。作者希望在不改变VLM主干架构、不引入额外部署负担的前提下，赋予VLA模型隐式3D空间推理能力。

Method: 提出三组件共训练框架：(1) 隐式3D几何感知模块，将中间视觉特征与3D基础模型对齐，获取低级几何线索；(2) 在线3D推理蒸馏模块，通过共享推理锚点令牌在3D VLM共训练时编码空间先验，在VLA训练时作为输入令牌传递高阶空间思维；(3) 空间增强动作集成模块，将解耦的几何与推理特征联合注入动作查询令牌。部署时仅保留轻量适配器，丢弃3D基础模型和教师分支。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、SimplerEnv及真实世界操作任务上达到SOTA性能，且仅基于2D图像运行，无需3D传感器、外部模型或显式文本生成，同时防止了预训练VLM的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 通过解耦3D几何感知与空间推理能力并在不同特征层级注入，可以高效地为VLA模型赋予隐式3D空间推理能力，显著提升操作任务性能，且部署轻量、兼容纯2D输入。

Original Abstract: We propose a 3D-thinking-guided co-training framework that enables vision-language-action (VLA) models to perform 3D spatial reasoning implicitly during action prediction. Our core insight is that 3D geometry perception and 3D spatial reasoning are distinct capabilities that can be disentangled and injected at different feature hierarchies. During training, three tightly coupled components work in concert primarily within the latent space: (1) To gain geometric priors, a latent 3D geometry perception module aligns intermediate visual features with a 3D foundation model, acquiring low-level geometric cues without architectural modifications to the VLM backbone. (2) Complementing this, an online 3D reasoning distillation module mitigates the prompt-induced reasoning gap via a shared reasoning anchor token. During 3D VLM co-training, this anchor is emitted as the first output token to robustly encode spatial priors. During VLA training, it serves as an input token inserted between the task and action instructions, transferring high-level spatial thinking from explicit teacher reasoning prompts to student action prompts without chain-of-thought text generation. (3) These disentangled geometric and reasoning features are then united by a spatially augmented action integration, which jointly injects them into the action-query tokens as hierarchical spatial conditions to prevent action shortcuts. At deployment, our method retains only its lightweight adapters to perform implicit 3D reasoning, discarding the 3D foundation model and the teacher branch used for supervision. Consequently, it operates purely on 2D images without 3D sensors, external models, or explicit text generation while preventing catastrophic forgetting of the pretrained VLM, achieving state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO, LIBERO-PLUS, SimplerEnv, and real-world manipulation tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种3D思维引导的共训练框架，使视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型能够在动作预测过程中隐式地进行3D空间推理。我们的核心观点是：3D几何感知和3D空间推理是两种可解耦的能力，可以在不同特征层级上分别注入。在训练过程中，三个紧密耦合的组件主要在潜在空间中协同工作：(1) 为获取几何先验，一个隐式3D几何感知模块将中间视觉特征与3D基础模型对齐，在不修改VLM主干架构的情况下获取低级几何线索。(2) 作为补充，一个在线3D推理蒸馏模块通过共享的推理锚点令牌缓解提示引起的推理差距。在3D VLM共训练期间，该锚点作为第一个输出令牌发出，以稳健地编码空间先验；在VLA训练期间，它作为插入在任务指令和动作指令之间的输入令牌，将高阶空间思维从显式的教师推理提示转移到学生的动作提示中，无需链式思维文本生成。(3) 这些解耦后的几何和推理特征随后通过空间增强的动作集成模块联合起来，作为层次化空间条件共同注入动作查询令牌，以防止动作捷径。部署时，我们的方法仅保留轻量适配器以执行隐式3D推理，丢弃了用于监督的3D基础模型和教师分支。因此，它仅基于2D图像运行，无需3D传感器、外部模型或显式文本生成，同时防止了预训练VLM的灾难性遗忘，在LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、SimplerEnv以及真实世界操作任务上均达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [38] [INTACT: Ego-Guided Typed Sparse Evidence Retrieval for Heterogeneous Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04437)
*Chen Li,Shengrong Yuan,Jialong Zuo,Xinzhong Zhu,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出INTACT，一种基于自我车辆引导的稀疏证据检索框架，通过让自我车辆发出类型化证据查询、协作车辆仅返回局部响应，实现了异构协作感知中的零训练插入协议，避免了传统翻译范式中的特征兼容性耦合问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有异构协作感知方法采用"先翻译后融合"范式，需要将协作方特征对齐到自我车辆特征空间，导致部署与特定协作方适配器耦合，新异构节点的集成成本高昂，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: INTACT采用自我车辆引导的类型化稀疏证据检索框架：自我车辆发出类型化证据查询（表达疑似目标区域和证据不足区域），协作方仅在被查询位置返回局部证据，通过稀疏逐查询路由筛选有用响应，再通过门控残差回写注入到自我车辆特征中。将兼容性要求从全局特征图可解释性降低为局部类型化响应的可比性，支持零训练异构插入协议。

Result: 在OPV2V-H基准上，INTACT达到80.1 AP70，仅增加0.52M参数，通信量18.0 log₂（约为密集特征传输的16倍压缩）。在DAIR-V2X真实世界挑战条件下达到43.8 AP50。

Conclusion: INTACT通过改变兼容性要求范式，有效解耦了异构协作感知中部署与协作方适配的耦合关系，实现了可扩展、低通信开销的零训练异构插入，在模拟和真实基准上均验证了有效性和可部署性。

Original Abstract: Collaborative perception extends the perceptual range of autonomous vehicles by sharing information across agents, but heterogeneous sensors and perception models make intermediate feature fusion difficult to deploy at scale. Existing heterogeneous collaboration methods typically follow a translation-first paradigm: collaborator features must be aligned, adapted, or projected into an ego-compatible space before fusion. Such feature-compatibility contracts improve fixed-system performance, but they couple deployment to collaborator-specific adaptation and make newly joined heterogeneous agents costly to integrate. To address this gap, we propose INTACT, an ego-guided typed sparse evidence retrieval framework for heterogeneous collaborative perception. Instead of translating an entire collaborator feature map, INTACT lets the ego vehicle issue typed evidence queries that express suspected objects and evidence-deficient regions. Collaborators respond only with local evidence at queried locations, and the ego selects useful responses through sparse per-query routing and injects them through gated residual write-back. This changes the compatibility requirement from global feature-map interpretability to local, typed response comparability under ego-issued queries, enabling a zero-training heterogeneous insertion protocol in which the ego interface is trained once and new collaborators join through checkpoint merging. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world heterogeneous collaborative perception benchmarks validate the effectiveness and deployability of INTACT. On OPV2V-H, INTACT achieves 80.1 AP70 with only 0.52M additional parameters and 18.0 $\log_2$ communication volume, corresponding to about 16$\times$ compression over dense feature transmission. On DAIR-V2X, INTACT achieves 43.8 AP50 under challenging real-world conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 协同感知通过跨智能体共享信息来扩展自动驾驶车辆的感知范围，但异构传感器和感知模型使得中间特征融合难以大规模部署。现有的异构协作方法通常遵循先翻译后融合的范式：协作方特征必须在融合前对齐、适配或投影到自我车辆兼容的空间中。这种特征兼容性契约提升了固定系统的性能，但将部署与特定协作方的适配耦合在一起，使得新加入的异构智能体集成成本高昂。为解决这一问题，我们提出INTACT——一种用于异构协同感知的自我车辆引导的类型化稀疏证据检索框架。INTACT不是翻译整个协作方特征图，而是让自我车辆发出类型化证据查询，表达疑似目标和证据不足的区域。协作方仅在被查询位置返回局部证据，自我车辆通过稀疏逐查询路由选择有用响应，并通过门控残差回写将其注入。这将兼容性要求从全局特征图可解释性转变为自我车辆查询下的局部类型化响应可比性，从而实现了一种零训练异构插入协议，其中自我车辆接口仅需训练一次，新协作方通过检查点合并加入。在模拟和真实世界的异构协同感知基准上进行的大量实验验证了INTACT的有效性和可部署性。在OPV2V-H上，INTACT以仅0.52M的额外参数和18.0 log₂的通信量（相当于密集特征传输约16倍压缩）达到80.1 AP70。在DAIR-V2X上，INTACT在具有挑战性的真实条件下达到43.8 AP50。

</details>


### [39] [Radiomic Feature Selection Using Gradient Loss of Deep Neural Network for Lung Cancer Stage Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04453)
*Hina Shakir,Mohammad Mohatram,Javeed Hussain,Syed Rizwan Ali,Muhammad Irfan Memon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于梯度敏感性分析的递归特征消除框架（GL-RFE），用于肺癌CT影像组学特征选择和分期检测，在小型高维数据集上取得了90%以上的分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 影像组学数据集通常具有高维度、小样本量的特点，特征选择对构建可靠预测模型至关重要，现有方法难以有效捕捉非线性特征交互。

Method: 提出梯度损失递归特征消除（GL-RFE）框架：利用深度神经网络对输入特征的梯度敏感性分析评估特征重要性，递归剔除贡献最小的特征，最终选取Top-15影像组学特征用于肺癌早期与晚期分类。

Result: 在测试集上达到准确率90.22%、精确率90.10%、召回率90.24%、F1-score 90.16%；可视化分析（相关性热图、分布图）证实了特征冗余减少和类别可分性提升。

Conclusion: GL-RFE能有效捕获非线性特征交互、增强模型泛化能力，为基于影像组学的癌症分期检测提供了可重复、可解释的方法论，适用于高维小样本生物医学数据集，可拓展至基因组学等多模态临床分析领域。

Original Abstract: Radiomics enables extraction of quantitative imaging biomarkers from medical images and has become an important tool for computer-aided cancer diagnosis. However, radiomics datasets are typically high-dimensional with limited samples, making feature selection a critical step for building reliable predictive models. This study proposes a Gradient-Loss Recursive Feature Elimination (GL-RFE) framework that integrates gradient sensitivity analysis from a deep neural network to identify the most influential radiomic features for lung cancer stage detection. A total of 106 radiomic features were extracted from chest Computed Tomography (CT) scans using the PyRadiomics extension of the 3D Slicer platform. The proposed method evaluates feature importance by computing gradients of the network loss with respect to input features and recursively eliminates features with minimal contribution. The resulting top-15 radiomic features are used to train a deep neural network classifier for distinguishing early-stage and advanced-stage lung cancer. The proposed framework achieves strong classification performance, with accuracy of 90.22%, precision of 90.10%, recall of 90.24%, and F1-score of 90.16% on the test dataset. Visualization analyses, including correlation heat maps and distribution plots, further confirm reduced feature redundancy and improved class separability. Compared to conventional feature selection techniques, GL-RFE effectively captures nonlinear feature interactions and enhances model generalization. The presented protocol provides a reproducible and interpretable methodology for radiomics-based cancer stage detection and is particularly suitable for high-dimensional, small-sample biomedical datasets, with potential applications in other domains such as genomics and multimodal clinical analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 影像组学能够从医学图像中提取定量影像生物标志物，已成为计算机辅助癌症诊断的重要工具。然而，影像组学数据集通常具有高维度、小样本量的特点，使得特征选择成为构建可靠预测模型的关键步骤。本研究提出了一种梯度损失递归特征消除（GL-RFE）框架，该框架集成深度神经网络的梯度敏感性分析，以识别对肺癌分期检测最具影响力的影像组学特征。利用3D Slicer平台的PyRadiomics扩展，从胸部计算机断层扫描（CT）影像中提取了106个影像组学特征。所提出的方法通过计算网络损失相对于输入特征的梯度来评估特征重要性，并递归剔除贡献最小的特征。最终选取的Top-15影像组学特征用于训练深度神经网络分类器，以区分早期与晚期肺癌。所提出的框架在测试集上取得了优异的分类性能：准确率90.22%、精确率90.10%、召回率90.24%、F1分数90.16%。包括相关性热图和分布图在内的可视化分析进一步证实了特征冗余的减少和类别可分性的提升。与传统特征选择技术相比，GL-RFE能有效捕获非线性特征交互并增强模型泛化能力。本文提出的方案为基于影像组学的癌症分期检测提供了一种可重复且可解释的方法论，特别适用于高维度、小样本量的生物医学数据集，并具有在基因组学和多模态临床分析等其他领域的潜在应用价值。

</details>


### [40] [Imagine Before You Draw: Visual Prompt Engineering for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04457)
*Liyu Jia,Fengda Zhang,Jiachun Pan,Kesen Zhao,Saining Zhang,Wang Lin,Weijia Wu,Yue Liao,Aojun Zhou,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉提示工程（VPE），将视觉语义Token作为中间步骤无缝集成到内部跨模态框架中，先自回归生成语义布局再生成图像，在类别条件生成、文生图、图像编辑任务上加速收敛、提升质量上限，尤其在编辑保真度上远超外部管线方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法存在两类问题：外部管线（如X-Omni）中扩散解码器无法联合访问原始输入和语义计划，造成信息瓶颈，限制下游编辑任务的细节保留；内部架构（如Transfusion）虽避免了信息瓶颈，但缺少中间语义引导，仍面临文本到像素的建模鸿沟。VPE旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Visual Prompt Engineering（VPE），将其无缝集成到内部框架中。模型先自回归生成视觉语义Token（如SigLIP 2）作为"视觉提示"以捕捉语义布局，然后基于该计划生成完整图像Token。该方法兼容多种Token类型和模型架构。

Result: 在类别条件生成、文生图和图像编辑任务上验证了有效性。VPE能加速收敛、提升质量上限；在图像编辑任务上，通过内部集成实现远优于外部管线方案的编辑保真度（PSNR：26.76 vs. 19.92），同时保持有竞争力的编辑响应能力。

Conclusion: VPE通过将视觉语义Token作为中间步骤无缝融入内部模型，有效解决了信息瓶颈与建模鸿沟问题，在多个生成任务上取得性能提升，尤其在编辑保真度方面表现出显著优势。

Original Abstract: Incorporating visual semantic representations as an intermediate step before image generation can reduce the modeling difficulty between text and images, thereby improving generation quality. Recent works such as X-Omni and BLIP3o-Next have explored this direction, but they typically use a two-stage external pipeline: a separate autoregressive model first generates semantic tokens, which are then fed as conditioning to an independent diffusion decoder. Since the decoder cannot jointly access the original input and the semantic plan, this design introduces an information bottleneck that limits detail preservation in downstream tasks such as editing. Internal architectures such as Transfusion, BAGEL, and Show-o2 avoid this bottleneck by enabling cross-modal interaction within a single model, but they still face the difficult text-to-pixel modeling gap without intermediate semantic guidance. We propose Visual Prompt Engineering (VPE), which can be seamlessly integrated into such internal frameworks. Specifically, the model first autoregressively generates visual semantic tokens (e.g., SigLIP 2) as "visual prompts" that capture the semantic layout, then generates the full image tokens conditioned on this plan. We validate VPE across class-conditional generation, text-to-image generation, and image editing, covering various token types and model architectures. Results show that VPE can accelerate convergence, raise quality ceilings, and through internal integration, achieve substantially better editing preservation (PSNR: 26.76 vs. 19.92) than external alternatives of the same parameter scale, while maintaining competitive editing responsiveness.

中文摘要翻译: 在图像生成之前将视觉语义表示作为中间步骤，可以降低文本与图像之间的建模难度，从而提升生成质量。近期工作如X-Omni和BLIP3o-Next探索了这一方向，但它们通常采用两阶段外部管线：一个独立的自回归模型首先生成语义Token，然后将其作为条件输入给一个独立的扩散解码器。由于解码器无法联合访问原始输入和语义计划，这种设计引入了信息瓶颈，限制了在编辑等下游任务中的细节保留。Transfusion、BAGEL和Show-o2等内部架构通过在单一模型内实现跨模态交互避免了这一瓶颈，但它们在没有中间语义引导的情况下，仍然面临困难的文本到像素建模鸿沟。我们提出视觉提示工程（VPE），可无缝集成到此类内部框架中。具体而言，模型首先自回归地生成视觉语义Token（例如SigLIP 2）作为捕捉语义布局的"视觉提示"，然后基于该计划生成完整的图像Token。我们在类别条件生成、文生图和图像编辑任务上验证了VPE，涵盖了多种Token类型和模型架构。结果表明，VPE能够加速收敛、提升质量上限，并且通过内部集成，在相同参数规模下，其编辑保真度（PSNR：26.76 vs. 19.92）显著优于外部替代方案，同时保持有竞争力的编辑响应能力。

</details>


### [41] [ChannelTok: Efficient Flexible-Length Vision Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04461)
*Sukriti Paul,Arpit Bansal,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于通道维度的灵活长度视觉分词器（ChannelTok），使用轻量级CNN-Transformer混合骨干网络和随机尾部丢弃训练范式，实现高性能、低参数量、快速解码的视觉表示，在ImageNet上以159M参数达到rFID 2.92，解码速度比最优替代方案快8.6倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的灵活视觉分词器虽然质量达到SOTA，但依赖参数庞大的骨干网络和缓慢的多步生成解码器，成本极高。因此需要一种更简单、轻量、快速的替代方案。

Method: 将每个潜变量通道视为一个视觉token，采用参数高效的CNN-Transformer混合骨干网络；在训练中引入随机尾部丢弃范式，迫使通道按语义重要性自发排序，推理时仅保留前k个通道即可实现灵活压缩和变长自回归图像生成。

Result: 在ImageNet上rFID达到2.92（SOTA感知质量），解码速度提升8.6倍，参数量仅159M（缩小2.1倍），在不同token预算下均保持一致的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通道维度分词化是一种高效且实用的视觉表示新范式，在质量-效率权衡上建立了新的边界。

Original Abstract: Leading flexible vision tokenizers achieve SOTA quality at an extreme cost, relying on parameter-heavy backbones and slow, multi-step generative decoders. We depart from this complex, spatial-token paradigm and introduce a simple, lightweight, and fast channel-wise flexible-length tokenizer. Our method treats each latent channel as a visual token, enabling a parameter-efficient CNN-Transformer hybrid backbone. Furthermore, employing a stochastic tail-dropping paradigm during training naturally forces channels to organize by semantic importance. This allows for flexible compression at inference by simply retaining the first $k$ channels, and naturally enables variable-length autoregressive image generation. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on ImageNet, demonstrating consistent quality across diverse token budgets. The results establish a new quality-efficiency frontier: our model achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality (rFID 2.92) while being $8.6\times$ faster in decoding and $2.1\times$ smaller (159M params) than the next-best alternative. Our work establishes channel-wise tokenization as a powerful and practical paradigm for efficient visual representation. Project page: https://channeltok.github.io

中文摘要翻译: 现有的灵活视觉分词器以极高的成本实现了SOTA质量，依赖参数庞大的骨干网络和缓慢的多步生成解码器。本文摆脱了这种复杂的空间token范式，引入了一种简单、轻量且快速的通道维灵活长度分词器。该方法将每个潜变量通道视为一个视觉token，采用参数高效的CNN-Transformer混合骨干网络。此外，训练中采用的随机尾部丢弃范式自然迫使通道按语义重要性进行组织。推理时仅需保留前k个通道即可实现灵活压缩，并自然支持变长自回归图像生成。通过ImageNet上的大量实验验证了该方法在不同token预算下的一致质量。实验结果建立了新的质量-效率边界：该模型在解码速度快8.6倍、参数量小2.1倍（1.59亿）的情况下，达到了SOTA感知质量（rFID 2.92）。这项工作确立了通道维度分词化作为高效视觉表示的一种强大且实用的新范式。项目页面：https://channeltok.github.io

</details>


### [42] [Adaptive Calibration for Fair and Performant Facial Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04469)
*Ryan Brown,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应校准（AC）方法，通过引入局部上下文对余弦相似度进行校准，将人脸识别的相似度映射为校准良好的概率，同时提升准确性和公平性，无需人口统计元数据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有余弦相似度存在根本性不匹配问题：相同的余弦距离在不同嵌入区域可能对应不同的匹配概率，导致校准不准确且不公平。

Method: 提出自适应校准（Adaptive Calibration），在归一化嵌入的余弦相似度中引入局部上下文信息，进行连续、区域特定的校准，将相似度映射为校准概率，无需人口统计分组标签。

Result: 在多种预训练模型和标准基准上，AC方法在准确性和公平性指标上均持续优于现有方法，避免了以牺牲某些群体性能为代价换取公平性的"降级"问题。

Conclusion: AC为公平的人脸识别提供了一种实用方案，无需人口统计组注释即可同时提升整体性能和校准公平性。

Original Abstract: We introduce Adaptive Calibration (AC), a novel calibration strategy for facial recognition that maps cosine similarity between normalized embeddings to well-calibrated probabilities. By incorporating local context into calibration, Adaptive Calibration corrects for a fundamental mismatch in cosine similarity, whereby the same distance can correspond to different match probabilities in different embedding regions. Our approach improves both overall performance and results in a fairer calibration without requiring demographic metadata.
  Our approach consistently dominates existing methods both on accuracy and fairness metrics across a variety of pretrained models and standard benchmarks. AC provides a practical solution for equitable facial recognition, without requiring demographic group annotations, and while improving overall performance. Unlike existing approaches, our method provides continuous, region-specific calibration that avoids "leveling down" where fairness comes at the cost of degraded performance for some groups.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了自适应校准（Adaptive Calibration, AC），一种面向人脸识别的全新校准策略，可将归一化嵌入之间的余弦相似度映射为校准良好的概率。通过将局部上下文引入校准过程，自适应校正确保了余弦相似度中的一个根本性不匹配问题——即相同的距离在不同嵌入区域可能对应不同的匹配概率。我们的方法在不依赖人口统计元数据的前提下，既提升了整体性能，又实现了更公平的校准。在多种预训练模型和标准基准测试上，我们的方法在准确性和公平性指标上均持续优于现有方法。AC为公平的人脸识别提供了一种实用解决方案，无需人口统计组注释，同时提升了整体性能。与现有方法不同，我们的方法提供了连续、区域特定的校准，避免了以牺牲某些群体性能为代价换取公平性的"降级"问题。

</details>


### [43] [Evaluating Reasoning Fidelity in Visual Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04479)
*Jiajun Hong,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了当前文本到图像（T2I）模型在生成包含复杂推理过程的视觉文本时的推理保真度，发现尽管渲染文本视觉清晰，但模型常出现语义错误、逻辑不一致和中间步骤错误，与纯文本模型的推理能力存在显著差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有T2I模型能够生成视觉清晰的文本图像，但其在需要表达完整推理过程时是否真正具备推理能力尚不清楚，模型可能只是模仿表面模式而非进行忠实推理。

Method: 通过设计包含长文本渲染、事实知识探测、上下文理解和多步推理等多个维度的评估框架，系统性地比较T2I模型与纯文本模型在同一任务上的推理表现。

Result: 当前T2I模型在视觉文本生成中频繁产生语义错误、逻辑不一致和错误的中间步骤，即使渲染的文本视觉清晰，其推理性能也远逊于纯文本模型。

Conclusion: 视觉文本生成与程序化推理之间存在显著差距，需要开发更可靠的视觉文本推理方法。

Original Abstract: Recent text-to-image (T2I) models can render highly legible and well-structured text within images, enabling applications including document generation and slide generation. However, it remains unclear whether such systems faithfully preserve reasoning ability when complex solutions must be expressed directly through rendered text, or whether they merely imitate surface-level patterns. We investigate this question by evaluating reasoning fidelity in visual text generation, where models must express complete reasoning processes as images. Our evaluation includes long text rendering, factual knowledge probing, context understanding, and multi-step reasoning. Across these settings, we find that current T2I models frequently produce semantic errors, logical inconsistencies, and incorrect intermediate steps, even when the rendered text appears visually clear. These failures contrast with the strong reasoning performance of text-only models on the same tasks. Our findings reveal a substantial gap between visual text generation and procedural reasoning, motivating more reliable visual text reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的文本到图像（T2I）模型能够在图像中渲染出高度清晰且结构良好的文本，从而支持文档生成和幻灯片生成等应用。然而，当复杂的解决方案必须通过渲染的文本直接表达时，这类系统是否忠实地保留了推理能力，抑或仅仅是在模仿表面模式，目前仍不清楚。我们通过评估视觉文本生成中的推理保真度来探究这一问题，其中模型必须将完整的推理过程表达为图像。我们的评估涵盖长文本渲染、事实知识探测、上下文理解和多步推理。在这些设定下，我们发现当前的T2I模型即使渲染出的文本在视觉上清晰可读，也经常产生语义错误、逻辑不一致以及错误的中间步骤。这些失败与纯文本模型在同一任务上表现出的强大推理能力形成鲜明对比。我们的发现揭示了视觉文本生成与程序化推理之间的显著差距，激励着更可靠的视觉文本推理方法的研究。

</details>


### [44] [IMPose: Interactive Multi-person Pose Estimation with Dynamic Correction Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04480)
*Haoyang Ge,Jian Ma,Ziwen Wang,Qihe Wang,Jianqi Fan,Hongzhi Yu,Xingyu Chen,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMPose是一个面向多人物动态姿态标注的交互工具，通过双层级跟踪机制（关键点级和实例级）将单帧人工修正传播至整个视频，显著减少人工重复标注，在极低点击量下实现高精度姿态标注。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态人体姿态标注工具要么缺乏时序矫正传播能力，要么无法处理多人场景，需要大量人工干预，标注效率低下。

Method: 提出IMPose交互工具，包含：1) 关键点级跟踪：通过时序建模实现单帧矫正的时序传播；2) 实例级跟踪：利用关键点感知嵌入和相对位置编码保持多人跨帧一致性；3) 轨迹银行：存储历史姿态和实例线索，增强长程时序关联，在遮挡和运动模糊等困难情况下稳定标注。

Result: 在3DPW上，对1050帧视频仅需27次点击；在PoseTrack21上，每84帧的tracklet仅需3次点击。扩展PoseTrack21至18.8万姿态实例（355万关键点），仅需10名标注员10小时。

Conclusion: IMPose通过将稀疏人工矫正转化为密集连贯的姿态轨迹，大幅减少了跨帧的重复人工精修，在低交互预算下实现了精度与效率的最优权衡。

Original Abstract: High-quality dynamic human pose annotation equips AI with precise motion kinematics to enable human behavior mastery, yet remains labor-intensive and time-consuming. Current annotation tools either lack temporal correction propagation or fail in multi-person scenarios, necessitating excessive manual intervention. In this paper, we introduce IMPose, an interactive tool for multi-person dynamic pose annotation. It features a dual-level tracking mechanism that propagates one-frame multi-person pose corrections from annotators across entire videos. The keypoint-level ensures corrections temporal propagation via sequential modeling, while the instance-level employs keypoint-aware embedding with relative positional encoding to maintain multi-person cross-frame consistency. To further improve robustness, IMPose maintains historical pose and instance cues in a trajectory bank, which enhances long-range temporal association and stabilizes annotation in challenging cases such as occlusion and motion blur. By converting sparse human corrections into dense and coherent pose trajectories, our framework significantly reduces repeated manual refinement across frames. Extensive experiments show that IMPose consistently achieves a strong accuracy efficiency trade off under different interaction budgets, demonstrating particular advantages in low click annotation settings. IMPose achieves high precision annotation with high efficiency, requiring only 27 clicks per 1,050 frame video on 3DPW and 3 clicks per tracklet per 84-frame on PoseTrack21. We further expand PoseTrack21 with 188K pose instances (3.55M keypoints) at a minimal cost of 10 annotators in 10 hours. The annotation tool, codes, and extended dataset will be open-sourced.

中文摘要翻译: 高质量动态人体姿态标注为人工智能提供了精确的运动运动学信息，使其能够掌握人类行为，然而这一过程仍然劳动密集且耗时。当前的标注工具要么缺乏时序矫正传播能力，要么无法处理多人场景，导致需要过多的人工干预。在本文中，我们介绍IMPose，一个面向多人动态姿态标注的交互工具。它采用双层级跟踪机制，能够将标注人员对单帧多人姿态的矫正传播到整个视频。关键点级通过时序建模实现矫正的时序传播，而实例级则采用关键点感知嵌入与相对位置编码来保持多人跨帧一致性。为了进一步提升鲁棒性，IMPose在轨迹银行中维护历史姿态和实例线索，增强了长程时序关联，并在遮挡和运动模糊等困难情况下稳定标注。通过将稀疏的人工矫正转化为密集且连贯的姿态轨迹，我们的框架显著减少了跨帧的重复人工精修。大量实验表明，IMPose在不同交互预算下 consistently 实现了强大的精度-效率权衡，在低点击标注设置中展现出特别优势。IMPose实现了高精度、高效率的标注，在3DPW数据集上对1050帧视频仅需27次点击，在PoseTrack21上每84帧的tracklet仅需3次点击。我们还以10名标注员10小时的最低成本将PoseTrack21扩展了18.8万个姿态实例（355万个关键点）。该标注工具、代码以及扩展数据集将开源。

</details>


### [45] [SFMambaNet: Spectral-Frequency Enhanced Selective State Space Model for Correspondence Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04493)
*Zhihua Wang,Yanping Li,Yizhang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMambaNet，首次将频域感知融入两视图对应点筛选任务，通过局部谱几何注意力块和谱集成全局Mamba块协同工作，增强内点与外点的可区分性，在多项挑战性任务上超越现有最先进方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于GNN的方法依赖粗粒度欧氏坐标映射的几何特征，难以捕捉内点间的细微几何一致性；而基于Mamba的方法虽具备全局感受野和长序列建模能力，但在隐状态空间中容易累积不一致特征，导致难以区分内点与外点。

Method: 提出SFMambaNet，由两个组件构成：(1) 局部谱几何注意力块(LSGA)，将谱位置编码融入局部图交互，并引入多尺度Mamba处理以增强对细微几何一致性的捕捉；(2) 谱集成全局Mamba块(SIGM)，在状态空间中嵌入频率门控机制，利用LSGA提供的频率信息显式抑制隐状态中的高频噪声累积，减轻不一致特征传播，提升内外点可分离性。

Result: 大量实验表明，SFMambaNet在多项挑战性任务上均优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 首次将频域感知引入对应点筛选任务，通过局部与全局的频谱-频率协同增强，以近线性复杂度实现了鲁棒的全局上下文建模和内外点区分能力。

Original Abstract: Correspondence pruning aims to identify inliers from an initial set of correspondences. Most existing Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods rely on geometric features mapped from coarse Euclidean coordinates, which struggle to capture the subtle geometric consistencies presented by inliers. While Mamba-based methods possess global receptive fields and long sequence modeling capabilities, they tend to accumulate substantial inconsistent features within the hidden state space, making it difficult to distinguish inliers from outliers. In this paper, we integrate frequency domain perception into this task for the first time and propose SFMambaNet, a novel Spectral-Frequency enhanced Mamba-based two-view correspondence pruning network. Our method is collaboratively composed of two components: First, we design a Local Spectral-Geometric Attention (LSGA) block. LSGA incorporates spectral positional encoding into local graph interactions and introduces multi-scale Mamba processing to enhance the capture of subtle geometric consistencies and improve local feature discriminability. Building upon this, we design a Spectral-Integrated Global Mamba (SIGM) block. SIGM embeds a frequency gating mechanism within the state space, utilizing the frequency information provided by LSGA to explicitly suppress high-frequency noise accumulation within hidden states and mitigate the propagation of inconsistent features. This enhances inlier-outlier separability and achieves robust global context modeling capabilities with nearly linear complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFMambaNet outperforms current state-of-the-art methods on several challenging tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Kirito14IT/SFMambaNet.

中文摘要翻译: 对应点筛选旨在从初始匹配集中识别内点。现有大多数基于图神经网络（GNN）的方法依赖于从粗粒度欧氏坐标映射得到的几何特征，难以捕捉内点呈现的细微几何一致性。虽然基于Mamba的方法具备全局感受野和长序列建模能力，但它们倾向于在隐状态空间中累积大量不一致特征，使得区分内点与外点变得困难。本文首次将频域感知融入该任务，提出SFMambaNet，一种新颖的频谱-频率增强型Mamba两视图对应点筛选网络。我们的方法由两个组件协同构成：首先，设计了局部谱几何注意力（LSGA）模块。LSGA将谱位置编码融入局部图交互，并引入多尺度Mamba处理以增强对细微几何一致性的捕捉，提升局部特征判别力。在此基础上，设计了谱集成全局Mamba（SIGM）模块。SIGM在状态空间中嵌入频率门控机制，利用LSGA提供的频率信息显式抑制隐状态中的高频噪声累积，减轻不一致特征的传播，从而增强内点-外点可分离性，并以近线性复杂度实现鲁棒的全局上下文建模能力。大量实验表明，SFMambaNet在多项挑战性任务上均优于当前最先进方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/Kirito14IT/SFMambaNet。

</details>


### [46] [Optical-Guided Neural Collapse for SAR Few-Shot Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04528)
*Fan Zhang,Sijin Zheng,Fei Ma,Qiang Yin,Yongsheng Zhou,Fei Gao,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对SAR图像小样本类增量学习（FSCIL）中数据稀缺、方位角敏感导致的类内差异大、类间混淆及灾难性遗忘问题，提出一种光学引导的SAR FSCIL框架。该框架利用光学ATR数据集导出正交特征子空间作为几何先验，通过主角度约束将SAR特征投影到这些子空间，并结合投影损失与基于冻结simplex-ETF几何的分类器损失，诱导神经坍缩（neural collapse），从而提升类内紧致性与类间可分性。实验结果表明，该方法在最终准确率和性能退化权衡上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAR图像FSCIL面临两大挑战：一是SAR本身方位角敏感性强，导致类内差异大、类间混淆严重；二是增量学习顺序更新导致对已学类别的灾难性遗忘。现有FSCIL方法难以同时解决SAR数据稀缺和特征判别性不足的问题，因此需要引入外部知识（光学ATR数据）来引导SAR特征学习。

Method: 提出光学引导的SAR FSCIL框架：首先从数据丰富的光学ATR数据集中学习正交特征子空间；然后将SAR特征通过主角度约束投影到这些正交子空间上，实现判别性结构从光学域到SAR域的迁移；最后结合投影损失和基于冻结simplex-ETF几何的分类器损失，共同诱导神经坍缩，使特征围绕类均值聚集并保持大类间角度。

Result: 在包含光学ATR数据集和24类SAR ATR数据集的基准上（1个基础训练会话+7个增量会话），该方法相比NCFSCIL等最新FSCIL方法取得了最高的最终准确率，并在最终性能与性能退化之间达到了更优的权衡。神经坍缩指标显示类内紧致性和类间可分性均有提升，学习到的特征更接近理想的simplex-ETF几何结构。

Conclusion: 光学引导的SAR FSCIL框架通过引入光学域的正交子空间先验，有效缓解了SAR数据稀缺和方位角敏感带来的问题，在抑制灾难性遗忘的同时提升了特征判别性，为SAR图像的小样本增量学习提供了新的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Few-shot class-incremental learning (FSCIL) in synthetic aperture radar imagery presents unique challenges due to severe data scarcity and SAR-specific variability. In particular, strong azimuth sensitivity in SAR induces large intra-class variation and inter-class confusion, and FSCIL sequential updates further lead to catastrophic forgetting of previously learned classes. Inspired by neural collapse, we propose an optical-guided SAR FSCIL framework, which derives orthogonal feature subspaces from a data-rich optical ATR dataset and uses them as geometric priors to guide SAR feature learning. SAR features are projected onto these orthogonal subspaces via principal angle constraints, effectively transferring discriminative structure from the optical to the SAR domain. Specifically, our projection loss and the classifier loss optimized with a frozen simplex-ETF geometry jointly induce neural collapse by concentrating features around class means while maintaining large inter-class angles. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark comprising an optical ATR dataset and a SAR ATR dataset with 24 target classes, organized into a base training session and seven incremental sessions. Compared with recent FSCIL methods including NCFSCIL and so on, our method achieves the highest final accuracy and a favorable trade-off between final performance and performance degradation. Moreover, neural collapse metrics show improved intra-class compactness and inter-class separability, indicating that the learned features more closely approximate the ideal simplex-ETF geometry.

中文摘要翻译: 合成孔径雷达图像中的小样本类增量学习由于严重的数据稀缺和SAR特有的变异性而面临独特挑战。特别是SAR中强烈的方位角敏感性会导致较大的类内差异和类间混淆，而FSCIL的顺序更新进一步导致先前学习类别的灾难性遗忘。受神经坍缩的启发，本文提出一种光学引导的SAR FSCIL框架，该框架从数据丰富的光学自动目标识别数据集中导出正交特征子空间，并将其作为几何先验来引导SAR特征学习。通过主角度约束将SAR特征投影到这些正交子空间上，从而有效地将判别性结构从光学域迁移到SAR域。具体地，我们的投影损失和基于冻结simplex-ETF几何优化的分类器损失共同诱导神经坍缩，使特征围绕类均值聚集同时保持大类间角度。我们在一个包含光学ATR数据集和具有24个目标类别的SAR ATR数据集的基准上评估了该方法，该基准被组织为一个基础训练会话和七个增量会话。与包括NCFSCIL在内的最新FSCIL方法相比，我们的方法取得了最高的最终准确率，并在最终性能与性能退化之间达到了更优的权衡。此外，神经坍缩指标显示了改进的类内紧致性和类间可分性，表明学习到的特征更接近理想的simplex-ETF几何结构。

</details>


### [47] [Impostor: An Agent-Curated Benchmark for Realistic AIGC Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04545)
*Zhenliang Li,Yutao Hu,Qixiong Wang,Wenpeng Du,Hongxiang Jiang,Jiasong Wu,Xiaolong Jiang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Impostor——一个包含10万张AI编辑图像的高质量操作定位数据集，以及PhaseAware-Net (PANet)——一种结合局部相位建模与语义-取证一致性学习的定位方法，显著提升了图像操作检测与定位的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像操作检测与定位（IMDL）基准在视觉真实性、操作多样性和生成器覆盖范围上存在局限，难以反映生成式图像编辑的最新趋势，亟需更全面、更具挑战性的基准数据集。

Method: 1) 提出CraftAgent闭环智能体框架，集成了场景感知、编辑规划、操作执行、质量验证和迭代反思，可自动生成多样且视觉真实的被操作图像；2) 提出PhaseAware-Net (PANet)，一个语义-取证框架，通过局部相位建模和语义-取证一致性学习来定位语义上看似合理但取证上已被破坏的区域。

Result: 实验表明，Impostor对现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）和专用IMDL方法构成了显著挑战，而PANet在Impostor和多个公开基准上均取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: Impostor为AIGC-based IMDL提供了更全面的基准，PANet通过结合局部相位和语义-取证信息有效提升了对语义合理但取证异常区域的定位能力。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in generative image editing have improved the realism and controllability of localized image manipulation, raising new challenges for image manipulation detection and localization (IMDL). However, existing IMDL benchmarks still have limitations in visual realism, manipulation diversity, and generator coverage, making it difficult to reflect recent trends in image manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce Impostor, a high-quality AI-edited image manipulation localization dataset containing 100K manipulated images. Impostor is constructed by CraftAgent, a closed-loop agent framework that integrates scene perception, editing planning, manipulation execution, quality validation, and iterative reflection to automatically generate diverse and visually realistic manipulated images. Moreover, Impostor contains images generated by seven recent AIGC models across three manipulation types and includes multiple manipulated regions, providing a more comprehensive benchmark for AIGC-based IMDL. Furthermore, we propose PhaseAware-Net (PANet), a semantic-forensic framework that introduces local phase modeling and semantic-forensic consistency learning to better localize semantically plausible yet forensically disrupted manipulated regions. Extensive experiments show that Impostor poses significant challenges to existing large vision-language models (LVLMs) and specialized IMDL methods, while PANet achieves superior performance on Impostor and multiple public benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式图像编辑的最新进展提升了局部图像操作的真实感和可控性，这给图像操作检测与定位（IMDL）带来了新的挑战。然而，现有的IMDL基准在视觉真实性、操作多样性和生成器覆盖范围方面仍存在局限，难以反映图像操作的最新趋势。为解决这些问题，我们提出了Impostor——一个高质量的人工智能编辑图像操作定位数据集，包含10万张被操作图像。Impostor由CraftAgent构建，这是一个闭环智能体框架，集成了场景感知、编辑规划、操作执行、质量验证和迭代反思模块，能够自动生成多样化且视觉真实的被操作图像。此外，Impostor包含由七种最新AIGC模型生成的三种操作类型的图像，并包含多个被操作区域，为基于AIGC的IMDL提供了更全面的基准。进一步地，我们提出了PhaseAware-Net（PANet），这是一个语义-取证框架，通过引入局部相位建模和语义-取证一致性学习，更好地定位那些语义看似合理但在取证上已被破坏的操作区域。大量实验表明，Impostor对现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）和专用IMDL方法构成了显著挑战，而PANet在Impostor和多个公开基准上均取得了优越的性能。

</details>


### [48] [4D Reconstruction from Sparse Dynamic Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04593)
*Kazuki Ozeki,Shun Kenney,Yuto Shibata,Eisuke Takeuchi,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Ryosuke Sawata,Yuki Mitsufuji,Yoshimitsu Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向稀疏动态相机设置（多台独立移动相机）的4D重建方法，通过3D轨迹初始化、深度排序正则化和多样化批采样策略，有效解决了跨视图和跨时间的时空不一致问题，并发布了新数据集LetCamsGo。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态3D（4D）重建方法多针对单目动态相机或密集固定相机阵列，在稀疏动态相机设置（少量独立移动相机）下，直接扩展这些方法无法解决复杂的跨视图、跨时间时空不一致问题。本文旨在探索一种低成本、实用的稀疏动态相机4D重建方案。

Method: 提出基于3D轨迹初始化的方法，通过集成相机间特征匹配与相机内点追踪来确保时空一致性；引入噪声鲁棒的深度排序正则化损失和时空多样化批采样策略，以增强优化稳定性和跨视图泛化能力。

Result: 在自建数据集LetCamsGo（5个序列、4种不同环境、3台独立移动相机+1台固定相机）上的综合基准测试表明，所提框架在动态区域的4D重建质量上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文的低成本稀疏动态相机4D重建方案有效可行，为野外真实场景下的4D重建开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: Although dynamic 3D (i.e., 4D) reconstruction from a monocular dynamic camera has recently advanced, it remains fundamentally limited by depth ambiguity. In this paper, we focus on an alternative practical way, i.e., sparse dynamic camera setup, where a handful of independently moving cameras capture the same subjects. While keeping capture costs low, this setup introduces multi-view constraints and remains practical for real-world video production such as sports, concerts, and TV shows. Despite its potential, our experiments show that naive extensions of existing monocular or dense-fixed camera-based methods are insufficient since they fail to resolve the complex spatiotemporal inconsistencies across views and time. To fill this gap, we propose a simple yet effective 3D track initialization method designed to ensure spatiotemporal consistency by integrating inter-camera feature matching with intra-camera point tracking. Additionally, we incorporate a noise-robust depth-ordering regularization loss and a spatiotemporally diverse batch sampling strategy to enhance optimization stability and cross-view generalization. Furthermore, to address the lack of standardized benchmarks for this task, we introduce LetCamsGo, a new real-world video dataset with 5 sequences across 4 diverse environments, recorded by three independently moving cameras and one fixed camera. Comprehensive benchmarking on LetCamsGo demonstrated that our proposed framework improves 4D reconstruction quality in dynamic regions compared with baselines, paving the way for a low-cost 4D reconstruction paradigm in the wild.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于单目动态相机的动态3D（即4D）重建近期取得了进展，但其根本上仍受限于深度模糊。本文聚焦于一种替代的实用方案：稀疏动态相机设置，即由少量独立移动的相机同时拍摄同一主体。这种设置在保持采集成本低廉的同时引入了多视图约束，并且对于体育赛事、音乐会、电视节目等真实视频制作场景具有实用性。尽管潜力巨大，但实验表明，直接扩展现有的单目或密集固定相机方法并不足够，因为它们无法解决视图间和时间维度上复杂的时空不一致问题。为填补这一空白，我们提出了一种简单而有效的3D轨迹初始化方法，通过集成相机间特征匹配与相机内点追踪来确保时空一致性。此外，我们引入了对噪声鲁棒的深度排序正则化损失和时空多样化批采样策略，以增强优化稳定性和跨视图泛化能力。针对该任务缺乏标准化基准的问题，我们发布了LetCamsGo——一个包含5个序列、覆盖4种不同环境的真实视频数据集，由三台独立移动相机和一台固定相机录制。在LetCamsGo上的全面基准测试表明，与基线方法相比，我们所提出的框架在动态区域的4D重建质量上取得了提升，为野外低成本4D重建范式铺平了道路。

</details>


### [49] [COMBINER: Composed Image Retrieval Guided by Attribute-based Neighbor Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04604)
*Zixu Li,Yupeng Hu,Zhiwei Chen,Haokun Wen,Xuemeng Song,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COMBINER网络，基于属性原型解决组合图像检索中视觉相似但属性不同样本的多模态特征融合与相似度建模问题，通过自适应语义解缠、统一原型组合和双重关系建模三个模块实现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CIR方法忽略视觉相似但属性不同的样本，导致多模态特征融合和相似度建模效果不佳。

Method: （1）自适应语义解缠模块（ASD）：基于多模态原始特征解缠属性特征；（2）统一原型组合模块（UPC）：构建跨模态统一原型（CUP），促进多模态特征组合；（3）双重关系建模模块（DRM）：基于属性相似度挖掘成对关系和邻居关系。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的综合实验验证了COMBINER的有效性。

Conclusion: COMBINER是首个处理视觉相似但属性无关样本的CIR方法，通过属性原型相似度度量实现了更准确的样本间语义关系理解。

Original Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) represents a challenging retrieval task that targets locating specific images through multimodal inputs. Despite recent progress in CIR techniques, prior approaches often overlook cases where images appear visually alike yet differ in attributes, potentially undermining both multimodal feature fusion and similarity modeling. To mitigate this limitation, we design a unified representation of cross-modal features based on attribute prototypes. Nevertheless, the task is far from straightforward, owing to three core issues: (1) entanglement in attribute-level semantics, (2) inconsistency across modalities, and (3) supervised signal missing. To tackle the above obstacles, we introduce a COMposed image retrieval network guided By attrIbute-based NEighbor Relations (COMBINER). Specifically, we first design an Adaptive Semantic Disentanglement module, which is capable of disentangling attribute features based on multimodal primitive features. Secondly, we propose a Unified Prototype-based Composition module, which can construct cross-modal unified prototypes (CUP) and facilitate multimodal feature composition. Finally, we introduce a Dual Relations Modeling module, which can mine pairwise and neighbor relations based on attribute similarity. Compared to traditional neighbor relations modeling CIR methods, COMBINER represents the first study addressing the phenomenon of visually similar but attribute-unrelated samples. It achieves a more accurate understanding of the semantic relations among samples by employing an attribute prototype-based similarity metric. Comprehensive experiments conducted on three benchmark datasets confirm the effectiveness of our proposed COMBINER. The implementation of our method will be accessed at https://github.com/Lee-zixu/COMBINER

中文摘要翻译: 组合图像检索（CIR）是一项具有挑战性的检索任务，旨在通过多模态输入定位特定图像。尽管CIR技术近期取得了进展，但先前的方法往往忽略了图像视觉上相似但属性不同的情况，这可能会削弱多模态特征融合和相似度建模的效果。为缓解这一局限性，我们基于属性原型设计了跨模态特征的统一表示。然而，该任务远非易事，主要面临三个核心问题：（1）属性级语义的纠缠性，（2）跨模态的不一致性，以及（3）监督信号的缺失。为解决上述障碍，我们引入了基于属性邻居关系引导的组合图像检索网络（COMBINER）。具体而言，我们首先设计了一个自适应语义解缠模块，该模块能够基于多模态原始特征解缠属性特征。其次，我们提出了一个统一原型组合模块，该模块可以构建跨模态统一原型（CUP）并促进多模态特征组合。最后，我们引入了一个双重关系建模模块，该模块能够基于属性相似度挖掘成对关系和邻居关系。与传统的邻居关系建模CIR方法相比，COMBINER是首个处理视觉相似但属性无关样本现象的研究。它通过采用基于属性原型的相似度度量，实现了对样本间语义关系更准确的理解。在三个基准数据集上进行的综合实验证实了我们提出的COMBINER的有效性。我们的方法实现将在https://github.com/Lee-zixu/COMBINER提供。

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Symmetric Alignment: Spectral Diagnostics of Modality Imbalance in Vision-Language Models in the Medical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04613)
*Alessandro Gambetti,Qiwei Han,Cláudia Soares,Hong Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAS（谱对齐分数），一种非对称度量指标，用于诊断视觉-语言模型在多模态医疗数据中的模态信息失衡问题。实验表明，医学图像比文本包含更丰富的结构信息，SAS在零标签下与检索性能的相关性最强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有表征对齐度量为对称指标，将图像和文本两个模态压缩为单一分数，无法揭示哪个模态导致了跨模态性能退化，缺乏诊断性的方向信息。

Method: 提出谱对齐分数（SAS），一种非对称度量方法：将两个模态分别投影到锚定模态的主特征基上，计算特征值加权的每特征模态相关性，得到方向性分数，其差值量化模态信息不平衡程度。

Result: 在15个VLM、自然与医疗图像-文本数据集上评估发现：（1）医学图像比临床报告包含更丰富的结构信息，这种方向性不对称性被所有现有对称指标掩盖；（2）SAS在医疗领域达到最强的零标签与检索性能相关性。

Conclusion: SAS可作为实用的诊断工具，用于临床部署中检测VLM在医疗多模态数据中的模态信息失衡问题。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) struggle when applied to medical image-text data, yet the tools available to diagnose this failure remain limited. Existing representation alignment metrics are symmetric, collapsing both modalities into a single score and hiding which modality drives cross-modal degradation. We introduce the Spectral Alignment Score (SAS), an asymmetric metric that projects both modalities onto the principal eigenbasis of an anchor modality and computes eigenvalue-weighted per-eigenmode correlations, resulting in directional scores whose difference quantifies modality information imbalance. We embed SAS within a benchmarking framework evaluating 15 VLMs across natural and medical image-text datasets alongside 6 alignment metrics and bidirectional retrieval. Our experiments show that medical images retain richer structural information than their paired clinical reports, a directional asymmetry invisible to all competing metrics, and that SAS achieves the strongest zero-label correlation with retrieval performance in the medical domain, positioning it as a practical diagnostic tool for clinical deployment. Code is available at this URL: https://github.com/iamalegambetti/medical-vlms-assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在应用于医学图像-文本数据时表现不佳，但目前可用于诊断这一失败的工具仍然有限。现有的表征对齐度量是对称的，将两个模态压缩为单一分数，从而隐藏了哪个模态驱动了跨模态退化。我们引入谱对齐分数（SAS），一种非对称度量，它将两个模态投影到锚定模态的主特征基上，并计算特征值加权的每特征模态相关性，从而得到方向性分数，其差值量化了模态信息不平衡度。我们将SAS嵌入一个基准框架中，评估了15个VLM在自然和医学图像-文本数据集上的表现，同时使用了6种对齐度量和双向检索。实验表明，医学图像比其配对的临床报告保留了更丰富的结构信息，这种方向性不对称性对所有竞争度量都是不可见的；此外，SAS在医疗领域达到了与检索性能最强的零标签相关性，使其成为临床部署中实用的诊断工具。代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/iamalegambetti/medical-vlms-assessment。

</details>


### [51] [MeshFlow: Efficient Artistic Mesh Generation via MeshVAE and Flow-based Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04621)
*Weiyu Li,Antoine Toisoul,Tom Monnier,Roman Shapovalov,Rakesh Ranjan,Ping Tan,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshFlow 提出了一种基于变分自编码器（VAE）和 Rectified Flow Transformer 的并行网格生成方法，相比自回归方法提速18倍，同时保持高精度，解决了自回归方法推理成本高和量化误差的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归（AR）网格生成方法存在两个主要缺陷：(1) 推理成本随网格规模呈二次增长，扩展性差；(2) 需要对顶点坐标进行离散化，引入量化误差。

Method: (1) 设计一个VAE，利用对比损失监督，将连续的顶点位置和离散的连接关系编码到连续的潜空间中，得到比基于token的网格表示更紧凑的潜表征；(2) 基于 Rectified Flow Transformer 构建3D生成器，并行生成所有网格顶点和边。

Result: MeshFlow 比最快的自回归生成器快18倍，同时在标准网格生成指标上保持了优秀的精度。

Conclusion: MeshFlow 通过连续潜空间表示和并行生成范式，有效克服了自回归方法的扩展性瓶颈和量化误差问题，实现了高效且高精度的3D网格生成。

Original Abstract: We present MeshFlow, a new method for generating artist-like 3D meshes. Current mesh generators often adopt Auto-Regressive (AR) next-token prediction, a natural choice given the discrete nature of mesh topology. However, AR methods scale poorly because the inference cost is quadratic in mesh size. They also require discretizing the vertex coordinates, which introduces quantization errors. To address these challenges, we introduce a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) that, supervised with a contrastive loss, represents both continuous vertex positions and discrete connectivity in a continuous latent space. This latent space is significantly more compact than prior token-based mesh representations. We then build a 3D generator based on a Rectified Flow transformer, generating all mesh vertices and edges in parallel. Our model generates meshes 18x faster than the fastest AR generator while also achieving excellent accuracy across standard mesh-generation metrics. Homepage: https://mesh-flow.github.io/, Code: https://github.com/facebookresearch/meshflow

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了 MeshFlow，一种生成艺术家风格3D网格的新方法。当前的网格生成器通常采用自回归（AR）下一个标记预测，鉴于网格拓扑的离散性质，这是一个自然的选择。然而，AR 方法的扩展性很差，因为推理成本与网格大小呈二次关系。它们还需要对顶点坐标进行离散化，这会引入量化误差。为了解决这些挑战，我们引入了一个变分自编码器（VAE），通过对比损失进行监督，将连续的顶点位置和离散的连接关系表示在一个连续的潜空间中。这个潜空间比先前基于标记的网格表示要紧凑得多。然后，我们基于 Rectified Flow Transformer 构建了一个3D生成器，并行生成所有网格顶点和边。我们的模型比最快的 AR 生成器快18倍，同时在标准网格生成指标上也达到了出色的精度。主页：https://mesh-flow.github.io/，代码：https://github.com/facebookresearch/meshflow

</details>


### [52] [Instance-Level Post Hoc Uncertainty Quantification in Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04656)
*Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Qunli Zhang,Feng Liu,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MC-GLM方法，实现自动驾驶中目标检测框的后验不确定性量化，无需重新训练，且支持实例级不确定性估计，在nuScenes数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动驾驶中的目标检测是安全关键任务，需要对边界框预测进行不确定性量化以确保安全。现有后验不确定性方法存在效率问题：线性化推理需要多次反向传播，时间效率低；采样方法不完全后验。因此需要一种高效、近似后验且支持实例级不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出蒙特卡洛广义线性模型（MC-GLM），结合拉普拉斯近似，通过蒙特卡洛采样实现实例级的不确定性量化。采样数量恒定且独立于输出实例数，因此可并行化，避免了线性化推理中多次反向传播的开销。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上使用CenterPoint检测器进行实验，验证了MC-GLM方法的有效性，生成的不确定性具有良好的质量。

Conclusion: MC-GLM能够为自动驾驶目标检测提供高效、近似后验且支持实例级的不确定性量化，满足实际部署需求。

Original Abstract: Object detection is a safety-critical component of autonomous driving. It is essential to quantify the uncertainty in bounding-box predictions for safety assurance. Post hoc uncertainty quantification without retraining aligns with real-world deployment requirements; therefore, we employ the Laplace approximation. Because instance-level uncertainty is needed, linearized inference methods that require multiple backpropagations are not time-efficient, and sampling-based methods are not fully post hoc. We propose Monte-Carlo generalized linearized model (MC-GLM), which provides instance-level and approximately post hoc uncertainty quantification. The number of samples required in the Monte Carlo step is constant and independent of the number of output instances, so it can be parallelized. Experiments on the nuScenes dataset with the CenterPoint detector validate the effectiveness of our method, and the resulting uncertainties exhibit good quality.

中文摘要翻译: 目标检测是自动驾驶中关乎安全的关键组成部分。对边界框预测进行不确定性量化对于安全保障至关重要。无需重新训练的后验不确定性量化符合实际部署需求，因此我们采用拉普拉斯近似。由于需要实例级的不确定性，需要多次反向传播的线性化推理方法时间效率不高，而基于采样的方法并非完全后验。我们提出了蒙特卡洛广义线性模型（MC-GLM），该方法提供实例级且近似后验的不确定性量化。蒙特卡洛步骤中所需的采样数量恒定且独立于输出实例数量，因此可以并行化。在nuScenes数据集上使用CenterPoint检测器进行的实验验证了我们方法的有效性，所得不确定性具有良好的质量。

</details>


### [53] [Real-Time Automatic License Plate Recognition Using YOLOv8, SORT Tracking, and Temporal Data Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04684)
*Mirza Muhammad Mobeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种5阶段端到端流水线，结合YOLOv8、SORT跟踪和EasyOCR，通过时序边界框插值修复断裂轨迹，提升复杂交通场景下的车牌识别鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实时视频处理中的光照剧烈变化、相机角度倾斜、车辆高速运动及物理遮挡等非受控变量导致车牌识别（ALPR）准确率低下，跟踪路径断裂且OCR性能差。

Method: 5阶段端到端算法流水线：①YOLOv8 nano车辆检测；②SORT多目标跟踪构建时空关联；③YOLOv8专用模型定位车牌区域；④EasyOCR执行OCR并辅以位置语法验证；⑤时序边界框离线插值修复断裂轨迹。

Result: 未提供具体实验数据或定量结果，仅描述了所提出流水线的组成模块及其预期功能。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端流水线通过深度学习检测、运动跟踪与插值机制的有机结合，有望有效缓解复杂动态场景中车牌识别的性能瓶颈。

Original Abstract: The real-time hardships of video processing seriously limit the usage of Automatic License Plate Recognition (ALPR) with application in dynamic traffic monitoring settings. High-fidelity recognition of unconstrained variables, e.g. drastic variations in illumination, acute camera scans, high vehicle speeds, and harsh physical concealment, is a problem that often leads to disjointed tracking paths and poor Optical Character Recognition (OCR) rates. In order to mitigate these weaknesses, the study proposes a 5 stage, end-to-end algorithmic pipeline, encompassing a smooth transition between deep learning based object detection, multi-object tracking which is kinematic in nature, and geometry temporal data interpolation. The suggested architecture takes advantage of a very powerful YOLOv8 nano model to localize the vehicle at the first stage and then Simple Online and Realtime Tracking (SORT) algorithm is used to build spatial-temporal links between frames. Another, more specific typology of YOLOv8 object detectors the license plate area, channeling the sliced array to an EasyOCR chain under the limitations of positional syntax verification. More importantly, an offline interpolation mechanism of temporal bounding box is initiated to recast fragmented paths.

中文摘要翻译: 视频处理的实时性困难严重限制了自动车牌识别（ALPR）在动态交通监控场景中的应用。对非受控变量（如光照剧烈变化、相机大角度倾斜、车辆高速行驶及严苛的物理遮挡）的高保真识别往往导致跟踪路径断裂和光学字符识别（OCR）成功率低下。为缓解这些不足，本研究提出了一种5阶段端到端算法流水线，涵盖了基于深度学习的物体检测、运动学性质的多目标跟踪以及几何时序数据插值之间的平滑过渡。所提出的架构利用强大的YOLOv8 nano模型在第一阶段定位车辆，随后采用简单在线实时跟踪（SORT）算法构建帧间时空关联。另一种更特化的YOLOv8检测器负责定位车牌区域，将切片图像送入受位置语法验证约束的EasyOCR流水线。更重要的是，启动了一种时序边界框离线插值机制以重建断裂的轨迹路径。

</details>


### [54] [MeshWeaver: Sparse-Voxel-Guided Surface Weaving for Autoregressive Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04688)
*Jiale Xu,Wang Zhao,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshWeaver提出了一种自回归网格生成框架，通过多级稀疏体素编码器将几何上下文注入生成过程，实现了更高的压缩比（18%）和更大规模（16K面）的网格生成，显著提升了几何保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归网格生成方法存在两个根本性局限：(i) 标记化效率低，导致长序列无法扩展到高多边形网格；(ii) 缺乏几何感知引导，生成仅依赖全局形状嵌入而非局部表面线索。

Method: 提出MeshWeaver框架，将网格生成视为表面编织过程，直接预测下一个顶点而非独立坐标。核心是多级稀疏体素编码器，以三种互补方式注入几何上下文：(1) 提供体素特征作为顶点表示；(2) 通过交叉注意力引导标记预测；(3) 作为结构支架约束生成在输入表面附近。层次化设计实现在单次解码步骤中从粗到细地预测顶点。

Result: MeshWeaver实现了18%的最先进压缩比，可生成多达16K面的网格，几何保真度显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过紧密耦合生成模型与3D几何结构，MeshWeaver有效解决了自回归网格生成中标记化效率低和缺乏几何感知引导的问题，实现了更高质量和大规模的网格生成。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive mesh generation has gained attention by tokenizing meshes into sequences and training models in a language-modeling fashion. However, existing approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: (i) low tokenization efficiency, which yields long token sequences and prevents scaling to high-poly meshes, and (ii) absence of geometry-aware guidance, as generation is conditioned only on global shape embeddings rather than local surface cues. We introduce MeshWeaver, an autoregressive framework that treats mesh generation as a surface weaving process by directly predicting the next vertex instead of independent coordinates. At its core is a multi-level sparse-voxel encoder that injects geometric context into the generative process in three complementary ways: providing voxel features as vertex representations, guiding token prediction via cross-attention to voxel features, and serving as a structural scaffold that constrains generation around the input surface. Our hierarchical design enables coarse-to-fine vertex prediction in a single decoding step, while tightly coupling the generative model with 3D geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MeshWeaver achieves a state-of-the-art compression ratio of 18%, can generate meshes with up to 16K faces, and significantly improves geometric fidelity over prior approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归网格生成通过将网格标记化为序列并以语言建模方式训练模型而受到关注。然而，现有方法存在两个根本性局限：(i) 标记化效率低，导致产生长标记序列且无法扩展到高多边形网格；(ii) 缺乏几何感知引导，因为生成仅依赖于全局形状嵌入而非局部表面线索。我们提出MeshWeaver，一个自回归框架，该框架将网格生成视为表面编织过程，通过直接预测下一个顶点而非独立坐标。其核心是一个多级稀疏体素编码器，以三种互补方式将几何上下文注入生成过程：提供体素特征作为顶点表示，通过交叉注意力引导标记预测，以及作为结构支架将生成约束在输入表面附近。我们的层次化设计能够在单次解码步骤中实现从粗到细的顶点预测，同时将生成模型与3D几何紧密耦合。大量实验表明，MeshWeaver实现了18%的最先进压缩比，能够生成多达16K面的网格，并且几何保真度显著优于先前方法。

</details>


### [55] [A New Angle on Bones: Robust Pose Estimation in X-Ray and Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04700)
*Ron Keuth,Christoph Großbröhmer,Franziska Halm,Miriam Johann,Anne-Nele Schröder,Ludger Tüshaus,Mattias P. Heinrich,Lasse Hansen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的点候选提议结合线模型的自动骨姿态估计方法，通过鲁棒拟合技术处理异常值，在三个儿科临床角度估计任务中达到临床可接受的精度并优于基于标志点的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像分析中骨骼角度测量是常规任务，但人工测量耗时且重复性差，现有自动方法中对异常值敏感的常规线模型（如最小二乘法）效果不佳，需要更鲁棒的自动骨姿态估计方法。

Method: 采用基于学习的点候选提议（point candidate proposal）生成候选点，随后利用线模型提取轴线参数；为克服常规线模型对异常值的敏感性，引入假阳性减少策略以及鲁棒拟合技术，包括RANSAC和霍夫变换。

Result: 在骨折碎片评估（X光和超声）以及Graf法髋关节发育不良评估三个任务上，平均角度误差分别为4.1°、5.4°和5.51°，不仅处于临床观察者变异范围内，且显著优于基于标志点的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自动骨姿态估计方法在多个儿科临床角度测量任务中表现可靠，精度满足临床要求，且代码和标注数据已公开，具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Measuring the angle between bone structures is a routine task in medical image analysis and provides a key quantitative parameter for diagnosis and treatment planning. Automated methods can reduce time and cost while improving reproducibility. In this work, we address automatic bone pose estimation using a learning-based point candidate proposal followed by a line model to extract axis parameters. Since conventional line models such as least squares are sensitive to outliers, we incorporate false-positive reduction strategies and robust fitting techniques, such as RANSAC and Hough transforms, to improve robustness. We evaluate our method on three clinically relevant paediatric angle estimation tasks: fracture fragment assessment in radiographs and ultrasound and developmental dysplasia of the hip evaluation in ultrasound using the Graf method. Our approach achieves mean errors of $4.1^\circ$, $5.4^\circ$, and $5.51^\circ$, respectively, not only remaining within the expected clinical observer variability, but also significantly outperforming landmark-based methods. Our code and annotations for fracture angle assessment in radiographs are publicly available on GitHub.

中文摘要翻译: 测量骨骼结构之间的角度是医学图像分析中的常规任务，为诊断和治疗计划提供关键的定量参数。自动化方法可以减少时间和成本，同时提高可重复性。在这项工作中，我们通过基于学习的点候选提议结合线模型来提取轴线参数，从而解决自动骨姿态估计问题。由于常规线模型（如最小二乘法）对异常值敏感，我们引入了假阳性减少策略和鲁棒拟合技术（如RANSAC和霍夫变换）以提高鲁棒性。我们在三个临床相关的儿科角度估计任务上评估了所提方法：X光片和超声中的骨折碎片评估，以及使用Graf法进行超声检查中的发育性髋关节发育不良评估。我们的方法分别达到了4.1°、5.4°和5.51°的平均误差，不仅保持在预期的临床观察者变异范围内，而且显著优于基于标志点的方法。我们的代码和用于X光片骨折角度评估的标注数据已在GitHub上公开。

</details>


### [56] [Benchmarking Living-Screen-Native GUI Agents on Short-Video Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04701)
*Jiashu Yao,Heyan Huang,Daiqing Wu,Wangke Chen,Huaxi Ai,Haoyu Wen,Zeming Liu,Yuhang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“动态屏幕原生GUI代理”概念，并构建LivingScreen基准，用于评估代理在短视频等动态界面中的表现。实验发现现有模型均未达到人类水平，主要失败模式是观察不足或过度，指出观察控制是未来GUI代理缺失的关键能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GUI代理假设屏幕是静态的（两次操作之间内容不变），但短视频等真实应用违反了这一假设——内容持续播放，用户需要决定看什么以及看多久。因此需要形式化并评测这类动态屏幕场景下的GUI代理能力。

Method: 形式化定义了“动态屏幕原生GUI代理”任务，并构建LivingScreen基准：包含基于浏览器的忠实环境、三级任务套件（由易到难），以及联合评估准确性和信息效率的指标。

Result: 评测多个前沿模型，发现没有任何模型能达到人类成本-准确率表现；模型的主要失败模式是过度观察或观察不足，表明观察控制是当前GUI代理缺失的能力维度。

Conclusion: 动态屏幕场景对现有GUI代理构成显著挑战，观察控制能力是未来代理设计的关键方向。数据与代码将开源。

Original Abstract: GUI agents today assume a static screen, where the world is frozen between two actions. However, real interfaces such as short-video applications violate this assumption, as their content keeps playing, and a competent user must decide what to watch and for how long. We formalize this task as Living-Screen-Native GUI agents and introduce LivingScreen, the first benchmark instantiating it on short-video platforms, with a faithful browser-based environment, a three-tier task suite, and metrics that jointly score accuracy and information efficiency. Evaluating extensive frontier models, we find that none reaches the human cost-accuracy performance, and that their dominant failure mode is over- and under-observation, pointing to observation control as a missing capability axis for future GUI agents. All data and code will be available at https://github.com/BITHLP/LivingScreen.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的GUI代理假设屏幕是静态的，即在两次操作之间世界是冻结的。然而，短视频应用等真实界面违反了这一假设，其内容持续播放，一个称职的用户必须决定看什么以及看多久。我们形式化地将这一任务定义为“动态屏幕原生GUI代理”，并引入LivingScreen——首个在短视频平台上实例化该任务的基准，包含一个基于浏览器的忠实环境、三级任务套件以及联合评分准确性与信息效率的指标。通过评估广泛的前沿模型，我们发现没有任何模型能达到人类的成本-准确率表现，且其主要的失败模式是过度观察和观察不足，这表明观察控制是未来GUI代理缺失的一个能力轴。所有数据和代码将在https://github.com/BITHLP/LivingScreen公开。

</details>


### [57] [Enhancing MedSAM with a Lightweight Box Predictor for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04705)
*Amirhossein Movahedisefat,Amirreza Fateh,Mohammad Reza Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种集成轻量级Box Predictor模块的MedSAM增强框架，通过单次点击生成边界框引导，减少点提示的歧义性，在四个医学影像数据集上取得显著性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学影像语义分割面临数据稀缺和跨模态高变异性的挑战；基础模型（如SAM）需要特定适配才能用于医学图像；点提示（point prompts）作为最自然的交互方式，提供的空间上下文信息不足，尤其在目标结构不规则或对比度差时导致分割不可靠。

Method: 在MedSAM架构中集成轻量级Box Predictor模块。该模块从单次用户点击出发，利用局部图像嵌入特征估计近似边界框，为分割提供空间引导以减少点提示的歧义性。采用两阶段训练流程：先独立训练Box Predictor，再集成到MedSAM中联合优化。仅增加1.6M参数量和极小的推理开销。

Result: 在四个跨模态数据集（CT、MRI、超声）上评估：FLARE22（0.93 Dice）、BRISC（0.88）、BUSI（0.89）、LungSegDB（0.98），证明了方法在不同解剖结构和影像域上的分割精度和鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过单次点击生成边界框引导，有效降低了点提示的歧义性，以极小的额外参数和计算代价显著提升了医学影像分割性能，具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Semantic segmentation in medical imaging is a critical yet challenging task due to data scarcity and high variability across modalities. While foundation models like the Segment Anything Model (SAM) show promise, they often struggle with medical images without specific adaptation. Moreover, point prompts, despite being the most natural form of user interaction, provide insufficient spatial context for reliable segmentation, particularly when target structures are irregular or poorly contrasted. In this paper, we propose an enhanced segmentation framework that integrates a lightweight Box Predictor module into the MedSAM architecture. The Box Predictor estimates an approximate bounding box from a single user click using localized image embedding features, providing spatial guidance that reduces the ambiguity of point prompts, while introducing only 1.6M additional parameters and negligible inference overhead. We introduce a two-stage training pipeline where the Box Predictor is trained independently before being integrated into MedSAM. To validate the generalization capability of our method, we conduct extensive evaluations on four diverse datasets (FLARE22, BRISC, BUSI, LungSegDB) spanning distinct imaging modalities, including CT, MRI, and Ultrasound. Our method improves segmentation accuracy and robustness across varied anatomical structures and imaging domains, achieving Dice scores of 0.89 (BUSI), 0.93 (FLARE22), 0.88 (BRISC), and 0.98 (LungSegDB). Code is available at https://github.com/Amirhosseinmovahedi/MedSAM-BoxPredictor

中文摘要翻译: 医学影像中的语义分割是一项关键且具有挑战性的任务，原因在于数据稀缺以及不同模态之间的高变异性。虽然像Segment Anything Model（SAM）这样的基础模型展现出潜力，但在没有特定适配的情况下，它们往往难以处理医学图像。此外，点提示（point prompts）尽管是最自然的用户交互形式，但提供的空间上下文信息不足以实现可靠的分割，尤其是在目标结构不规则或对比度较差的情况下。本文提出了一种增强的分割框架，将轻量级边界框预测器（Box Predictor）模块集成到MedSAM架构中。该边界框预测器利用局部图像嵌入特征，从单次用户点击出发估计一个近似的边界框，提供空间引导以减少点提示的歧义性，同时仅引入160万个额外参数且推理开销可忽略不计。我们引入了一个两阶段训练流程：先独立训练边界框预测器，再将其集成到MedSAM中。为了验证我们方法的泛化能力，我们在四个涵盖不同成像模态（包括CT、MRI和超声）的多样化数据集（FLARE22、BRISC、BUSI、LungSegDB）上进行了广泛评估。我们的方法在不同解剖结构和影像域中均提升了分割精度和鲁棒性，在BUSI、FLARE22、BRISC和LungSegDB上分别取得了0.89、0.93、0.88和0.98的Dice分数。代码已开源：https://github.com/Amirhosseinmovahedi/MedSAM-BoxPredictor

</details>


### [58] [ReConFuse: Reconstruction-Error Guided Semantic Fusion for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04706)
*Xiaojing Chen,Xinyu Lu,Changtao Miao,Yunfeng Diao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReConFuse框架，利用预训练WF-VAE的重建误差作为判别线索，结合多帧语义特征和Mamba时序建模，实现有效的AI生成视频检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AI生成视频日益逼真，带来虚假信息、内容真实性等严重问题，需要可靠的检测方法；现有基于重建误差的图像检测方法难以直接扩展到视频，因为视频重建误差在帧间具有时序组织，需要语义上下文才能有效解释。

Method: 提出ReConFuse框架：首先使用预训练WF-VAE重建输入视频提取重建误差线索，然后将重建误差与多帧语义特征对齐，最后使用基于Mamba的模块建模时序演化，进行视频级分类。

Result: 在多个生成器及多种评估设置下的实验表明，ReConFuse具有有效性和强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 重建误差可作为AI生成视频检测的有效判别线索，ReConFuse通过重建引导的语义融合框架实现了良好的检测性能和泛化性。

Original Abstract: AI-generated videos are becoming increasingly realistic, raising serious concerns about misinformation, content authenticity, and media trust. Reliable AI-generated video detection is therefore essential for multimedia forensics, yet remains challenging due to the need to capture spatial artifacts, temporal dynamics, and generalize to evolving generative models. In this paper, we explore reconstruction error as a discriminative forensic cue for AI-generated video detection. By reconstructing input videos with a pretrained WF-VAE, we observe that real and generated videos exhibit distinguishable frame-wise reconstruction error patterns, suggesting that reconstruction errors can reveal their distributional discrepancies. However, extending reconstruction-based image detection to videos is non-trivial, since video reconstruction errors are temporally organized across frames and require semantic context for effective interpretation. To address these challenges, we propose ReConFuse, a reconstruction-guided semantic fusion framework for video-level AI-generated video detection. ReConFuse extracts reconstruction error cues from WF-VAE reconstructed videos, aligns them with multi-frame semantic features, and uses a Mamba-based module to model temporal evolution for video-level classification. Experiments across multiple generators and evaluation settings demonstrate the effectiveness and strong generalization ability of ReConFuse.

中文摘要翻译: AI生成的视频正变得越来越逼真，这引发了关于虚假信息、内容真实性和媒体信任的严重担忧。因此，可靠的AI生成视频检测对于多媒体取证至关重要，但由于需要捕捉空间伪影、时序动态以及泛化到不断演变的生成模型，这一任务仍然具有挑战性。在本文中，我们探索将重建误差作为AI生成视频检测的判别性取证线索。通过使用预训练的WF-VAE重建输入视频，我们观察到真实视频和生成视频表现出可区分的逐帧重建误差模式，这表明重建误差可以揭示它们的分布差异。然而，将基于重建误差的图像检测扩展到视频并非易事，因为视频重建误差在帧间具有时序组织，并且需要语义上下文才能有效解释。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了ReConFuse，一个用于视频级AI生成视频检测的重建引导语义融合框架。ReConFuse从WF-VAE重建的视频中提取重建误差线索，将其与多帧语义特征对齐，并使用基于Mamba的模块建模时序演化以进行视频级分类。在多个生成器和多种评估设置下的实验证明了ReConFuse的有效性和强大的泛化能力。

</details>


### [59] [Data Efficient Complex Feature Fusion Network For Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04710)
*Maitreya Shelare,Atharva Satam,Poonam Sonar,Sneha Burnase*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DE-CFFN，一种数据高效的注意力双分支复数特征融合网络，用于高光谱图像分类。通过因子分析降维、逐层减半3D卷积滤波器数以及SE模块，在保持与CFFN相当分类性能的同时显著降低模型大小、内存消耗和推理延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始CFFN模型参数量大、计算开销高，不适合实时高光谱成像应用。本文旨在保持分类性能的前提下，通过特征提取过程优化和架构增强，大幅降低模型复杂度，实现轻量化部署。

Method: 保留CFFN的双流结构（RVNN处理标准高光谱块，CVNN处理傅里叶变换后的数据）；使用因子分析替代主成分分析进行降维以改进潜在特征表示；在RVNN和CVNN分支中逐层将3D卷积层的滤波器数量减半以降低复杂度；将双流输出拼接后通过Squeeze and Excitation (SE)模块增强联合特征表示。

Result: 在Pavia University和Salinas数据集上，DE-CFFN的分类性能与CFFN相当，但模型大小、内存消耗和推理延迟显著降低，适用于实时高光谱成像应用。

Conclusion: DE-CFFN在保持高光谱图像分类精度的同时实现了显著的模型轻量化，证明了因子分析降维和滤波器数量递减策略在减少计算开销方面的有效性。

Original Abstract: This work presents a data-efficient variant of the Attention-Based Dual-Branch Complex Feature Fusion Network (CFFN) for hyperspectral image classification. The proposed model, termed DE-CFFN, retains the original two-stream structure: the Real-Valued Neural Network (RVNN) processes standard hyperspectral patches, while the Complex-Valued Neural Network (CVNN) handles their Fourier-transformed counterparts. The main contribution of this work lies in the feature extraction process and architectural enhancement. Factor Analysis is used for dimensionality reduction, offering improved latent feature representation over Principal Component Analysis. Additionally, both the RVNN and CVNN streams are structurally modified by successively halving the number of filters in the 3D convolutional layers to reduce complexity. The outputs of both branches are concatenated and passed through a Squeeze and Excitation (SE) block to enhance joint feature representation. Evaluated on the Pavia University and Salinas datasets, DE-CFFN achieves classification performance comparable to CFFN, while significantly reducing model size, memory consumption, and inference latency, making it suitable for real-time hyperspectral imaging applications.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种数据高效的注意力双分支复数特征融合网络（CFFN）变体，用于高光谱图像分类。所提出的模型称为DE-CFFN，保留了原始的双流结构：实值神经网络（RVNN）处理标准高光谱块，而复值神经网络（CVNN）处理其傅里叶变换后的对应数据。本文的主要贡献在于特征提取过程和架构增强方面。采用因子分析进行降维，相比主成分分析提供了更优的潜在特征表示。此外，对RVNN和CVNN流均进行了结构修改，通过逐层将3D卷积层的滤波器数量减半来降低复杂度。两个分支的输出被拼接后通过Squeeze and Excitation（SE）模块以增强联合特征表示。在Pavia University和Salinas数据集上的评估结果表明，DE-CFFN在实现与CFFN相当分类性能的同时，显著减小了模型尺寸、降低了内存消耗和推理延迟，使其适用于实时高光谱成像应用。

</details>


### [60] [StrokeTimer: Robust Representation Learning for Ischemic Stroke Onset-Time Estimation from Non-contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04722)
*Weiru Wang,Susanne G. H. Olthuis,Elizaveta Lavrova,Robert J. van Oostenbrugge,Charles B. L. M. Majoie,Wim H. van Zwam,Ruisheng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StrokeTimer框架，利用自监督解耦学习与能量引导对比学习，从非增强CT图像中估计急性缺血性卒中发病时间，在大型多中心数据集上显著优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 缺血性卒中治疗高度依赖发病时间窗，但真实临床中发病时间常不确定，且现有数据集存在类别不平衡和中心-扫描仪异质性，亟需自动化、鲁棒的发病时间估计方法。

Method: 集成自监督解耦学习与能量引导对比学习，捕获细微缺血模式，同时应对长尾数据分布与采集变异性；将发病时间分为<4.5小时、4.5-6小时、>6小时三个临床相关窗口。

Result: 在两个全国性多中心NCCT数据集上，macro AUC达0.69，macro F1-score达0.57，较最强基线提升近50%（p<0.005）；基线方法在该挑战性场景下表现接近随机。

Conclusion: StrokeTimer能有效估计急性缺血性卒中发病时间，模型解释显示出与已知放射学生物标志物一致的灰白质模糊和低密度区域，有望支持临床治疗决策。

Original Abstract: Ischemic stroke is a major global disease. Treatment decisions are highly time-sensitive, as eligibility for reperfusion therapies relies on the interval between stroke onset and intervention. However, the true onset time is often uncertain in clinical practice, necessitating imaging-based assessment of tissue age as a surrogate marker. Early ischemic changes on routinely acquired non-contrast CT (NCCT) are often subtle, and real-world clinical datasets exhibit pronounced onset-time class imbalance and center-scanner-related heterogeneity. In this work, we propose StrokeTimer, a fully automated framework for onset-time estimation in acute ischemic stroke. StrokeTimer integrates self-supervised disentanglement learning with energy-guided contrastive learning to capture subtle ischemic patterns while addressing long-tailed data distributions under acquisition variability. Onset time is categorized into three clinically relevant windows: <4.5 h, 4.5-6 h, and >6 h.
