Experimental results on a large multi-center NCCT dataset from two national cohorts, MR CLEAN Registry and MR CLEAN LATE, show that StrokeTimer achieves a macro AUC of 0.69 and a macro F1-score of 0.57, improving the strongest baseline by nearly 50% (p < 0.005). In this realistic, challenging setting, representative baseline approaches exhibit near-chance macro performance. Model explanations further highlight subtle gray-white matter blurring and hypodense regions consistent with established radiological biomarkers. These findings demonstrate the potential of StrokeTimer to support treatment decision-making in acute ischemic stroke. Code is available at https://github.com/BrainVas/StrokeTimer.

中文摘要翻译: 缺血性卒中是一种全球性的重大疾病。治疗决策具有高度时间敏感性，因为再灌注治疗的资格取决于卒中发病到干预之间的时间间隔。然而，在临床实践中，真实的发病时间往往不确定，因此需要基于影像的组织老化评估作为替代标志物。常规非增强CT上早期缺血性改变通常很细微，而真实世界的临床数据集表现出显著的发病时间类别不平衡和中心-扫描仪相关的异质性。在这项工作中，我们提出了StrokeTimer，一个用于急性缺血性卒中发病时间估计的全自动框架。StrokeTimer集成了自监督解耦学习和能量引导对比学习，以捕获细微的缺血模式，同时应对采集变异性下的长尾数据分布。发病时间被分为三个临床相关窗口：<4.5小时、4.5-6小时和>6小时。在两个国家队列（MR CLEAN Registry和MR CLEAN LATE）的大型多中心NCCT数据集上的实验结果表明，StrokeTimer达到了0.69的macro AUC和0.57的macro F1-score，较最强基线提升了近50%（p<0.005）。在这个具有挑战性的现实场景中，代表性基线方法表现出接近随机的宏观性能。模型解释进一步突出了与已知放射学生物标志物一致的细微灰白质模糊和低密度区域。这些发现证明了StrokeTimer支持急性缺血性卒中治疗决策的潜力。代码可在https://github.com/BrainVas/StrokeTimer获取。

</details>


### [61] [Physics-Informed Video Generation via Mixture-of-Experts Latent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04737)
*Cong Wang,Hanxin Zhu,Jiayi Luo,Yonglin Tian,Xiaoqian Cheng,Peiyan Tu,Xin Jin,Long Chen,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PILA是一个将物理结构化的潜在引导注入预训练视频生成模型（如Wan）的框架，通过锚定场估计、物理属性库、混合专家路由和操作残差正则化，在不破坏视觉先验的前提下提升生成视频的物理合理性，在VBench-2.0等多个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大规模视频生成模型虽在语义一致性和视觉质量上进步显著，但其基于像素级拟合的动态无法自然适应真实世界的运动规律和交互规则，导致生成的视频在物理合理性（如物体运动、相互作用）方面存在持续缺陷。

Method: PILA包含三个关键步骤：(1) 锚定场估计：从冻结生成器的潜在表示中提取可观测运动作为运动学锚点，构建物理属性库（由场代理槽组织）；(2) 混合专家路由：先验标签遮蔽的专家路由选择类别特定的算子专家，并通过从物理关系中抽象出的操作残差进行正则化；(3) 将精炼后的代理融合回物理属性库并解码为流匹配向量场的修正，向预训练主干注入物理感知引导。

Result: 在Wan 2.1-1.3B上进行分阶段适配器训练，并将学到的适配器直接迁移到Wan 2.2-14B，PILA在VBench-2.0、VideoPhy-2和PhyGenBench上同时在视觉质量和基准测得的物理合理性方面达到SOTA结果。

Conclusion: PILA通过物理信息潜在对齐机制，在保持预训练视频模型视觉先验的同时有效注入物理感知约束，显著提升了生成视频的物理合理性，且适配器具有良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Original Abstract: Large-scale video generation models have made remarkable progress in semantic consistency and visual quality, producing videos that are increasingly coherent and visually convincing. Nevertheless, the dynamics induced by pixel-level fitting do not naturally accommodate the regularities that govern real-world motion and interaction, resulting in persistent shortcomings in physical plausibility. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{PILA} (Physics-Informed Latent Alignment), a framework that injects physics-structured latent guidance into the frozen flow-matching dynamics of pretrained video models. Specifically, PILA first employs anchored field estimation to map frozen-generator latents into an operational physical attribute bank organized by field-proxy slots, using observable motion as a kinematic anchor for constructing less directly observed proxies. To handle the heterogeneity of real-world dynamics, PILA adopts a mixture-of-experts design over physical categories. Label-prior masked expert routing selects category-specific operator experts, whose refinements are regularized by operational residuals abstracted from physical relations. Finally, the refined proxies are fused into the physical attribute bank and decoded into a correction to the flow-matching vector field, injecting physics-aware guidance while preserving the visual prior of the pretrained backbone. With staged adapter training on Wan 2.1-1.3B and direct transfer of the learned adapter to Wan 2.2-14B, PILA achieves state-of-the-art results on VBench-2.0, VideoPhy-2, and PhyGenBench in both visual quality and benchmark-measured physical plausibility.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模视频生成模型在语义一致性和视觉质量方面取得了显著进展，能够生成越来越连贯且视觉上令人信服的视频。然而，由像素级拟合诱导的动态过程无法自然适应支配真实世界运动和交互的规律，导致在物理合理性方面存在持续缺陷。为解决这一局限，我们提出了\textbf{PILA}（物理信息潜在对齐，Physics-Informed Latent Alignment），一个将物理结构化的潜在引导注入预训练视频模型冻结流匹配动力学的框架。具体来说，PILA首先采用锚定场估计，将冻结生成器的潜在映射到由场代理槽组织的可操作物理属性库中，利用可观测运动作为运动学锚点来构建较难直接观测的代理。为处理真实世界动态的异质性，PILA采用基于物理类别的混合专家设计。先验标签遮蔽的专家路由选择类别特定的算子专家，这些专家的精炼过程通过从物理关系中抽象出的操作残差进行正则化。最后，将精炼后的代理融合回物理属性库并解码为流匹配向量场的修正，在保持预训练主干视觉先验的同时注入物理感知引导。通过在Wan 2.1-1.3B上进行分阶段适配器训练，并将学到的适配器直接迁移到Wan 2.2-14B，PILA在VBench-2.0、VideoPhy-2和PhyGenBench上同时在视觉质量和基准测得的物理合理性方面取得了最先进的结果。

</details>


### [62] [Do Foundation Models See Biology? Evaluating Attention Coherence with Spatial Transcriptomics in Glioblastoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04764)
*Dilakshan Srikanthan,Amoon Jamzad,Paul Wilson,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Robert Policelli,Gabor Fichtinger,John F. Rudan,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于空间转录组学的正交无假设框架，系统评估五种病理基础模型（CONCH v1.5、UNI v2、Virchow2、GigaPath、H-Optimus-1）的注意力图生物学一致性，发现注意力捕获的是多基因转录程序而非单个分子事件，且空间平滑不等于生物学相干性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 病理基础模型的注意力图是否捕获真实的生物学信息尚不明确，但这一问题是临床信任和监管审批的关键。当前缺乏客观、量化的评估方法，现有研究多依赖定性显著性图审查。

Method: 基于注意力多实例学习，训练单任务和多任务模型预测胶质母细胞瘤的5种分子改变，在CPTAC队列上训练并在TCGA队列上独立验证；利用18个样本的共配准Visium空间转录组数据，以87个转录特征为基准评估注意力图的生物学一致性。

Result: 1）无单一编码器在所有任务上占优，外部验证反转了内部性能排名；2）注意力图从通路（Cohen's d=0.329）到单个基因（d=0.055）呈现五倍富集梯度，表明注意力捕获的是涌现的多基因转录程序；3）空间平滑的注意力图不意味着生物学相干性；4）不同编码器关注不同的生物学区域。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了对病理基础模型从组织病理学中学到什么内容的客观量化评估，推动该领域超越定性显著性图审查。

Original Abstract: Whether attention maps from pathology foundation models capture genuine biology remains unknown, yet this question is critical for clinical trust and regulatory approval. We propose a spatial transcriptomics-based framework for orthogonal, hypothesis-free evaluation of attention and apply it to five pathology foundation models (CONCH v1.5, UNI v2, Virchow2, GigaPath, H-Optimus-1) and a ResNet50 baseline. Using attention-based multiple instance learning, we train single-task and multi-task models to predict five molecular alterations in glioblastoma on the CPTAC cohort, validate on an independent TCGA cohort, and evaluate biological coherence of attention maps against 87 transcriptional signatures using co-registered Visium spatial transcriptomics data from 18 samples. Internally, no single encoder dominates across all tasks, and external validation inverts internal performance rankings. Attention maps show a five-fold enrichment gradient from pathways (Cohen's d=0.329) to individual genes (d=0.055), indicating that attention captures emergent multi-gene transcriptional programs rather than individual molecular events. Spatially smooth attention maps do not imply biological coherence, and different encoders attend to distinct biological compartments. Our framework provides objective, quantitative assessment of what foundation models learn from histopathology, moving the field beyond qualitative saliency map review.

中文摘要翻译: 病理基础模型的注意力图是否捕获了真实的生物学信息仍然未知，然而这一问题对于临床信任和监管审批至关重要。我们提出一个基于空间转录组学的正交无假设评估框架，并将其应用于五种病理基础模型（CONCH v1.5、UNI v2、Virchow2、GigaPath、H-Optimus-1）以及一个ResNet50基线模型。采用基于注意力的多实例学习，我们训练单任务和多任务模型以预测胶质母细胞瘤的5种分子改变，在CPTAC队列上训练并在独立的TCGA队列上验证，同时利用来自18个样本的共配准Visium空间转录组数据，以87个转录特征为基准评估注意力图的生物学一致性。在内部评估中，没有单一编码器在所有任务上占优，而外部验证则反转了内部性能排名。注意力图呈现从通路（Cohen's d=0.329）到单个基因（d=0.055）的五倍富集梯度，表明注意力捕获的是涌现的多基因转录程序而非单个分子事件。空间平滑的注意力图不意味着生物学相干性，且不同编码器关注不同的生物学区域。我们的框架提供了对病理基础模型从组织病理学中学到什么内容的客观量化评估，推动该领域超越定性显著性图审查。

</details>


### [63] [Coarse-to-fine Hierarchical Architecture with Sequential Mamba for Brain Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04772)
*Hoang-Son Vo,Van-Hung Bui,Minh-Huy Mai-Duc,Tien-Dung Mai,Soo-Hyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHASMBrain，一种分层双阶段图像到fMRI编码框架，利用双流Mamba架构分离全局语义和局部空间信息，在NSD数据集上达到SOTA性能，并揭示视觉皮层中不同信息流的因果特异性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索深度视觉表征与人脑视觉系统之间的对应关系，解决现有视觉模型在层级组织上与人脑视觉皮层对应关系不明的问题。

Method: 提出CHASMBrain分层两阶段框架：采用双流Mamba设计分别处理全局语义token和局部空间patch；第一阶段预测去噪的ROI级激活，第二阶段通过Mamba-VAE将粗粒度响应细化为全voxel级预测，采用粗到细策略。

Result: 在NSD数据集上Pearson相关系数达0.429，MSE为0.261，超越岭回归和DINOv2线性探针等基线；因果消融实验发现patch流特异性锁定早期视皮层，CLS流提供更高阶区域的语义上下文；跨被试迁移实验表明骨架网络可泛化于不同个体。

Conclusion: CHASMBrain不仅实现了高效的fMRI编码，还通过因果分析揭示了视觉皮层中全局语义与局部空间信息的功能分工，且模型捕获了共享的、与个体无关的视觉表征。

Original Abstract: Understanding the relationship between deep visual representations and the human visual system is a fundamental challenge in computational neuroscience. While modern vision models achieve strong performance in image recognition, their correspondence with the hierarchical organization of the human visual cortex remains an open question. In this study, we propose CHASMBrain, a novel hierarchical two-stage framework for image-to-fMRI encoding. Our architecture leverages a dual-stream Mamba design to explicitly separate and process global semantic tokens and local spatial patches, motivated by the functional organization of the visual cortex. A coarse-to-fine strategy is employed: Stage 1 predicts denoised ROI-level activations, while Stage 2 refines these coarse responses into full voxel-level predictions using a Mamba-VAE. Experiments on the Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD) demonstrate that our method achieves a Pearson correlation of 0.429 and an MSE of 0.261, outperforming all evaluated baselines including ridge regression and DINOv2 linear probes. Beyond predictive performance, causal branch-ablation experiments reveal an asymmetric specialization: the patch stream is specifically locked to early visual cortex (retinotopic regions), while the CLS stream contributes broader semantic context to higher-order areas -- a correspondence that holds causally, not merely correlationally. Cross-subject transfer experiments further show that the learned backbone generalizes across individuals with minimal per-subject adaptation, suggesting the model captures a shared, subject-agnostic visual representation.

中文摘要翻译: 理解深度视觉表征与人脑视觉系统之间的关系是计算神经科学中的一个基本挑战。尽管现代视觉模型在图像识别中表现出色，但它们与人脑视觉皮层层级组织之间的对应关系仍是一个开放问题。在本研究中，我们提出了CHASMBrain，一种新颖的分层两阶段图像到fMRI编码框架。我们的架构采用双流Mamba设计，受视觉皮层功能组织的启发，显式分离并处理全局语义token和局部空间patch。采用粗到细策略：第一阶段预测去噪的ROI级激活，第二阶段通过Mamba-VAE将粗粒度响应细化为全voxel级预测。在自然场景数据集（NSD）上的实验表明，我们的方法达到了0.429的Pearson相关系数和0.261的MSE，优于所有评估的基线方法，包括岭回归和DINOv2线性探针。除了预测性能外，因果分支消融实验揭示了不对称的特化现象：patch流特异性锁定早期视觉皮层（视网膜拓扑区域），而CLS流为高阶区域提供更广泛的语义上下文——这种对应关系是因果性的，而不仅仅是相关性的。跨被试迁移实验进一步表明，学习到的骨架网络可以在最小的个体适应下跨被试泛化，表明该模型捕获了共享的、与个体无关的视觉表征。

</details>


### [64] [NextMotionQA: Benchmarking and Judging Human Motion Understanding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04773)
*Yong Cao,Chuqiao Li,Xianghui Xie,Gerard Pons-Moll,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NextMotionQA基准，通过三项互补任务（多项选择问答、视频描述、细粒度错误纠正）和三级难度分层，系统评估VLM对人体运动理解的能力，发现现有模型在细粒度判断上存在严重缺陷，而粗粒度判断与专家高度一致。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有运动理解基准存在语义粒度粗糙、难度无区分、注释质量有限和答案模糊等问题，无法诊断模型的真实失败原因，亟需一个更全面、精细的评估体系。

Method: 构建NextMotionQA基准：利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行半自动化生成，经专家验证；包含三项任务（多项选择问答、视频描述、细粒度错误纠正），沿三个核心语义轴组织，并划分为三个任务复杂度等级；评估12种代表性VLM。

Result: 在粗粒度标准上，VLM与专家评分高度一致（Cohen's κ=0.70），但在细粒度、部位级判断上一致性极低（κ=0.10）；传统单任务评估无法暴露的模型能力缺口在NextMotionQA中被揭示。

Conclusion: NextMotionQA能够有效诊断VLM在运动理解中的能力边界：粗粒度评估范式验证有效，但细粒度、部位级判断仍是当前模型的显著短板。

Original Abstract: Reliable evaluation of human motion understanding is fundamental to advancing embodied AI, robotics, and animation. However, existing benchmarks suffer from coarse semantic granularity, undifferentiated difficulty, limited annotation quality, and pervasive answer ambiguity, leaving them unable to diagnose where current models fail. To bridge this gap, we introduce NextMotionQA, a comprehensive benchmark that leverages vision-language models (VLMs) for semi-automated, expert-verified dataset. NextMotionQA features three complementary tasks: multiple-choice question answering, video captioning, and fine-grained error correction. Each task is systematically structured across three core semantic axes and stratified into three task complexity levels. Our extensive evaluation of twelve representative VLMs uncovers critical capability gaps and weakness that remain invisible under conventional, single-task evaluations. In a complementary direction, recent work has begun using VLMs as judges for text-to-motion evaluation; we ask whether they show the same degradation under harder tasks. We find that VLMs align strongly with expert ratings on coarse criteria (Cohen's κ=0.70) but break down on fine-grained, part-level judgment (κ=0.10), validating the paradigm in its strong regime while clarifying its limits.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的人体运动理解评估对于推进具身人工智能、机器人技术和动画领域至关重要。然而，现有基准存在语义粒度粗糙、难度无区分、注释质量有限以及答案普遍模糊等问题，无法诊断当前模型的失败根源。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了NextMotionQA——一个利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行半自动化生成并经专家验证的综合基准。NextMotionQA包含三项互补任务：多项选择问答、视频描述和细粒度错误纠正。每项任务沿三个核心语义轴系统组织，并划分为三个任务复杂度等级。我们对12种代表性VLM的广泛评估揭示了在传统单任务评估中不可见的关键能力缺口和弱点。在互补方向上，近期研究开始将VLM用作文本到运动评估的裁判；我们探究它们在更难的任务上是否表现出同样的性能退化。研究发现，VLM在粗粒度标准上与专家评分高度一致（Cohen's κ=0.70），但在细粒度、部位级判断上一致性极低（κ=0.10），这既验证了该范式在其强适用范围内的有效性，也明确了其局限性。

</details>


### [65] [Z-FLoc: Zero-Shot Floorplan Localization via Geometric Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04788)
*Ayumi Umemura,Toshinori Kuwahara,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本（zero-shot）的室内地板平面图定位方法，通过对单目3D重建的鸟瞰图提取直线与圆等几何基元，并与地板平面图进行鲁棒匹配，无需任何重新训练即可泛化到全新环境。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于学习的地板平面图定位方法需要大规模训练数据和环境特定的重新训练，限制了实际部署能力，亟需一种无需重新训练即可泛化的零样本方法。

Method: 从单目图像重建3D场景并投影至鸟瞰图，从中提取直线与圆等几何基元；利用专用最小求解器在鲁棒估计框架内将这些基元与地板平面图进行匹配，实现零样本定位。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，该方法在未见过的环境中超越了最先进的基于学习的方法，且所有实验使用同一组固定超参数。

Conclusion: 几何基元（直线和圆）提供外观不变的 structural 约束，使零样本地板平面图定位成为可能，兼具泛化性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Visual localization -- estimating a camera pose within a pre-existing map -- is a fundamental problem in computer vision.
  Floorplans are an attractive map representation: they are readily available for most buildings, compact, and inherently invariant to visual appearance changes.
  However, bridging the severe domain gap between camera observations and floorplan geometry remains challenging.
  Existing methods address this gap through data-driven learning, yet they require large-scale training data and environment-specific retraining, limiting their practical deployment.
  We propose a zero-shot floorplan localization method that generalizes to novel environments without any retraining.
  Our key insight is that dominant geometric primitives -- lines and circles -- are ubiquitous in human-made environments and provide appearance-invariant structural constraints.
  We extract these primitives from a bird's-eye-view (BEV) projection of monocular 3D reconstructions and match them to the floorplan via dedicated minimal solvers within a robust estimation framework.
  Experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based methods on unseen environments, while using a single fixed set of hyperparameters across all experiments.
  The source code will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉定位——在预先构建的地图中估计相机位姿——是计算机视觉中的一个基本问题。地板平面图是一种极具吸引力的地图表示形式：它们在大多数建筑中易于获取、紧凑且天然对视觉外观变化具有不变性。然而，弥合相机观测与地板平面图几何之间的严重域差距仍然具有挑战性。现有方法通过数据驱动的学习来应对这一差距，但需要大规模训练数据和环境特定的重新训练，限制了其实际部署。我们提出了一种零样本的地板平面图定位方法，无需任何重新训练即可泛化到新环境。我们的关键洞察是，主导性的几何基元——直线和圆——在人造环境中普遍存在，并提供外观不变的结构约束。我们从单目3D重建的鸟瞰图投影中提取这些基元，并通过鲁棒估计框架内的专用最小求解器将其与地板平面图进行匹配。在模拟和真实数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在未见过的环境中优于最先进的基于学习的方法，且所有实验使用同一组固定超参数。源代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [66] [A Pathology Foundation Model for Gastric Cancer with Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04792)
*Ling Liang,Jiabo Ma,Zhengyu Zhang,Fengtao Zhou,Yingxue Xu,Yihui Wang,Cheng Jin,Zhengrui Guo,On Ki Tang,Zhijian Cen,Zhen Wang,Qi Xie,Chengyu Lu,Chenglong Zhao,Feifei Wang,Yu Cai,Hongyi Wang,Jing Zhang,Yaping Ye,Shijun Sun,Shenglei Li,Yu Wang,Zhenhui Li,Ronald Cheong Kin Chan,Xiuming Zhang,Zhe Wang,Hao Chen,Li Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE是一个针对胃癌的专用病理基础模型，在28项任务上优于通用病理模型，并通过严格的临床验证（包括安全门控标准和病理医师-AI协作阅读研究）证明了其转化价值，显著提升了诊断准确性、效率和一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 胃癌的异质性导致诊断和风险分层困难，通用病理基础模型在胃癌细粒度任务上表现停滞，且缺乏前瞻性验证和临床阅读研究。

Method: 基于48,364张H&E染色全切片图像（来自37,493名患者）开发胃癌专用基础模型GRACE，在28项临床任务上评估，并通过安全门控标准（100% NPV/PPV）和随机交叉阅读研究验证临床转化价值。

Result: GRACE在28项任务上宏观AUC达0.9188，优于通用PFM；在安全门控下可简化69.6%恶性病例审查；病理医师辅助下准确率从82.0%提升至89.9%，诊断时间减少14.9%，置信度提升9.0%，一致性显著改善。

Conclusion: GRACE作为胃癌专用病理基础模型，在多种任务上展现优越性能，并通过严格的临床验证证明了其在实际诊疗中的转化可行性和辅助价值。

Original Abstract: Gastric cancer remains a major cause of cancer mortality, yet its histological and molecular heterogeneity complicates diagnosis and risk stratification. General-purpose pathology foundation models (PFMs) often plateau on fine-grained endpoints central to gastric cancer care, and few have undergone rigorous prospective validation or clinical reader studies. We present GRACE, a Gastric-specific foundation model for Real-world Assessment and Clinical dEcision support. GRACE was developed from multicenter gastric pathology datasets totaling 48,364 primarily HE-stained whole-slide images from 37,493 patients. When evaluated on 28 clinically relevant tasks, GRACE consistently outperformed representative pancancer PFMs, achieving a macro-AUC of 0.9188, with strong performance for precancerous lesion diagnosis (macro-AUC 0.9322), tumor histopathological assessment (macro-AUC 0.9119), molecular profiling (macro-AUC 0.8682), and prognostic prediction. Beyond benchmarking, GRACE's translational value was substantiated through a rigorous evidence chain. Under safety-gated criteria requiring 100% NPV for rule-out and 100% PPV for rule-in, GRACE streamlined review for up to 69.6% of malignancy-diagnosis cases and triaged 46.8% of MMR-IHC follow-up requests. This translational feasibility was further strengthened by a randomized crossover reader study of pathologist-AI collaboration. With GRACE assistance, diagnostic accuracy improved from 82.0% to 89.9%, yielding nearly twofold higher adjusted odds of a correct diagnosis (OR 1.987) alongside concurrent gains in sensitivity and specificity. AI assistance also reduced diagnostic time by 14.9%, elevated diagnostic confidence by 9.0%, and markedly improved inter-rater agreement. When calibrated to maintain non-inferior performance to senior pathologists, the AI-assisted workflow could triage 60.7% of atrophy and 82.7% of intestinal metaplasia cases.

中文摘要翻译: 胃癌仍然是癌症死亡的主要原因，但其组织学和分子异质性给诊断和风险分层带来了复杂性。通用病理基础模型（PFMs）在胃癌诊疗的关键细粒度任务上往往表现停滞，且很少有模型经过严格的前瞻性验证或临床阅读研究。我们提出了GRACE——一个面向真实世界评估和临床决策支持的胃癌专用基础模型。GRACE基于多中心胃癌病理数据集开发，共包含来自37,493名患者的48,364张以H&E染色为主的全切片图像。在28项临床相关任务的评估中，GRACE持续优于代表性的泛癌种病理基础模型，宏观AUC达到0.9188，在癌前病变诊断（宏观AUC 0.9322）、肿瘤组织病理学评估（宏观AUC 0.9119）、分子分型（宏观AUC 0.8682）以及预后预测方面均表现出色。除基准测试外，GRACE的转化价值通过一条严格的证据链得到了证实。在要求排除诊断时阴性预测值（NPV）为100%、确认诊断时阳性预测值（PPV）为100%的安全门控标准下，GRACE可简化高达69.6%的恶性肿瘤诊断病例的审查，并分流46.8%的MMR-IHC随访请求。这种转化可行性通过一项病理医师-AI协作的随机交叉阅读研究得到了进一步强化。在GRACE辅助下，诊断准确率从82.0%提升至89.9%，正确诊断的校正后优势比（OR）提高了近1.97倍，同时灵敏度和特异度也同步提升。AI辅助还将诊断时间缩短了14.9%，将诊断置信度提高了9.0%，并显著改善了评估者间的一致性。当校准至与资深病理医师保持非劣效性能时，AI辅助工作流可分流60.7%的萎缩病例和82.7%的肠化生病例。

</details>


### [67] [Crafting Your Evolving Dreams: Concept-Incremental Versatile Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04797)
*Jiahua Dong,Wenqi Liang,Hongliu Li,Yang Cong,Duzhen Zhang,Hanbin Zhao,Henghui Ding,Yulun Zhang,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种持续可定制扩散模型（CCDM），通过属性解耦LoRA和相关性引导聚合策略解决增量学习中的灾难性遗忘和概念忽视问题，并采用可控区域上下文合成策略增强多概念合成的一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有定制扩散模型假设用户概念集合是静态的、无法增量增长，且在增量学习新概念时存在严重的灾难性遗忘和概念忽视问题。

Method: 1) 设计属性解耦LoRA（AD-LoRA）模块和相关性引导的AD-LoRA聚合策略，保留每个任务的概念特定属性并利用任务间相关性缓解灾难性遗忘；2) 提出可控区域上下文合成策略，通过保证用户定义区域的语义独立性和平滑边界过渡来增强多概念合成的一致性，解决概念忽视问题。

Result: 实验表明CCDM在持续定制任务上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CCDM通过AD-LoRA和可控区域上下文合成策略有效解决了增量学习中的灾难性遗忘和概念忽视问题，实现了概念增量式的多功能定制。

Original Abstract: Custom diffusion models (CDMs) have garnered significant interest owing to their remarkable capacity for generating personalized concepts. However, the majority of CDMs unrealistically presume that the user's collection of personalized concepts is static and incapable of incremental growth over time. Furthermore, they exhibit significant catastrophic forgetting and concept neglect of previously learned concepts when incrementally learning a sequence of new ones. To resolve the above challenges, we develop a novel Continually Customizable Diffusion Model (CCDM), enabling users to perform concept-incremental versatile customization. Specifically, we design an attribute-decoupled LoRA (AD-LoRA) module and a relevance-guided AD-LoRA aggregation strategy to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. They can preserve concept-specific attributes of each task and leverage beneficial inter-task correlations to enhance the continual learning of new customization tasks. Additionally, to address the challenge of concept neglect, we propose a controllable regional context synthesis strategy that performs multi-concept composition in alignment with user-provided conditions. This strategy enhances the overall consistency in multi-concept synthesis by guaranteeing semantic independence between user-defined regions and their smooth boundary transitions. Experiments show our CCDM exhibits significant improvements over baseline methods.

中文摘要翻译: 定制扩散模型（CDMs）因其生成个性化概念的卓越能力而引起了广泛关注。然而，大多数CDMs不切实际地假设用户收集的个性化概念集合是静态的，无法随时间推移而逐步增长。此外，当增量学习一系列新概念时，它们对先前学过的概念表现出显著的灾难性遗忘和概念忽视。为解决上述挑战，我们开发了一种新颖的持续可定制扩散模型（CCDM），使用户能够进行概念增量式的多功能定制。具体来说，我们设计了一个属性解耦LoRA（AD-LoRA）模块和一个相关性引导的AD-LoRA聚合策略来缓解灾难性遗忘。它们能够保留每个任务的概念特定属性，并利用有益的任务间相关性来增强新定制任务的持续学习。此外，为解决概念忽视的挑战，我们提出了一种可控区域上下文合成策略，该策略根据用户提供的条件进行多概念合成。该策略通过保证用户定义区域之间的语义独立性及其平滑的边界过渡，增强了多概念合成的整体一致性。实验表明，我们的CCDM相较于基线方法有显著改进。

</details>


### [68] [Fast Cubical Persistent Homology on 2D and 3D Images via Union-Find, Pruning, and Lookup Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04801)
*Titouan Le Breton,Karol Szustakowski,Marie Piraud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flash Cubical，一种在V-滤子下高效计算2D/3D图像立方体持续同调的方法，通过并查集、边剪枝和查找表三大核心技术，实现了目前最快的V-滤子立方体持续同调计算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有立方体持续同调计算效率低下，尤其在V-滤子下时间和内存成本较高，缺乏针对性和优化实现。

Method: 基于三个核心思想：(1)利用立方体复形的性质，通过并查集和对偶性计算最高维度的持续同调；(2)通过剪枝特定边来加速并查集实现；(3)利用查找表预计算局部信息，利用立方体复形的规则性避免运行时重复计算。

Result: 在V-滤子立方体持续同调计算中，该方法在时间和内存成本上均达到目前最优效率。

Conclusion: 虽然本文聚焦于V-滤子，但核心思想可自然推广到T-滤子立方体复形，并为其他复形上的持续同调计算提供了有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: We present Flash Cubical, a highly efficient computation of cubical persistence on a V-filtration for 2D and 3D images over $\mathbb{F}_2$. The implementation is built around three core ideas. First, cubical complexes satisfy properties that allow for the computation of persistence of the highest dimension via union-find and duality. Second, pruning of certain edges allows for a fast and efficient implementation of union-find. Third, the use of a lookup table, which exploits the regularity of cubical complexes to pre-compute local information. This avoids the need to compute local information at run time. To the best of our knowledge, this is the most efficient implementation of cubical persistence with a V-filtration, both in terms of time and memory costs. Although the paper focuses on persistence for V-filtration cubical complexes, the underlying ideas generalise naturally to T-filtrations on cubical complexes and suggest promising directions for other complexes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Flash Cubical，一种在V-滤子下高效计算2D和3D图像上$\mathbb{F}_2$域立方体持续同调的方法。该实现围绕三个核心思想构建。第一，立方体复形具有的特性允许通过并查集和对偶性计算最高维度的持续同调。第二，对特定边的剪枝使得并查集得以快速高效实现。第三，利用查找表，利用立方体复形的规则性预计算局部信息，避免了运行时计算局部信息的需要。据我们所知，这是在时间和内存成本上最高效的V-滤子立方体持续同调实现。尽管本文聚焦于V-滤子立方体复形的持续同调，但其核心思想可自然推广到立方体复形上的T-滤子，并为其他复形提供了有前景的方向。

</details>


### [69] [NoRA: Evaluating Grounded Reasonableness in Visual First-person Normative Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04806)
*Sichao Li,Sai Ma,Daniel Kilov,Secil Yanik Guyot,Zhuang Li,Seth Lazar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NoRA基准，通过第一人称视频评估LLM/智能体系统生成并合理性、有依据的候选动作的能力，发现现有VLM能恢复合理动作，但难以构建完整动作空间并绑定正确支撑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么只在文本中评估规范性判断，要么将问题简化为从固定候选动作中选择，而实际智能体必须从零开始识别合理动作，基于可见事实并提供可审查的理由。

Method: 构建NoRA基准，包含1,420个带标注的第一人称视频片段（HumanGold-190和LLMSilver-1230），要求模型生成候选动作并通过事实-理由-动作支持图进行合理性证明，从动作对齐、事实依据和支撑绑定三个维度聚合为合理性评分。

Result: 对12个多模态系统在直接、深思和结构化三种提示策略下进行基准测试，发现当前VLM能恢复合理动作和相关场景事实，但难以构建完整合理动作空间，且无法将选定动作绑定到正确的局部支撑上。

Conclusion: NoRA使当前模型的缺陷可量化，将评估问题从模型能否选择动作，转变为模型能否基于正确的可见理由证明合适动作的合理性。

Original Abstract: LLMs and agentic systems are increasingly deployed in social environments, making normative competence critical for safe and appropriate behavior. However, existing approaches either assess normative judgment in text alone or reduce it to choosing among a fixed set of candidate actions. We argue both are insufficient. In practice, agents are never handed a menu of options; they must identify a reasonable action from scratch, grounded in visible facts and supported by inspectable reasons. We introduce NoRA, a visual first-person video benchmark that requires models to generate candidate next actions and justify each through an explicit fact-reason-action support graph. The benchmark comprises 1,420 annotated video clips, including HumanGold-190 and LLMSilver-1230 splits. Each instance is evaluated through action alignment, factual grounding, and support binding, aggregated into a single grounded reasonableness score. We benchmark 12 multimodal systems under direct, deliberate, and structured prompting regimes, finding that current VLMs frequently recover plausible actions and relevant scene facts, but consistently struggle to construct the full reasonable action space and bind selected actions to the correct local support. NoRA makes this gap measurable, shifting the evaluation question from whether a model can pick an action to whether it can justify an appropriate action for the right visible reasons.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型和智能体系统越来越多地部署在社会环境中，使规范性能力对安全且恰当的行为变得至关重要。然而，现有方法要么仅通过文本评估规范性判断，要么将问题简化为从一组固定候选动作中进行选择。我们认为这两种方法都不充分。在实践中，智能体永远不会被给予一个选项菜单；它们必须从零开始识别一个合理的动作，该动作基于可见事实并受可审查的理由支撑。我们提出了NoRA，一个视觉第一人称视频基准，要求模型生成候选下一步动作，并通过显式的事实-理由-动作支撑图对每个动作进行合理性证明。该基准包含1,420个已标注视频片段，包括HumanGold-190和LLMSilver-1230两个子集。每个实例通过动作对齐、事实依据和支撑绑定三个维度进行评估，并聚合为一个基于事实的合理性评分。我们在直接、深思和结构化三种提示策略下对12个多模态系统进行了基准测试，发现当前的视觉语言模型经常能恢复合理的动作和相关场景事实，但始终难以构建完整的合理动作空间，并将选定动作绑定到正确的局部支撑上。NoRA使这一差距变得可量化，将评估问题从模型能否选择一个动作，转变为模型能否基于正确的可见理由证明一个合适动作的合理性。

</details>


### [70] [Dream.exe: Can Video Generation Models Dream Executable Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04811)
*Rui Zhao,Kaiming Yang,Jifeng Zhu,Siyang Chen,Ziqi Wang,Weijia Wu,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Heng Wang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dream.exe框架，通过将生成视频转化为机器人可执行轨迹，评估视频生成模型对物理世界的理解能力，发现视觉质量与可执行性并不相关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视频生成模型在视觉上表现出色，但缺乏衡量其对物理世界理解程度的定量标准。机器人操作可作为检验模型是否内化物理规律的具体窗口。

Method: 构建视频到执行的评估流水线：给定场景图像和任务描述，Dream.exe合成操作视频，将生成运动转换为机器人轨迹，并在物理模拟器中执行获得接地信号。覆盖8个模型（闭源、开源、机器人专用），101个手工整理的操作任务，三个物理复杂度级别。

Result: 多个模型取得了可测量的执行成功率，表明互联网规模数据学习到的生成先验已编码有意义的物理知识；但视觉质量与可执行性之间相关性很弱，说明标准视觉评估无法捕捉模型在物理可执行维度上的能力。

Conclusion: Dream.exe可作为视频生成模型物理世界理解能力的补充评估工具，揭示了视觉质量之外的重要能力维度。

Original Abstract: Video generation models have made impressive strides in synthesizing visually compelling content, yet their outputs remain confined to the virtual domain. A natural question follows: how well do these models reflect the physical world when their generated videos leave the screen and enter reality? We propose robotic manipulation as a concrete, measurable window onto this question: if a model has truly internalized physical laws, the motion it depicts should translate into executable robot behavior. We introduce Dream.exe, an evaluation framework that operationalizes this criterion through a video-to-execution pipeline. Given a scene image and a task description, Dream.exe synthesizes a manipulation video, converts the generated motion into robot trajectories, and executes them in a physics simulator, yielding a grounding signal that purely visual metrics cannot offer. Using this pipeline, we evaluate 8 models spanning frontier closed-source generators, open-source generators, and robot-specific models. Our benchmark covers 101 manually curated manipulation tasks at three levels of physical complexity, measured across visual quality, trajectory fidelity, and execution success. Encouragingly, several models achieve measurable execution success, suggesting that generative priors learned from internet-scale data already encode meaningful physical knowledge. Yet visual quality proves a poor predictor of executability, exposing a dimension of model capability that standard visual evaluations do not capture. Dream.exe will be open-sourced at https://github.com/showlab/Dream.exe.

中文摘要翻译: 视频生成模型在合成视觉上引人注目的内容方面取得了令人瞩目的进展，但其输出仍局限于虚拟领域。一个自然的问题随之而来：当生成的视频离开屏幕进入现实时，这些模型在多大程度上反映了物理世界？我们提出机器人操作作为衡量这一问题的具体、可量化的窗口：如果一个模型真正内化了物理定律，它所描绘的运动应当能够转化为可执行的机器人行为。我们提出了Dream.exe，一个通过视频到执行流水线来操作化这一标准的评估框架。给定场景图像和任务描述，Dream.exe合成操作视频，将生成的运动转换为机器人轨迹，并在物理模拟器中执行，从而提供纯视觉指标无法提供的接地信号。利用该流水线，我们评估了8个模型，涵盖前沿闭源生成器、开源生成器和机器人专用模型。我们的基准测试涵盖101个手工整理的操作任务，分为三个物理复杂度级别，从视觉质量、轨迹保真度和执行成功率三个维度进行衡量。令人鼓舞的是，多个模型取得了可测量的执行成功率，表明从互联网规模数据中学习到的生成先验已经编码了有意义的物理知识。然而，视觉质量被证明是执行性的糟糕预测指标，暴露出标准视觉评估无法捕捉的模型能力维度。Dream.exe将在https://github.com/showlab/Dream.exe开源。

</details>


### [71] [OA-CutMix: Correcting the Label Bias of CutMix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04820)
*Tobias Christian Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Federico Raue,Brian B. Moser,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对象感知的CutMix（OA-CutMix），通过分割掩码修正标签偏差，在不改变图像混合过程的情况下，以极低的训练成本超越多种静态和动态混合增强方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CutMix的标签分配基于一个错误假设：粘贴补丁的面积忠实反映其语义贡献。实际上补丁常落在背景区域，导致标签与可见目标区域之间存在21.5%的平均偏差，且17%的样本贡献了零可见目标像素却获得非零标签权重。

Method: OA-CutMix用预计算的分割掩码替代基于面积的标签权重，根据每个图像在混合中贡献的可见目标区域面积比例来分配标签，而图像混合过程本身保持不变。

Result: 在4种架构和6个数据集上与10余种静态和动态混合方法对比，OA-CutMix在所有任务中持续取得最高精度，超越动态混合方法，但训练成本仅为动态方法的一小部分；对小物体的改进尤为显著。

Conclusion: 修正标签偏差本身就足以匹配甚至超越那些修改图像混合算法的方法的性能。

Original Abstract: CutMix has become the de facto standard mixing augmentation, yet its label assignment rests on a flawed assumption: The area of the pasted patch faithfully reflects its semantic contribution to the mixed image. In practice, however, patches frequently land on background regions, assigning label credit to classes whose objects are not visible. The mean discrepancy of the CutMix label and the semantic object area is $21.5\%$. In $17\%$ of samples an image contributes zero visible object pixels yet receives nonzero label weight. We propose Object-Aware CutMix (OA-CutMix), which corrects this bias by replacing the area-based CutMix weight with one derived from precomputed segmentation masks, assigning labels in proportion to the visible object area each image contributes to the mix. The image mixing procedure is left entirely unchanged. We evaluate OA-CutMix against 10+ static and dynamic mixing methods across 4 architectures and 6 datasets. OA-CutMix consistently achieves the highest accuracy over all tasks, outperforming even dynamic mixing methods, but at a fraction of the training-time cost. Improvements are largest for small objects, where the label bias from CutMix is greatest. Thus, correcting the label is sufficient to match or exceed the performance of methods modifying the image mixing algorithm.

中文摘要翻译: CutMix已成为事实上的标准混合增强方法，但其标签分配基于一个有缺陷的假设：粘贴补丁的面积忠实地反映了其对混合图像的语义贡献。然而在实践中，补丁经常落在背景区域，将标签权重分配给其目标对象不可见的类别。CutMix标签与语义目标区域之间的平均偏差达到21.5%。在17%的样本中，某个图像贡献了零个可见目标像素，却获得了非零的标签权重。我们提出对象感知的CutMix（OA-CutMix），通过用预计算的分割掩码导出的权重替代基于面积的CutMix权重来纠正这一偏差，根据每个图像在混合中贡献的可见目标区域面积比例来分配标签。图像混合过程本身完全保持不变。我们在4种架构和6个数据集上将OA-CutMix与10余种静态和动态混合方法进行了评估。OA-CutMix在所有任务中持续取得最高精度，甚至超越了动态混合方法，但训练成本仅为动态方法的一小部分。对于小物体，改进幅度最大，因为CutMix的标签偏差在这些物体上最为严重。因此，修正标签本身就足以匹配甚至超越那些修改图像混合算法的方法的性能。

</details>


### [72] [3D Temporal Analysis for Autism Spectrum Disorder Screening During Attention Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04836)
*Inam Qadir,Elizabeth B Varghese,Dena Al-Thani,Marwa Qaraqe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于DECA的3D时序分析框架，利用LSTM/GRU对VR-CPT任务中儿童的3D头部姿态和面部特征进行自闭症筛查，GRU模型在39名7-12岁参与者中达到84.6%的多模态融合准确率，优于传统2D方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学龄儿童自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）筛查至关重要，但现有筛查依赖主观评估和2D分析方法，无法捕捉ASD行为的空间位移模式，缺乏客观、自动化的筛查工具。

Method: 基于DECA 3D建模框架提取头部姿态参数（平移分量Tx, Ty, Tz）和与姿态无关的面部表情特征；训练LSTM和GRU时序分类器；采用PCA降维进行多模态融合（头部姿态+面部特征）。

Result: GRU模型在3D头部姿态特征上准确率83.9%，3D面部特征81.4%，分别超越2D基线10.7%和7.5%；多模态融合达84.6%最高准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作为学龄儿童ASD客观自动化筛查奠定了基础，有效解决了当前诊断方法的局限性，证明了3D时序特征相比2D方法的显著优势。

Original Abstract: Accurate Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) screening for school-age children is crucial to identify cases that may have been missed earlier and to enable timely interventions supporting social, cognitive, and academic development. Current ASD screening relies on subjective assessments and 2D analysis methods that fail to capture spatial displacement patterns characteristic of ASD behaviors. In this study, a novel 3D temporal analysis framework is presented, built on top of DECA (Detailed Expression Capture and Animation), a 3D modeling framework, to extract comprehensive head pose parameters (including translational components $T_x, T_y, T_z$) and facial expressions independent of pose variations. LSTM and GRU-based temporal classifiers were trained on the extracted 3D features from video data collected from 39 participants (19 ASD, 20 TD) aged 7-12 years during Virtual Reality-Continuous Performance Test tasks. The GRU-based models demonstrated superior performance, with 3D head pose features achieving 83.9\% accuracy and 3D facial features reaching 81.4\% accuracy, outperforming 2D baseline approaches by 10.7\% and 7.5\%, respectively. Furthermore, multimodal fusion of 3D head pose and facial features with PCA-based dimensionality reduction achieved the highest accuracy of 84.6\%, outperforming unimodal approaches. This work establishes a foundation for objective, automated screening tools addressing current diagnostic limitations in ASD identification for school-age populations.

中文摘要翻译: 对学龄儿童进行准确的自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）筛查至关重要，有助于识别早期可能被遗漏的病例，并及时实施干预措施以支持其社交、认知和学业发展。目前的ASD筛查依赖主观评估和2D分析方法，无法捕捉ASD行为特征性的空间位移模式。本研究提出了一种新颖的3D时序分析框架，构建于DECA（细节表情捕捉与动画）这一3D建模框架之上，用于提取全面的头部姿态参数（包括平移分量Tx, Ty, Tz）以及与姿态变化无关的面部表情。基于LSTM和GRU的时序分类器在从39名7-12岁参与者（19名ASD，20名正常发育）在虚拟现实-持续性能测试任务中采集的3D特征上进行训练。GRU模型表现更优，3D头部姿态特征达到83.9%的准确率，3D面部特征达到81.4%的准确率，分别比2D基线方法高出10.7%和7.5%。此外，基于PCA降维的3D头部姿态与面部特征多模态融合达到了84.6%的最高准确率，优于单模态方法。本研究为克服当前学龄人群ASD识别中诊断方法的局限性、建立客观自动化筛查工具奠定了基础。

</details>


### [73] [MusaCoder: Native GPU Kernel Generation with Full-Stack Training on Moore Threads GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04847)
*Kun Cheng,Songshuo Lu,Sicong Liao,Tankun Li,Yafei Zhang,Dong Yang,Qiheng Lv,Hua Wang,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MusaCoder是一个面向CUDA和MUSA后端的原生GPU内核生成全栈训练框架，结合渐进式数据合成、多样性拒收微调和执行反馈强化学习，在KernelBench上9B模型匹配甚至超越前沿闭源模型，27B模型达到新SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM难以生成高效的底层GPU内核代码，而基于执行的强化学习面临稀疏奖励、奖励黑客和训练不稳定问题。

Method: MusaCoder包括三大组件：1) 渐进式面向内核的数据合成；2) 保持多样性的拒收微调；3) 通过MooreEval分布式验证器与奖励环境进行执行反馈强化学习。为稳定RL，引入PrimeEcho（首轮锚定多轮奖励）、Buffered Dynamic Retry（从全失败难样本恢复信号）和MirrorPop（离策略序列过滤）。

Result: 在KernelBench和MUSA移植变体上，MusaCoder在正确性和经验加速比上均超越强开源和专有基线。9B模型匹配或超越前沿闭源模型，27B模型达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 全栈执行反馈训练对原生内核生成有效，且Moore Threads GPU能够支持完整的LLM后训练栈，为新兴加速器上的大模型训练与优化提供了实用基础。

Original Abstract: Native GPU kernel generation turns high-level tensor programs into executable, efficient low-level code. Existing Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle with this task, while execution-based reinforcement learning suffers from sparse rewards, reward hacking, and training instability. We present MusaCoder, a full-stack training framework for native GPU kernel generation on CUDA and MUSA backends. MusaCoder combines progressive kernel-oriented data synthesis, diversity-preserving rejection fine-tuning, and execution-feedback Reinforcement Learning (RL) through MooreEval, a distributed verifier and reward environment. To stabilize RL, MusaCoder introduces PrimeEcho for first-turn-anchored multi-turn rewards, Buffered Dynamic Retry for recovering signals from all-failed hard samples, and MirrorPop for off-policy sequence filtering. Experiments on KernelBench and a MUSA-ported variant show that MusaCoder outperforms strong open-source and proprietary baselines in both correctness and empirical speedup, with the 9B model matching or exceeding frontier closed-source models and the 27B model establishing a new state of the art. These results demonstrate not only the effectiveness of full-stack execution-feedback training for native kernel generation, but also the capability of Moore Threads GPUs to support the complete LLM post-training stack, providing a practical foundation for large-model training and optimization on emerging accelerators.

中文摘要翻译: 原生GPU内核生成将高层张量程序转化为可执行的、高效的低层代码。现有的大语言模型难以胜任此任务，而基于执行的强化学习面临稀疏奖励、奖励黑客和训练不稳定的问题。我们提出MusaCoder，一个面向CUDA和MUSA后端的原生GPU内核生成的全栈训练框架。MusaCoder结合了渐进式面向内核的数据合成、保持多样性的拒收微调以及通过MooreEval（一个分布式验证器和奖励环境）进行的执行反馈强化学习。为稳定强化学习，MusaCoder引入了PrimeEcho用于首轮锚定的多轮奖励、Buffered Dynamic Retry用于从全失败难样本中恢复信号，以及MirrorPop用于离策略序列过滤。在KernelBench及其MUSA移植变体上的实验表明，MusaCoder在正确性和经验加速比上均优于强开源和专有基线，其中9B模型匹配或超越前沿闭源模型，27B模型达到了新的最优水平。这些结果不仅证明了全栈执行反馈训练对原生内核生成的有效性，还展示了摩尔线程GPU能够支持完整的LLM后训练栈，为新兴加速器上的大模型训练与优化提供了实用基础。

</details>


### [74] [IRIS-GAN: Staged Specialist Detection of Deepfake Faces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04863)
*Jaume M. Trenchs,Veronica Sanz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IRIS-GAN是一个针对GAN生成人脸图像的专用取证检测器，采用分阶段训练策略（逐步引入更具挑战性的GAN系列并保留早期生成器），在GAN系列上达到99%以上的假图检测率，在真实人脸数据集上达到98.9%准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有通用合成图像检测器在跨生成器迁移场景下性能下降，而GAN生成的人脸是深度伪造内容中最先进、最具危害性的类型，需开发专门的鲁棒检测器以应对不同GAN系列间的分布偏移。

Method: 采用分阶段暴露式训练（staged training），逐步向模型引入难度递增的GAN系列，同时保留早期生成器数据，使模型逐步学习到跨GAN系列的通用伪造特征。

Result: 1) 在考虑的GAN系列上假图检测率超过99%；2) 外部真实人脸数据集分类准确率达98.9%；3) Grad-CAM分析显示存在可测量的、依赖于生成器的空间响应模式，这些模式对仅使用热图的二级分类器仍有信息量；4) 对扩散模型生成人脸的跨族测试表明IRIS-GAN是专用检测器，具备一定泛化到非GAN深度伪造的能力。

Conclusion: 分阶段训练是构建鲁棒的GAN人脸取证检测器的有效策略，专用检测器在跨生成器场景下表现优异。

Original Abstract: We introduce IRIS-GAN, a specialist forensic detector for synthetic face images under cross-generator shift. Rather than addressing universal synthetic-image detection, we focus on faces generated by generative adversarial networks (GANs), which are state-of-the-art in deepfake content, and train the detector through staged exposure to increasingly demanding GAN families while retaining earlier generators. The final model reaches fake-detection rates above 99% across the GAN families considered and classifies an external real-face dataset with 98.9% accuracy. Grad-CAM analysis further reveals measurable generator-dependent spatial response patterns, which remain informative for a secondary heatmap-only classifier. Out-of-family tests on diffusion-generated faces confirm that IRIS-GAN is a specialist detector, with some capability to reach non-GAN deepfakes. These results establish staged training as an effective strategy for robust GAN-face forensics.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了IRIS-GAN，一种用于跨生成器迁移场景下合成人脸图像取证的专用检测器。我们不追求通用的合成图像检测能力，而是聚焦于生成对抗网络（GAN）生成的人脸——这是深度伪造内容中最先进的技术——并通过分阶段暴露于难度递增的GAN系列同时保留早期生成器的策略来训练检测器。最终模型在考虑的GAN系列上达到了99%以上的假图检测率，并将外部真实人脸数据集的分类准确率提升至98.9%。Grad-CAM分析进一步揭示了可测量的、依赖于生成器的空间响应模式，这些模式对仅使用热图的二级分类器仍有信息量。对扩散模型生成人脸的跨族测试证实了IRIS-GAN是一种专用检测器，具备一定的能力覆盖非GAN深度伪造内容。这些结果证明了分阶段训练是实现鲁棒GAN人脸取证的有效策略。

</details>


### [75] [Recent Advances and Trends in Learning-based 3D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04871)
*Adrien Schockaert,Hamid Laga,Hazem Wannous,Vincent Magnier,Guillaume Dufaye,Jean-françois Witz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述论文系统梳理了从离散显式格式到连续隐式场（基于神经渲染或基元溅射）的3D表示家族，重点分析了隐式表示带来的范式转变，并讨论了各表示的公式、变体、优缺点及关键应用，最后指出了开放挑战和未来方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3D表示的选择是现代计算机视觉与图形学管线中的基础性设计决策，直接影响效率、质量和能力。尽管传统表示仍是3D传感器的标准输出并被广泛使用，但近期基于神经网络和基元的表示（如3D高斯溅射）提供了紧凑且可微的替代方案。现有综述多广泛覆盖3D重建任务，而缺乏对3D表示本身演化的聚焦分析，尤其是对隐式表示范式转变的深入探讨。

Method: 本文采用综述方法论，系统调查了主要3D表示家族：从离散显式格式（网格、点云、体素网格）到连续隐式场（神经辐射场、符号距离函数等），以及基于基元溅射的表示（如3D高斯溅射）。对每一类表示，呈现通用公式与变体，讨论其优势与局限性，并突出关键应用场景。

Result: 本文的"结果"是对各3D表示家族的全面比较分析：显式表示（网格、点云、体素网格）在编辑与仿真等下游应用中成熟可靠，但缺乏可微性；隐式神经表示（如NeRF）提供高质量连续场和可微性，但计算成本高；基元溅射表示（如3D高斯溅射）在实时渲染与可微性之间取得了良好平衡。论文揭示了向隐式表示演变的范式转变趋势。

Conclusion: 3D表示正经历从离散显式向连续隐式的范式转变，这一转变从根本上改变了3D/4D工作流。未来研究方向包括：提高隐式表示的计算效率、增强可编辑性与可解释性、融合显式与隐式表示的混合方法，以及拓展到动态4D场景和时间序列数据。

Original Abstract: The selection of an appropriate 3D representation is a fundamental design decision that dictates the efficiency, quality, and capabilities of modern computer vision and graphics pipelines for tasks such as 3D reconstruction, novel-view synthesis and rendering, shape and motion analysis, recognition, and generation. While traditional representations (\eg meshes, point clouds, and volumetric grids) remain standard outputs of 3D sensors (\eg LiDAR and 3D scanners) and are widely used in downstream applications (\eg editing and simulation), recent neural and primitive-based representations (\eg 3D Gaussian Splatting) offer compact and differentiable alternatives opening a wide range of opportunities in applications such as games, AR/VR, autonomous driving, robot navigation, and medical imaging, to name a few. The goal of this paper is to survey the main families of 3D representations from discrete explicit formats to continuous implicit fields based either on neural rendering or primitive splatting. For each type of representation, we present the general formulation and its variants, discuss its benefits and limitations, and highlight key applications. We conclude the paper by outlining the open challenges and potential directions for future research. Distinct from recent surveys that broadly cover 3D object and scene reconstruction, this paper provides a focused analysis on the evolution of 3D representations themselves. We specifically emphasize the paradigm shift toward implicit representations, offering a novel perspective on how these emerging formats fundamentally alter 3D/4D workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 选择合适的三维表示是一个基础性设计决策，它决定了现代计算机视觉与图形学管线在三维重建、新视角合成与渲染、形状与运动分析、识别以及生成等任务中的效率、质量和能力。尽管传统表示（如网格、点云和体素网格）仍然是三维传感器（如激光雷达和三维扫描仪）的标准输出，并广泛应用于下游应用（如编辑和仿真），但近期基于神经网络和基元的表示（如三维高斯溅射）提供了紧凑且可微分的替代方案，在游戏、增强现实/虚拟现实、自动驾驶、机器人导航和医学成像等众多应用中开辟了广泛机遇。本文旨在系统综述从离散显式格式到基于神经渲染或基元溅射的连续隐式场的主要三维表示家族。针对每一类表示，我们给出其通用公式及变体，讨论其优势与局限性，并突出关键应用。最后，我们概述了开放挑战和未来研究的潜在方向。与近期广泛覆盖三维物体与场景重建的综述不同，本文聚焦于三维表示本身的演化分析，特别强调了向隐式表示的范式转变，为这些新兴格式如何从根本上改变三维/四维工作流提供了新颖的视角。

</details>


### [76] [MAOAM: Unified Object and Material Selection with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04880)
*Jaden Park,Valentin Deschaintre,Jason Kuen,Kangning Liu,Iliyan Georgiev,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee,Michael Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAOAM，一个统一的图像选择框架，支持通过文本或点击交互进行物体级和材质级选择，利用VLM结合分割头生成像素级掩码，并设计可扩展的数据生成流程来克服材质选择数据集缺失问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于VLM的图像选择方法以物体为中心且仅支持单一交互模态（文本或点击），无法实现材质级选择，限制了实际应用如纹理重贴或特定材质实例编辑。

Method: 1) 使用VLM结合分割头，VLM理解用户选择意图（物体/材质级）并编码视觉实体、属性和空间关系，分割头将输出token解码为掩码；2) 提出可扩展数据生成流程：收集真实/合成图像并利用VLM生成材质描述；3) 采用多任务训练（点击+文本选择）及辅助VQA任务增强材质理解。

Result: 实验表明MAOAM在多种物体、材质和交互场景下均能实现准确且一致的掩码选择；即使仅用单模态提示训练，模型在推理时组合文本和点击表现出涌现性提升。

Conclusion: MAOAM统一了物体级和材质级选择，支持多模态交互，为实际图像编辑提供了灵活且鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Selection is a core operation in interactive image editing. To be practical, a user should be able to specify and disambiguate the desired selection region through either text or click-based interactions, and the system should support selecting not only objects but also other criteria, such as materials. Material-based selection is valuable for tasks like re-texturing surfaces or editing instances of a specific material. However, existing vision-language-model (VLM) based selection methods are object-centric and typically support a single interaction modality, limiting their applicability. In this work, we thus present Mask Any Object And Material (MAOAM), a unified selection framework that enables precise object and material-level selection across both text- and click-based interactions. MAOAM leverages a VLM with a segmentation head to produce pixel-accurate masks from user prompts: the VLM interprets the user's selection intent (object or material-level) and encodes visual entities, attributes, and spatial relations, while the segmentation head decodes the output token into a mask. A key challenge is the lack of material selection datasets with text annotations. We propose a scalable data generation pipeline: we collect real and synthetic images with material masks, and leverage VLMs to generate material descriptions with rich visual-semantics. We train MAOAM with a multi-task objective over click and text-based selection, along with an auxiliary VQA task derived from the material descriptions to facilitate deeper material understanding. Despite being trained with uni-modal prompts, our model exhibits an emergent improvement in selection when combining text and clicks at inference, enabling flexible image editing workflows. Experiments demonstrate accurate and coherent selections across diverse objects, materials, and interaction scenarios, highlighting robustness in practice.

中文摘要翻译: 选择是交互式图像编辑中的核心操作。为了实用，用户应能够通过文本或点击交互来指定和消除所需选择区域的歧义，并且系统应支持不仅选择物体，还能选择其他标准，例如材质。基于材质的选择对于纹理重贴表面或编辑特定材质实例等任务非常有价值。然而，现有的基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的选择方法以物体为中心，并且通常仅支持单一交互模态，限制了其适用性。因此，在本文中，我们提出了MAOAM（Mask Any Object And Material），一个统一的选择框架，能够在文本和点击两种交互方式下实现精确的物体级和材质级选择。MAOAM利用带有分割头的VLM从用户提示中生成像素级精确掩码：VLM解释用户的选择意图（物体级或材质级）并编码视觉实体、属性和空间关系，而分割头将输出token解码为掩码。一个关键挑战是缺乏带有文本标注的材质选择数据集。我们提出了一种可扩展的数据生成流程：收集带有材质掩码的真实和合成图像，并利用VLM生成具有丰富视觉语义的材质描述。我们通过多任务目标训练MAOAM，涵盖基于点击和文本的选择任务，并辅以一个从材质描述派生的VQA任务，以促进更深层次的材质理解。尽管仅使用单模态提示进行训练，我们的模型在推理时组合文本和点击时表现出涌现性的选择提升，从而实现了灵活的图像编辑工作流。实验表明，该方法在多样的物体、材质和交互场景下均能实现准确且一致的选择，凸显了其实用中的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [77] [DiverAge: Reliable Pluralistic Face Aging with Cross-Age Identity Relation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04881)
*Yueying Zou,Peipei Li,Qianrui Teng,Dianyan Xu,Zekun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiverAge，一种基于扩散自编码的分层多元人脸老化框架，通过CARR推理时引导策略，在保持外观多样性的同时提升跨年龄序列的身份可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有人脸老化方法存在两难：确定性方法缺乏随机多样性，而多样化方法虽引入局部外观变化，但未能显式规范完整老化序列的身份演化，导致序列级序贯可靠性不足。

Method: 基于扩散自编码的分层多元框架：1) 通过随机扩散解码和年龄条件语义调制保持外观级多样性；2) 提出跨年龄身份关系调节器(CARR)，利用真实同身份跨年龄对的跨年龄身份相似性(CIS)先验，通过单边采样时引导抑制过度的身份漂移，无需修改训练目标或引入额外可训练参数。

Result: 实验表明，DiverAge在保持身份保持、年龄准确性、图像质量和外观多样性的同时，改善了序列级序贯可靠性。

Conclusion: DiverAge通过分层设计和CARR引导策略有效解决了人脸老化中多样性与身份可靠性之间的权衡，实现了一对多生成中外观多样性与序贯可靠性的统一。

Original Abstract: Face aging plays an important role in long-term biometric analysis, cross-age identity verification, and forensic identity analysis. Since the same subject may exhibit multiple plausible appearances at a target age due to genetic, environmental, and lifestyle factors, face aging is inherently a one-to-many generation problem. However, pluralism alone is insufficient for reliable face aging: a model should provide appearance-level candidate diversity within each age group while maintaining sequence-level ordinal reliability across ordered age groups. Existing deterministic aging methods can synthesize visually plausible age-progressed faces, but usually lack stochastic diversity. In contrast, pluralistic aging methods introduce local appearance variations, but often fail to explicitly regulate the identity evolution of the full aging sequence. In this paper, we propose \textbf{DiverAge}, a hierarchical pluralistic face aging framework based on diffusion autoencoding. DiverAge preserves appearance-level diversity through stochastic diffusion decoding and age-conditioned semantic modulation. To improve sequence-level reliability, we introduce a Cross-age Identity Relation Regulator (CARR), an inference-time guidance strategy that jointly denoises multiple target age groups. CARR is guided by a Cross-age Identity Similarity (CIS) prior estimated from real same-identity cross-age pairs, and suppresses excessive cross-age identity drift through one-sided sampling-time guidance without modifying the training objective or introducing extra trainable parameters. Experiments demonstrate that DiverAge improves sequence-level ordinal reliability while maintaining identity preservation, age accuracy, image quality, and appearance-level diversity.

中文摘要翻译: 人脸老化在长期生物特征分析、跨年龄身份验证和法医身份分析中扮演着重要角色。由于同一个体在目标年龄下可能因遗传、环境和生活方式因素而表现出多种可信外观，人脸老化本质上是一个一对多的生成问题。然而，仅有多元化对于可靠的人脸老化是不够的：模型应当在每个年龄组内提供外观级别的候选多样性，同时在有序年龄组间保持序列级别的序贯可靠性。现有的确定性老化方法可以合成视觉上可信的年龄递进人脸，但通常缺乏随机多样性。相比之下，多元老化方法引入了局部外观变化，但往往未能显式规范完整老化序列的身份演化。在本文中，我们提出了\textbf{DiverAge}，一种基于扩散自编码的分层多元人脸老化框架。DiverAge通过随机扩散解码和年龄条件语义调制来保持外观级别的多样性。为了提升序列级别的可靠性，我们引入了跨年龄身份关系调节器(CARR)，这是一种推理时的引导策略，能够联合去噪多个目标年龄组。CARR受从真实同身份跨年龄对中估计的跨年龄身份相似性(CIS)先验引导，通过单边采样时引导来抑制过度的跨年龄身份漂移，而无需修改训练目标或引入额外可训练参数。实验表明，DiverAge在保持身份保持、年龄准确性、图像质量和外观级别多样性的同时，改善了序列级别的序贯可靠性。

</details>


### [78] [HD-DinoMoE: A Class-Aware Hierarchical Dual Mixture-of-Experts Network for Scleral Anomaly Segmentation in Complex Acquisition Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04888)
*Yinxiang Yu,Maoxiang Chu,Qi Niu,Guanghu Liu,Wei Xu,Haotian Wang,Zhi Chen,Yutian Zhu,Yuelong Fan,Guanghao Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HD-DinoMoE网络，用于中医目诊中巩膜表面异常（血管、黄/黑斑、血斑）的像素级分割，通过类感知双流DINOv3特征融合与多专家解码，在构建的多标签巩膜异常分割数据集上取得72.11%的平均Dice，为复杂采集场景下的智能目诊提供了可行方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 中医目诊依赖经验性巩膜观察，主观性强且难以量化；现有方法无法有效处理临床和用户采集图像中多源分布差异、异常形态多样及巩膜镜面反射等问题，亟需一个智能、可量化的辅助诊断系统。

Method: 提出HD-DinoMoE网络：（1）类感知双流DINOv3特征融合实现双骨干自适应；（2）类特定多专家解码器分别分割血管、黄/黑斑、血斑三类异常；（3）三阶段骨干冻结路由策略稳定双骨干适应；（4）渐进置信惩罚损失降低镜面反射区域的高置信假阳性与分割泄漏；（5）类感知自适应样本加权平衡样本与类别级训练贡献。同时构建了包含Clinical、Wild、Mix三种设置的多标签巩膜异常分割数据集（ML-SASD）。

Result: 在ML-SASD-Mix上平均Dice为72.11%，平均IoU为58.44%；具有良好的边界定位和镜面区域假阳性控制；在公共SBVPI数据集的血管子集上展现出有竞争力的泛化性能。

Conclusion: HD-DinoMoE为TAO在复杂采集场景下提供了一种可行的分割解决方案，其代码和数据已公开。

Original Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) ocular inspection provides empirical cues for assessing scleral surface anomalies, but its clinical use remains subjective and difficult to quantify. To support intelligent and quantifiable ocular inspection, this study presents the TCM-inspired Artificial Intelligence Ocular Auxiliary Diagnosis System (TAO) and focuses on pixel-level scleral surface anomaly segmentation. For clinical and user-acquired images affected by multi-source distributional discrepancies, diverse anomaly morphologies, and scleral specular reflection (SSR), we propose HD-DinoMoE, a class-aware hierarchical dual mixture-of-experts network. HD-DinoMoE combines class-aware dual-stream DINOv3 feature fusion with class-specific multi-expert decoding to segment Vessels, Yellow and Black Spots, and Blood Spots. A three-stage backbone-frozen routing strategy stabilizes dual-backbone adaptation; Progressive Confidence Penalty (PCP) Loss reduces high-confidence false positives and segmentation leakage in SSR regions; and Class-Aware Adaptive Sample Weighting (CA-ASW) balances sample- and class-level training contributions. We further construct the Multi-label Scleral Anomaly Segmentation Dataset (ML-SASD), a new benchmark with Clinical, Wild, and Mix settings and pixel-wise annotations for three anomaly categories. On ML-SASD-Mix, HD-DinoMoE achieves a mean Dice of 72.11% and a mean Intersection-over-Union of 58.44%, while maintaining favorable boundary localization and specular-region false-positive control. It also shows competitive generalization on the Vessels subset of the public SBVPI dataset. These results indicate that HD-DinoMoE provides a feasible segmentation solution for TAO under complex acquisition scenarios. The code and data access information are available at https://github.com/FX-CMX/HD-DinoMoE.

中文摘要翻译: 中医（TCM）目诊通过观察巩膜表面异常提供经验性诊断线索，但其临床应用仍存在主观性强、难以量化的问题。为支持智能化、可量化的目诊，本研究提出了受中医启发的人工智能眼部辅助诊断系统（TAO），并聚焦于巩膜表面异常的像素级分割。针对临床和用户采集图像中存在的多源分布差异、异常形态多样以及巩膜镜面反射（SSR）问题，我们提出了HD-DinoMoE——一种类感知层次化双混合专家网络。HD-DinoMoE结合了类感知双流DINOv3特征融合与类特定多专家解码，用于分割血管、黄/黑斑和血斑。三阶段骨干冻结路由策略稳定了双骨干自适应过程；渐进置信惩罚（PCP）损失降低了SSR区域的高置信假阳性和分割泄漏；类感知自适应样本加权（CA-ASW）平衡了样本级和类别级的训练贡献。我们还构建了多标签巩膜异常分割数据集（ML-SASD），这是一个包含Clinical、Wild和Mix三种设置以及三个异常类别的像素级标注的新基准。在ML-SASD-Mix上，HD-DinoMoE达到了72.11%的平均Dice和58.44%的平均交并比，同时保持了良好的边界定位和镜面区域假阳性控制。在公共SBVPI数据集的血管子集上，它也展现出有竞争力的泛化性能。这些结果表明，HD-DinoMoE为TAO在复杂采集场景下提供了一种可行的分割解决方案。代码和数据访问信息可在https://github.com/FX-CMX/HD-DinoMoE获取。

</details>


### [79] [Hierarchical Space Partition for Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04891)
*Minjie Tang,Xiangfei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种平面组装策略，通过将场景平面分为三类并按优先级进行分层分区，从点云生成紧凑且水密的多边形网格，有效恢复LiDAR扫描中缺失的细节。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LiDAR扫描存在距离限制和遮挡等固有限制，导致关键场景信息缺失，传统方法重建精度下降。需要一种能恢复缺失细节同时保持模型紧凑性的方法。

Method: 将提取的平面分为高可见、低可见和不可见三类（不可见平面通过场景结构分析恢复），三类平面对应三种生长优先级，按优先级逐步进行空间分层分区，然后基于最小割优化生成水密多边形网格。

Result: 在公开数据集上的比较表明，该方法相较主流方法具有有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的平面组装与分层分区策略能有效恢复LiDAR点云中缺失的几何细节，生成紧凑且水密的网格模型，性能优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Generating compact polygonal models from point clouds is a key problem in 3D vision and computer graphics. However, due to inherent limitations of LiDAR scanning (e.g. range constraints and occlusions), critical scene information is often missing, leading to degraded reconstruction accuracy. To address this, we propose a plane assembling strategy that effectively recovers missing details while maintaining model compactness. We classify all the planes extracted from the scene into three categories: highly visible, barely visible, and invisible. The invisible planes, which are recovered by scene structure analysis, indicate the missing details. The three types of planes correspond to the three growth priorities. Each plane grows according to the priority level, and the space is partitioned progressively, namely, the hierarchical partition. Subsequently, we generate a watertight polygonal mesh from the partition via a min-cut-based optimization. Finally, comparisons on public datasets show the effectiveness and superiority of our method against mainstream approaches. The project page is available at https://hsr-3dv.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 从点云生成紧凑的多边形模型是三维视觉和计算机图形学中的一个关键问题。然而，由于LiDAR扫描的固有限制（例如距离约束和遮挡），关键场景信息常常缺失，导致重建精度下降。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种平面组装策略，该策略在保持模型紧凑性的同时有效恢复缺失的细节。我们将从场景中提取的所有平面分为三类：高可见平面、低可见平面和不可见平面。其中不可见平面通过场景结构分析恢复，指示了缺失的细节。三类平面对应三种生长优先级。每个平面根据优先级水平逐步生长，空间被渐进地划分，即分层分区。随后，我们通过基于最小割的优化从分区生成水密多边形网格。最后，在公开数据集上的比较表明，我们的方法相较于主流方法具有有效性和优越性。项目页面见https://hsr-3dv.github.io/。

</details>


### [80] [CDPM-Align: Multi-Scale Guidance-Aligned Diffusion Pretraining for Robust Few-Shot Anatomical Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04898)
*Roberto Di Via,Irina Voiculescu,Francesca Odone,Vito Paolo Pastore*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDPM-align，一种多尺度引导对齐的条件扩散预训练方法，用于在少量标注数据下进行解剖标志点检测。实验表明，生成式预训练能学习鲁棒表示，提升下游任务精度和不确定性估计，推动安全高效的临床部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有解剖标志点检测方法虽已实现亚毫米级定位精度，但仅靠精度不足以满足临床部署需求，还需要预测的可靠性和鲁棒性。然而，表示学习在该场景下的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出CDPM-align，一种多尺度引导对齐的条件扩散预训练方法。采用三种流行的异构小规模基准数据集进行条件生成式预训练，然后在下游标志点检测任务中仅使用少量标注图像（10张和25张）进行微调。

Result: 生成式预训练使模型学习到鲁棒的表示，在少标注（10张和25张图像）的下游标志点检测任务中，同时提升了预测精度和不确定性估计质量。

Conclusion: 条件扩散预训练能有效提升模型在少标注场景下的解剖标志点检测性能，在精度和不确定性两方面均有改善，为实现安全高效的临床部署迈出了重要一步。

Original Abstract: Anatomical landmark detection is a fundamental task in medical image analysis supporting a wide range of diagnostic and interventional workflows. Although recent methods have achieved sub-millimetric localisation, accuracy alone is not sufficient for clinical deployment, requiring reliability and robustness in prediction. Despite its clinical relevance, the impact of representation learning in this context is still underexplored. In this work, we introduce CDPM-align, a multi-scale guidance-aligned conditional diffusion pre-training for anatomical landmark detection. Our experimental setup focuses on a few images and a few annotation regimes. Specifically, we employ three popular heterogeneous small-scale benchmark datasets for representation learning via conditional generative pre-training. Furthermore, we consider low-annotation scenarios for the downstream task of landmark detection, with 10 and 25 annotated images, reflecting realistic trade-offs between clinical effort and resource constraints for annotations. Our results confirm that generative pre-training enables the model to learn a robust representation. This improves both accuracy and uncertainty on the downstream tasks, advancing towards safe and efficient clinical deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 解剖标志点检测是医学图像分析中的一项基础任务，支持广泛的诊断和介入工作流程。尽管近年来的方法已实现亚毫米级定位，但仅靠精度不足以满足临床部署需求，还需要预测的可靠性和鲁棒性。尽管具有临床相关性，表示学习在此背景下的影响仍未得到充分探索。在本工作中，我们提出了CDPM-align，一种用于解剖标志点检测的多尺度引导对齐的条件扩散预训练方法。我们的实验设置专注于少量图像和少量标注的场景。具体而言，我们采用三种流行的异构小规模基准数据集，通过条件生成式预训练进行表示学习。此外，我们考虑了标志点检测下游任务的低标注场景，分别使用10张和25张标注图像，以反映临床努力与标注资源约束之间的现实权衡。我们的结果证实，生成式预训练使模型能够学习到鲁棒的表示，从而在下游任务中同时提升了精度和不确定性估计，向安全高效的临床部署迈进。

</details>


### [81] [BreastGPT: A Multimodal Large Language Model for the Full Spectrum of Breast Cancer Clinical Routine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04911)
*Yang Liu,Jiajin Zhang,Danyang Tu,Yaojun Hu,Jiao Qu,Jiuyu Zhang,Yu Shi,Wei Fang,Shi Gu,Ling Zhang,Yingda Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向乳腺癌临床工作流的统一多模态大模型BreastGPT，通过构建覆盖5种影像模态、136个任务模板的1.86M指令微调语料BreastStage，并设计双分支视觉编码器与概念保持令牌压缩机制，在乳腺癌筛查-诊断-治疗全流程的多模态推理任务上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学多模态大模型受限于数据稀缺和模型泛化能力，通常仅在单一模态或狭窄任务族上评估，无法支持跨筛查、诊断、治疗规划等临床工作流级别的推理能力。乳腺癌临床管理需要多模态推理，但缺乏工作流对齐的数据和评估基准。

Method: (1) 构建BreastStage语料：包含1.86M指令-回答对，覆盖5种影像模态、17个子数据集、136个任务模板，并划分出评估基准BreastStage-Bench；(2) 提出BreastGPT模型：设计双分支视觉编码器以分别处理标准放射影像和千兆像素病理图像，并引入概念保持令牌压缩机制来桥接不同尺度之间的差异。

Result: 在BreastStage-Bench上，BreastGPT在封闭式任务上达到75.66%准确率，开放式任务上达到89.92分，在临床阶段和任务格式上均优于通用型与医学专用型MLLM。

Conclusion: 工作流对齐的数据和跨尺度视觉建模对于构建临床落地的医学多模态大模型至关重要。

Original Abstract: Breast cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women. Its clinical management requires multimodal reasoning across a clinical workflow that spans \textit{screening}, \textit{diagnosis} and \textit{treatment planning}, where each stage involves distinct imaging modalities, task objectives, and reasoning patterns. However, constrained by data scarcity and model versatility, existing medical MLLMs are typically evaluated on isolated modalities or narrow task families, limiting their ability to support workflow-level clinical reasoning. In this work, we first introduce \textbf{BreastStage}, a workflow-aligned breast imaging instruction corpus comprising 1.86M instruction-following pairs curated from 17 sub-datasets across 5 imaging modalities and 136 task templates. Its held-out split, \textbf{BreastStage-Bench}, provides a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating multimodal reasoning across the breast cancer care continuum. Building on this corpus, we propose \textbf{BreastGPT}, a unified MLLM equipped with a dual-branch visual encoder and concept-preserving token compression to bridge the scale gap between standard radiology and gigapixel pathology. On BreastStage-Bench, BreastGPT achieves 75.66\% closed-ended accuracy and 89.92\% open-ended score, outperforming both general-purpose and medical-specific MLLMs across clinical stages and task formats. These results suggest that workflow-aligned data and cross-scale visual modeling are critical for clinically grounded medical MLLMs. All data, code, and model checkpoints are released at https://yangyy-liu.github.io/BreastGPT.io.

中文摘要翻译: 乳腺癌仍然是女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。其临床管理需要在跨越筛查、诊断和治疗规划的临床工作流中进行多模态推理，每个阶段涉及不同的成像模态、任务目标和推理模式。然而，受限于数据稀缺和模型泛化能力，现有的医学多模态大语言模型通常仅在孤立模态或狭窄任务族上进行评估，限制了其支持工作流级别临床推理的能力。本文首先介绍BreastStage——一个工作流对齐的乳腺影像指令语料库，包含从5种成像模态、17个子数据集和136个任务模板中精选的186万条指令-回答对。其独立测试集BreastStage-Bench为评估乳腺癌全程护理中的多模态推理提供了全面的基准。基于该语料，我们提出BreastGPT——一个统一的多模态大语言模型，配备双分支视觉编码器和概念保持令牌压缩机制，以桥接标准放射影像与千兆像素病理图像之间的尺度差异。在BreastStage-Bench上，BreastGPT在封闭式任务上达到75.66%的准确率，在开放式任务上达到89.92分，在临床阶段和任务格式上均优于通用型和医学专用型多模态大语言模型。这些结果表明，工作流对齐的数据和跨尺度视觉建模对于构建临床落地的医学多模态大语言模型至关重要。所有数据、代码和模型检查点均已发布在https://yangyy-liu.github.io/BreastGPT.io。

</details>


### [82] [Geometry-Aware Distillation for Prompt Tuning Biomedical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04922)
*Tran Dinh Tien,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Omni-Geometry知识蒸馏（OGKD）框架，通过向教师模型中注入类别间几何关系结构，生成保留真实标签同时尊重类别间几何关系的方向性目标，并设计全局几何感知蒸馏（GAD）和标签引导几何蒸馏（LGD）两种损失函数，在11个医学数据集上显著优于现有VLM适配方法，平均绝对精度提升1.7%-2.8%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于提示或适配器的视觉-语言模型微调方法仅优化真实标签类别，将所有其他类别视为同等错误，忽略了临床上有意义的类别间关系，导致在有限监督场景下决策边界不稳定。

Method: 提出OGKD框架：（1）将类别关系结构注入教师模型，生成保留真实标签同时尊重类别间几何关系的方向性目标；（2）基于这些目标设计两种蒸馏损失：全局几何感知蒸馏（GAD）作用于全局图像token，标签引导几何蒸馏（LGD）将相同几何关系应用于注意力补丁token以改善细粒度对齐。

Result: 在11个广泛使用的医学数据集上进行base-to-novel和少样本评估，OGKD相比所有现有最先进的VLM适配方法，平均绝对精度提升1.7%-2.8%，且对未见类别具有鲁棒泛化能力，预测更可靠。

Conclusion: 注入类别几何关系的知识蒸馏框架能有效提升视觉-语言模型在医学图像上的适配性能，尤其在有限监督条件下实现更稳定可靠的分类决策边界。

Original Abstract: Current prompt-based and adapter-based tuning of vision-language models (VLMs) is attractive for medical imaging, where clinical data sensitivity favors frozen backbones and annotations are limited. However, these methods typically optimize only the ground-truth class, treating all other classes as equally incorrect, ignoring clinically meaningful class relations and yielding unstable decision boundaries in limited-supervision settings. We propose Omni-Geometry Knowledge Distillation (OGKD), a new framework that injects class-relation structure into the teacher to produce directional targets that preserve the ground truth while respecting inter-class geometry. Using these targets, we develop two distillation losses: Global Geometry-Aware Distillation (GAD) operates on the global image token, and Label-Guided Geometry Distillation (LGD) applies the same geometry to attentive patch tokens to improve fine-grained alignment. Across comprehensive experiments and analyses on 11 widely-used medical datasets for base-to-novel and few-shot evaluations, our OGKD achieves substantially better performance, consistently improving accuracy by an average absolute gain of 1.7%-2.8% over all prior state-of-the-art VLM adaptation counterparts. It also robustly generalizes to unseen classes and yields more reliable predictions than other approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/tientrandinh/OGKD.

中文摘要翻译: 当前基于提示或适配器的视觉-语言模型（VLM）微调方法在医学影像领域具有吸引力，因为临床数据的敏感性要求使用冻结的主干网络且标注数据有限。然而，这些方法通常仅优化真实标签类别，将所有其他类别视为同等错误，忽略了临床上有意义的类别间关系，并在有限监督场景下产生不稳定的决策边界。本文提出Omni-Geometry知识蒸馏（OGKD），一种新的框架，将类别关系结构注入教师模型，生成保留真实标签同时尊重类别间几何关系的方向性目标。基于这些目标，我们设计了两种蒸馏损失：全局几何感知蒸馏（GAD）作用于全局图像token，标签引导几何蒸馏（LGD）将相同几何关系应用于注意力补丁token以改善细粒度对齐。在11个广泛使用的医学数据集上进行base-to-novel和少样本评估的综合实验和分析表明，我们的OGKD取得了显著更优的性能，相比所有先前的VLM适配方法，平均绝对精度持续提升1.7%-2.8%。该方法还能鲁棒地泛化到未见类别，并产生比其他方法更可靠的预测。我们的代码可在https://github.com/tientrandinh/OGKD获取。

</details>


### [83] [Scene-Centric Unsupervised Video Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04925)
*Christoph Reich,Oliver Hahn,Nikita Araslanov,Laura Leal-Taixé,Christian Rupprecht,Daniel Cremers,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了首个无监督视频全景分割方法VideoCUPS，利用深度、运动和视觉线索生成时序一致的伪标签，并引入新的评估协议和基线，在无监督VPS任务上超越所有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督场景理解主要聚焦于图像分割任务，视频领域（尤其是视频全景分割）尚未被充分探索，且缺乏无监督VPS方法。

Method: 提出VideoCUPS方法，通过无监督深度、运动和视觉线索从场景中心视频生成时序一致的全景视频伪标签，并设计Video DropLoss损失函数在这些伪标签上训练模型。

Result: VideoCUPS在所有基线方法上表现最优，展现出强大的标签高效学习能力。

Conclusion: 通过VideoCUPS、评估协议和基线，为无监督视频全景分割的未来研究提供了坚实基础。

Original Abstract: Video panoptic segmentation (VPS) aims to jointly detect, segment, and track all objects while partitioning the video into semantically consistent regions. We introduce the task setting of unsupervised VPS, omitting any human supervision. Existing unsupervised scene understanding works mainly focused on image segmentation tasks; the video domain remains underexplored. We propose VideoCUPS, the first unsupervised VPS approach. VideoCUPS generates temporally consistent panoptic video pseudo-labels from scene-centric videos by exploiting unsupervised depth, motion, and visual cues. Training on these pseudo-labels using a novel Video DropLoss yields an accurate, unsupervised VPS model. To benchmark progress, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation protocol and four competitive baselines, extending state-of-the-art unsupervised panoptic image and instance video segmentation models to VPS. VideoCUPS outperforms all baselines and demonstrates strong label-efficient learning. With VideoCUPS, our evaluation protocol, and baselines, we provide a strong foundation for future research on unsupervised VPS.

中文摘要翻译: 视频全景分割（VPS）旨在联合检测、分割和跟踪所有目标，同时将视频划分为语义一致的区域。我们引入了无监督VPS的任务设定，摒弃任何人工监督。现有无监督场景理解工作主要集中在图像分割任务上，视频领域仍未被充分探索。我们提出了VideoCUPS，这是首个无监督VPS方法。VideoCUPS通过利用无监督深度、运动和视觉线索，从场景中心视频中生成时序一致的全景视频伪标签。使用新颖的Video DropLoss在这些伪标签上进行训练，即可获得准确的无监督VPS模型。为了衡量进展，我们引入了一套全面的评估协议和四个具有竞争力的基线方法，将最先进的无监督全景图像分割和实例视频分割模型扩展到VPS任务。VideoCUPS在所有基线上表现更优，并展现出强大的标签高效学习能力。通过VideoCUPS、我们的评估协议和基线方法，我们为无监督VPS的未来研究奠定了坚实基础。

</details>


### [84] [Plan, Watch, Recover: A Benchmark and Architectures for Proactive Procedural Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04970)
*Kaustav Kundu,Ritvik Shrivastava,Maxim Arap,Nanshu Wang,Xianhui Zhu,Quintin Fettes,Gautam Tiwari,Parth Suresh,Théo Moutakanni,Alejandro Castillejo Munoz,Allen Bolourchi,Pascale Fung,Pinar Donmez,Babak Damavandi,Anuj Kumar,Seungwhan Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了EgoProactive大规模可穿戴数据集和Pro²Bench统一基准，并设计了面向主动式程序辅助的解耦规划器-交互架构与后训练方法，实验表明该方法在干预质量和异常恢复上显著优于多个闭源与开源基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主动式多模态助手系统缺乏大规模、跨领域的真实场景基准，尤其是用户偏离预期步骤序列（Out-of-Plan）这一常见情况未被充分覆盖，限制了该领域的发展。

Method: (1) 发布EgoProactive数据集，包含Out-of-Plan标注与恢复步骤；(2) 将五个已有基准扩展为统一主动式指导模式的Pro²Bench；(3) 提出解耦的规划器-交互架构，专门处理程序状态、视觉线索和恢复注入；(4) 提出跨模型家族的后训练方法，并在Llama 4和Qwen-3.6-VL上验证。

Result: 训练的Llama-4系统在所有六个数据集上显著优于Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT 5.2等强基线；Oracle-plan实验表明在控制计划质量时，训练的duplex模型能产生高质量指导并在Out-of-Plan恢复上取得大幅提升。

Conclusion: 通过大规模数据集、统一基准和专用架构设计，可显著提升主动式程序辅助系统的干预质量和异常恢复能力，验证了所提方法在多个模型家族上的通用性和有效性。

Original Abstract: We envision a proactive multi-modal assistant system which gives users real-time step-by-step guidance on a procedural task, autonomously deciding \textit{when} to interrupt, and \textit{how} to coach. However, progress is limited by the absence of large-scale, cross-domain benchmarks that reflect realistic conditions, particularly the common case in which users deviate from the expected step sequence. We address this gap with four contributions: \textbf{(1)}~we release \textbf{EgoProactive}, a large-scale wearable-egocentric dataset for proactive procedural assistance with explicit Out-of-Plan (OOP) annotations and recovery steps; \textbf{(2)}~we augment five established benchmarks (Ego4D, EPIC-KITCHENS, EgoExo4D, HoloAssist, HowTo100M) into \textbf{Pro\textsuperscript{2}Bench} under a unified proactive-guidance schema; \textbf{(3)}~we propose a \textbf{decoupled planner--interaction architecture} specialized for procedural state, visual cues, and recovery injection; \textbf{(4)}~we introduce a post-training recipe that transfers across model families, validated by cross-backbone replication on Llama~4 and Qwen-3.6-VL. In extensive experiments, our trained Llama-4 system substantially improves objective intervention quality over strong proprietary baselines (Claude Opus~4.6, Gemini~3.1~Pro, GPT~5.2) and open-weight baselines (Qwen3~VL~235B) baselines across all six datasets. Oracle-plan experiments further show that, when plan quality is controlled, the trained duplex model produces high-quality guidance and large gains on Out-of-Plan recovery.

中文摘要翻译: 我们设想一种主动式多模态助手系统，能够在程序性任务中为用户提供实时的逐步指导，自主决定何时打断以及如何指导。然而，由于缺乏大规模、跨领域的真实场景基准，尤其是用户偏离预期步骤序列的常见情况，相关进展受到限制。针对这一空白，我们做出四项贡献：(1) 发布EgoProactive——一个大规模可穿戴第一人称数据集，用于主动式程序辅助，包含显式的计划外（Out-of-Plan, OOP）标注与恢复步骤；(2) 将五个已有基准（Ego4D、EPIC-KITCHENS、EgoExo4D、HoloAssist、HowTo100M）扩展为统一的主动式指导模式Pro²Bench；(3) 提出一种解耦的规划器-交互架构，专门针对程序状态、视觉线索和恢复注入进行设计；(4) 提出一种跨模型家族迁移的后训练方案，并通过在Llama 4和Qwen-3.6-VL上的跨骨干网络复制实验进行验证。在大量实验中，我们训练的Llama-4系统在所有六个数据集上的客观干预质量显著优于强闭源基线（Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT 5.2）和开源基线（Qwen3 VL 235B）。Oracle-plan实验进一步表明，在控制计划质量的情况下，训练的duplex模型能产生高质量指导，并在计划外恢复上取得大幅提升。

</details>


### [85] [Food-R1: A Unified Multi-Task Food Vision-Language Model with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04986)
*Yu Zhu,Yongkang Li,Wenjie Zhu,Haoyi Jiang,Wenyu Liu,Wei Yang,Bin Li,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CalorieBench-80K基准数据集和Food-R1视觉语言模型，通过思维链标注和强化微调提升食物图像分析中的推理与泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有食物分析VLM方法主要依赖监督微调，限制了推理和泛化能力；同时高质量营养标注数据稀缺。为此需要构建大规模基准并设计更好的训练范式。

Method: 构建CalorieBench-80K基准数据集，包含热量标签和饮食建议注释，并首次引入思维链标注用于热量推理；提出Food-R1统一食物VLM，采用多任务学习范式，先进行基于CoT的冷启动指令微调，再使用GRPO进行强化微调以提升推理能力。

Result: 在CalorieBench-80K和代表性基准上的实验表明，Food-R1在食物相关任务上持续优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: Food-R1结合CoT冷启动指令微调和GRPO强化微调的方法能有效提升食物视觉语言模型的推理和泛化能力，代码、模型权重和基准注释已开源。

Original Abstract: Recent studies have explored Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for food analysis. However, most existing methods rely primarily on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which often limits reasoning and generalization capabilities. Moreover, high-quality large-scale nutritional annotations remain scarce. To address these issues, we introduce CalorieBench-80K, a large-scale benchmark with curated calorie labels and dietary advice annotations. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first food image benchmark to incorporate Chain-of-Thought (CoT) annotations for calorie reasoning. We also propose Food-R1, a unified food VLM trained in a multi-task learning paradigm to equip the model with broad capabilities. Food-R1 undergoes CoT-based cold-start instruction tuning, followed by reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to improve reasoning and performance. Experiments on CalorieBench-80K and representative benchmarks show that Food-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines across food-related tasks. The code, model weights, and benchmark annotations are available at the project repository.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究探索了用于食物分析的视觉语言模型（VLM）。然而，现有方法大多依赖于监督微调（SFT），这通常限制了推理和泛化能力。此外，高质量的大规模营养标注数据仍然稀缺。为解决这些问题，我们引入了CalorieBench-80K——一个带有精心校准的热量标签和饮食建议注释的大规模基准数据集。据我们所知，这是首个在热量推理中融入思维链（CoT）标注的食物图像基准。我们还提出了Food-R1，一个统一的食物视觉语言模型，采用多任务学习范式进行训练，使模型具备广泛的能力。Food-R1首先进行基于CoT的冷启动指令微调，然后使用基于群体相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化微调（RFT）来提升推理能力和性能。在CalorieBench-80K及代表性基准上的实验表明，Food-R1在食物相关任务上持续优于强基线模型。代码、模型权重和基准注释已开源至项目仓库。

</details>


### [86] [Multi-Camera AR Guidance System for Surgical Instrument Handling and Assembly: Investigating Workload and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04992)
*Shiyu Li,Julian Kreimeier,Hannah Schieber,Dirk Müller,Bernhard Kainz,Rüdiger von Eisenhart-Rothe,Daniel Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无标记的多相机AR引导系统，结合6D位姿估计与头戴式AR显示，帮助手术室护士进行器械组装，显著降低了认知负荷并缩短了任务完成时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 手术中器械的搬运和组装对刷手护士（尤其是不熟悉器械者）造成高认知负荷，现有方法依赖标记物或缺乏实时引导，因此需要一种无标记、泛化性好、可在术中提供实时AR引导的辅助系统。

Method: 采用多相机系统进行6D位姿估计，利用已知物体实现位姿估计与相机标定；6D位姿估计网络仅使用合成数据训练以提升泛化性；通过头戴式显示器提供AR可视化引导，包括工具尖端定位提示和分步组装动画；用户可通过视线选择和脚踏板切换组装步骤。

Result: 技术评估中，该方法优于现有最先进的6D位姿估计方法。在29名刷手护士参与的膝关节置换手术模拟用户研究中，AR引导相比纸质手册显著降低了感知工作负荷，任务完成时间减少21.3%（4.76分钟），尤其对不熟悉该器械组的护士帮助更大。错误率在两种条件下相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无标记多相机AR引导系统能够主观和客观地改善术中器械操作表现，尤其对未经培训的刷手护士效果显著，提升了流程清晰度、降低了信息过载并增强了操作独立性。

Original Abstract: The handling and assembly of instruments during surgery imposes high cognitive demands on scrub nurses, particularly when instruments are unfamiliar. We present a supporting guidance system for surgical instrumentation that combines multi-camera 6D pose estimation with augmented reality in-situ visualization on a head-mounted display without the requirement for additional markers. Pose estimation and consecutive camera calibration are achieved through known objects. The 6D pose estimation network is trained purely on synthetic data, aiming for better generalizability and real-world applicability. The AR guidance displays tooltip localization cues and step-wise assembly animations. Via gaze-based selection and a foot pedal, users can switch between assembly steps in intraoperative use.
  In a technical evaluation, our approach outperforms state-of-art 6D pose estimation. A user study with 29 scrub nurses was conducted in a surgical simulation of knee arthroplasty, comparing the system against a paper manual. AR guidance significantly reduced the perceived workload compared. Objectively, AR guidance reduced task completion time by 21.3\% (4.76 minutes). Specifically, scrub nurses less experienced with the instrument set benefited when using the system. Error frequencies were comparable between conditions. Qualitative feedback highlighted improved process clarity, reduced information overload, and perceived independence. To summarize, our marker-free multi-camera AR guidance approach for surgical instruments can, subjectively and objectively, improve intraoperative instrumentation performance, particularly for untrained scrub nurses.

中文摘要翻译: 手术过程中器械的搬运和组装对刷手护士提出了很高的认知要求，尤其是在面对不熟悉的器械时。本文提出了一种用于手术器械的辅助引导系统，该系统将多相机6D位姿估计与头戴式显示器上的增强现实（AR）原位可视化相结合，无需额外标记物。位姿估计和后续的相机标定通过已知物体实现。6D位姿估计网络仅使用合成数据进行训练，旨在获得更好的泛化性和实际应用能力。AR引导显示工具尖端定位提示和分步组装动画。通过基于视线的选择和脚踏板，用户可以在术中操作中切换组装步骤。在技术评估中，我们的方法优于现有的最先进的6D位姿估计方法。我们招募了29名刷手护士，在全膝关节置换术的手术模拟中进行了一项用户研究，将本系统与纸质手册进行对比。AR引导显著降低了感知工作负荷。在客观指标上，AR引导将任务完成时间减少了21.3%（4.76分钟）。具体而言，对器械组不太熟悉的刷手护士在使用本系统时获益更大。两种条件下的错误频率相当。定性反馈表明，AR引导提升了流程清晰度、减少了信息过载，并增强了操作独立感。总之，本文提出的用于手术器械的无标记多相机AR引导方法能够从主观和客观两方面改善术中器械操作表现，特别是对于未经培训的刷手护士。

</details>


### [87] [M$^3$Eval: Multi-Modal Memory Evaluation through Cognitively-Grounded Video Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05008)
*Jie Huang,Ruixun Liu,Sirui Sun,Xinyi Yang,Yin Li,Yixin Zhu,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M$^3$Eval 是首个系统性评估多模态模型记忆能力的综合框架与基准，基于认知心理学设计任务。实验发现模型在并行视频处理中难以保持解耦表征、记忆干扰模式异于人类、空间记忆优于时间记忆、符号记忆有限，揭示了记忆作为多模态模型关键但未被充分探索的能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频数据集和基准主要关注感知与推理，缺乏对多模态模型记忆能力的系统评估——模型记住了什么、信息保存的忠实度如何、记忆在干扰下的鲁棒性如何，这些问题尚未被回答。

Method: 基于认知心理学理论，设计精心构造的评估任务来隔离记忆的不同关键维度，构建了首个综合性评估框架与基准 M$^3$Eval，并在代表性多模态模型上进行广泛实验。

Result: 模型在处理并行视频流时难以维持解耦表征；记忆干扰模式与人类记忆存在显著差异；空间域的记忆溯源比时间域更可靠；符号记忆能力有限。

Conclusion: 记忆是多模态模型的基础但尚未被充分探索的能力，M$^3$Eval 为未来研究提供了有价值的资源，研究结果也为设计更有效的多模态模型记忆机制提供了启示。

Original Abstract: As multi-modal models advance towards long-form video understanding, memory emerges as a critical capability. Despite substantial efforts in developing video datasets and benchmarks, existing works primarily focus on perception and reasoning, without systematically evaluating memory: what models retain, how faithfully information is preserved, and how robust memory remains under interference. To address this gap, we introduce M$^3$Eval, the first comprehensive evaluation framework and benchmark for probing different memory dimensions in multi-modal models. Grounded in cognitive psychology, our design features carefully constructed tasks that isolate key aspects of memory. Leveraging M$^3$Eval, we conduct extensive experiments across representative multi-modal models, revealing consistent weaknesses and distinctive behaviors. We find that models struggle to maintain disentangled representations when processing parallel video streams, exhibit interference patterns differing substantially from those observed in human memory, ground memory sources more reliably in the spatial domain than the temporal domain, and demonstrate limited symbolic memory. Collectively, our benchmark provides a valuable resource for future research, while our findings highlight memory as a fundamental yet underexplored capability and offer insights for designing more effective memory mechanisms in multi-modal models. Our code and dataset are available at https://pku-value-lab.github.io/m3eval-homepage.

中文摘要翻译: 随着多模态模型向长视频理解方向发展，记忆成为一项关键能力。尽管在视频数据集和基准构建方面付出了大量努力，现有工作主要关注感知与推理，而未系统评估记忆：模型记住了什么、信息保存的忠实度如何、记忆在干扰下保持鲁棒的程度。为填补这一空白，我们提出了 M$^3$Eval——首个用于探测多模态模型中不同记忆维度的综合性评估框架与基准。基于认知心理学理论，我们的设计包含精心构造的任务，用于隔离记忆的关键方面。借助 M$^3$Eval，我们在代表性多模态模型上进行了广泛实验，揭示了一致的弱点和独特的行为模式。我们发现：模型在处理并行视频流时难以保持解耦的表征；其干扰模式与人类记忆存在显著差异；模型在空间域的记忆溯源比时间域更可靠；符号记忆能力有限。总体而言，我们的基准为未来研究提供了宝贵资源，同时我们的发现表明记忆是一项基础但尚未充分探索的能力，并为设计更有效的多模态模型记忆机制提供了启示。

</details>


### [88] [CIPER: A Unified Framework for Cross-view Image-retrieval and Pose-estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05011)
*Yurim Jeon,Dongseong Seo,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CIPER提出了一种统一的Transformer架构，能够同时进行城市级跨视角图像检索和精确的3自由度位姿估计，通过共享编码器和任务特定令牌实现相互促进的特征学习，在多个基准数据集上取得了竞争性表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法存在检索与位姿估计分离的问题：检索方法可进行大范围搜索但定位精度低，位姿估计方法精度高但搜索空间窄。简单级联这两个流程会导致误差传播和特征表示不一致，因此亟需一个统一框架同时实现城市级检索和精确位姿估计。

Method: 提出CIPER，包含：(1) 共享Transformer编码器搭配任务特定令牌，解耦全局检索特征与空间定位线索；(2) 双向Transformer位化解码器，利用地面特征作为空间查询进行双向交叉注意力，弥合地面与航拍视角的域差距；(3) 集合预测策略实现稳定的3自由度回归，并在统一多任务目标下联合优化。

Result: 在VIGOR、KITTI和Ford Multi-AV数据集上取得竞争性性能，尤其在有限视场角和任意朝向条件下表现优异。

Conclusion: CIPER证明了通过单一架构联合优化检索与位姿估计是可行且有效的，任务特定令牌和双向交叉注意力机制能有效弥合视角域差距，为跨视角地理定位提供了新的统一范式。

Original Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization estimates the geographic location of a ground image by matching it against an aerial image database. Existing methods tackle this through either large-scale retrieval or precise pose estimation, but not both: retrieval-based methods enable wide-area search at the cost of localization accuracy, while pose estimation methods achieve high precision within only a narrow search space. Naively cascading these pipelines introduces error propagation and inconsistent feature representations. We formulate cross-view geo-localization as a unified problem requiring simultaneous city-scale retrieval and precise 3-DoF pose estimation. We propose CIPER (Cross-view Image-retrieval and Pose-estimation transformER), a single architecture that jointly performs both tasks through mutually beneficial feature learning. CIPER uses a shared transformer encoder with task-specific tokens to disentangle global retrieval features from spatial localization cues. To bridge the large domain gap between ground and aerial views, we introduce a two-way transformer pose decoder that uses ground features as spatial queries for bidirectional cross-attention. A set prediction strategy further enables stable 3-DoF regression under a unified multi-task objective. Experiments on VIGOR, KITTI, and Ford Multi-AV demonstrate competitive performance, especially under limited field-of-view and arbitrary orientation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/yurimjeon1892/CIPER.

中文摘要翻译: 跨视角地理定位通过将地面图像与航拍图像数据库进行匹配来估计其地理位置。现有方法要么通过大规模检索实现，要么通过精确位姿估计实现，但无法兼顾两者：基于检索的方法能够进行广域搜索，但牺牲了定位精度；而位姿估计方法仅在狭窄搜索空间内实现高精度。简单级联这些流程会引入误差传播和特征表示不一致的问题。本文将跨视角地理定位表述为一个统一问题，要求同时进行城市级检索和精确的3自由度位姿估计。我们提出了CIPER（跨视角图像检索与位姿估计Transformer），这是一种单一架构，通过相互促进的特征学习联合执行两项任务。CIPER使用共享Transformer编码器搭配任务特定令牌，将全局检索特征与空间定位线索解耦。为了弥合地面与航拍视角之间的巨大域差距，我们引入了一种双向Transformer位化解码器，该解码器使用地面特征作为空间查询进行双向交叉注意力。集合预测策略进一步实现了在统一多任务目标下的稳定3自由度回归。在VIGOR、KITTI和Ford Multi-AV数据集上的实验展示了竞争性性能，尤其在有限视场角和任意朝向条件下表现优异。代码开源在https://github.com/yurimjeon1892/CIPER。

</details>


### [89] [Handwriting Extraction and Analysis of Signature Lists in Swiss Popular Initiatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05018)
*Marco Peer,Thomas Gorges,Mathias Seuret,Vincent Christlein,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了利用OCR和基于AI的笔迹分析来自动化验证瑞士全民公投签名列表的可行性，构建了结合模板行分割、文本识别与书写者检索的流水线。实验发现OCR处理短姓名等词汇外手写内容时表现不佳（名字CER达29.6%），而书写者检索方法表现更稳健（mAP达50.6%），可用于检测基于笔迹相似性的潜在重复提交。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 瑞士全民公投中签名列表的手写验证是一项劳动密集型的人工过程，需要自动化方法来提高效率。本文旨在探索OCR与AI笔迹分析等自动化文档分析方法支持此任务的潜力。

Method: 提出一个流水线方法，结合了基于模板的行分割、文本识别（OCR）以及书写者检索技术，在包含418位书写者共443条手写条目的数据集上进行评估。

Result: OCR对词汇外手写的错误率较高，名字的字符错误率（CER）达29.6%；而书写者检索表现更稳健，平均精度均值（mAP）达50.6%。现成的OCR系统对手写签名数据（尤其是短词汇外条目如姓名或地址）转录不够可靠，但书写者检索方法能有效识别签名列表中视觉相似的条目。

Conclusion: 现成的OCR系统不足以可靠地转录手写签名数据，但书写者检索方法可有效辅助检测基于笔迹相似性的潜在重复提交，是支持此任务的合适工具。

Original Abstract: Popular initiatives and referendums are central to Swiss democracy, yet the validation of handwritten signature lists remains a labor-intensive manual process. This paper investigates the potential of automated document analysis methods, including OCR and AI-based handwriting analysis, to support this task.
